Abstract: Open synthetic test systems are essential for reproducible power system research, yet the available cases represent almost exclusively North American and European grids. No open transmission test system exists for any Middle Eastern country, whose grids raise different questions such as single corridor fuel-supply resilience and high-renewable operation within small synchronous systems, and established benchmarks cannot demonstrate. This paper presents a synthetic test system for the Jordanian transmission grid, assembled entirely from public sources, where the real topology is reconstructed from a published diagram and restored to its 2018 energized state, with plant-level generation and renewable fleets, per-site hourly profiles, and loads calibrated to the values published by Jordan's grid operators. The case is validated through structural statistics against real-grid criteria, power flow and $N\!-\!1$ screening, an energy-weighted loss decomposition, in addition to a full-year production-cost run compared against the published per-plant energy, and cross-solver verification. The model reproduces the annual energy mix at technology level (largest producers within 4\%, system total within 0.1\%), and the released dataset includes the bus-identity key, all scripts, and a post-2019 scenario variant. This test case is designed to benchmark system-level resource adequacy, time-series dispatch and unit commitment, renewable integration, fuel-supply resilience scenarios, interconnection studies and steady-state studies.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化的电力系统稳定性分析与控制框架，通过引入输入-输出微分无源性（IODP）概念，实现对平衡集而非单一运行点的渐近稳定性认证，并设计控制器补偿IODP不足，从而在高可再生能源波动场景下实现可扩展的系统稳定性验证与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电力系统稳定性分析依赖于集中式方法和单一平衡点，在大规模波动性可再生能源接入背景下日益受限，难以适应高度变化的运行条件。

Method: 提出输入-输出微分无源性（IODP）新概念，将互联系统的平衡集稳定性分解为各设备局部条件；基于I/O变换设计无源化控制器以补偿IODP不足；通过本地化条件构建认证区域，全局稳定性验证简化为检查平衡点是否落在该区域内。

Result: 在改进的IEEE 39节点系统上验证了所提框架的有效性，仿真结果表明其能够在多变运行条件下实现系统的可扩展稳定性认证与控制。

Conclusion: 所提出的去中心化框架通过IODP理论实现了对平衡集的稳定性认证，提升了系统在高比例可再生能源接入下的适应性和可扩展性，为未来电力系统的稳定性分析提供了新的理论工具和控制手段。

Abstract: Conventional power-system stability analysis is largely centralized and centered on a single equilibrium point, which becomes increasingly restrictive in the presence of large-scale fluctuating renewable generation. This paper develops a decentralized framework for stability analysis and control that certifies the asymptotic stability of an equilibrium set rather than that of a given single operating point. To this end, we introduce a new notion termed input--output differential passivity (IODP), which decomposes equilibrium-set stability of the interconnected system into local requirements imposed on individual devices. These requirements are formulated without embedding a particular operating equilibrium into the local conditions; once the certified regions are constructed, stability verification for a given operating scenario reduces to checking whether its equilibrium lies in the certified set. The proposed conditions require each bus to possess a sufficient level of IODP, quantified by an IODP index. To compensate for an IODP shortage, we further develop an I/O-transformation-based passivation controller that reshapes the local input--output behavior of the corresponding device. In this way, all grid-connected components can be made to satisfy the decentralized conditions for system-wide stability. The proposed framework is validated on a modified IEEE 39-bus system. Simulation results demonstrate that it provides a scalable and equilibrium-set-oriented solution for stability certification and control under highly variable operating conditions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在爬坡能力限制和净需求预测不准的电力系统中，现有滚动调度可能导致节点边际电价低于发电机组报价，需依赖不透明且可能引发扭曲激励的市场外补偿机制。为解决此问题，需要一种更优的定价机制。

Method: 通过数学推导得出一种闭式表达的最优统一电价规则，并将其与现有定价方案进行比较，利用数值模拟评估其在发电机利润、价格波动和激励相容性方面的表现。

Result: 所提定价规则可消除市场外补偿，保持LMP拥塞费用，实现收入充足；数值结果显示其降低了价格波动，消除了发电机组虚高报价的动机，但导致需求支付增加，反映了爬坡成本在市场内统一分摊的结果。

Conclusion: 该最优统一定价规则能有效改善实时市场的价格信号透明度与公平性，促进真实报价，尽管增加了需求侧支付，但在维持现有市场结构基础上提供了更优的经济激励机制。

Abstract: In a network with ramp-limited generators and inaccurate net-demand forecasts, practical rolling-window dispatch can drive locational marginal prices (LMPs) below generators' bid-in offers. In such cases, out-of-market (OOM) settlements are used to compensate generators and maintain dispatch-following incentives, but OOM can have negative consequences, including nontransparent real-time price signals, discriminatory compensation, and incentives for untruthful bidding. This paper presents an optimal uniform pricing rule that minimizes demand payments, eliminates OOM make-whole payments, preserves LMP-based congestion charges, and ensures revenue adequacy. We derive the proposed pricing rule in closed form and relate it to existing pricing schemes. Numerical comparisons demonstrate favorable generator profits and reduced price volatility. However, higher generator profits are accompanied by increased demand payments, reflecting the in-market, uniform allocation of ramping costs while preserving the LMP-based congestion charges widely used in real-time market settlements. The numerical results also show that, under LMP with OOM settlement, a price-taking generator has an incentive to inflate its offer, whereas this incentive is absent under the proposed pricing rule within the tested bid range.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents an adaptive stochastic spectral embedding (ASSE) method to solve the probabilistic AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF), a critical aspect of power system operation. The proposed method can efficiently and accurately estimate the probabilistic characteristics (e.g., mean, variance, median, and quantile-based metrics) of AC-OPF solutions while minimizing power losses. Based on estimated AC-OPF decisions (i.e., generator outputs), the confidence interval (CI)-based production cost index can be determined. Specially, an adaptive domain partition strategy is adopted to guide refinement domain selection and partition. The Bayesian compressive sensing-based coefficient calculation algorithm is integrated to enhance its performance. Numerical studies on modified IEEE 9-bus and IEEE 118-bus systems demonstrate that the proposed ASSE method offers accurate and fast evaluations compared to Monte Carlo simulations. Comparisons with a sparse polynomial chaos expansion, Gaussian process regression, and deep neural networks, further illustrate its efficacy in accurately assessing the responses with strongly localized behavior and non-symmetric distributions, providing practical decision-making bounds for generator outputs and operating costs under uncertainty.

Abstract: Bluetooth Low Energy Channel Sounding (BLE CS), standardized in Bluetooth Core Specification 6.0 (September 2024), enables distance estimation via phase-based ranging (PBR) and round-trip time (RTT). Although prior work has studied CS accuracy in configurations with a fixed orientation, no published work has studied how device orientation affects ranging error on commercial hardware. We present the first study of orientation-induced CS ranging error and a Machine Learning correction using IMU features. This study used the EFR32xG24 Channel Sounding Development Kit, the only commercial CS platform with an integrated six-axis Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU). Our results show that device orientation has a substantial effect on CS ranging accuracy; we found that a Random Forest model trained on the IMU derived orientation achieved a 74.6\% Mean Absolute Error (MAE) reduction under a Leave One Orientation Out Evaluation, demonstrating that IMU readings have potential to improve ranging accuracy.

Abstract: Wind turbines require regular maintenance and the resulting costs are a substantial component of a wind farm's cost of electricity production. As a result, there has been ongoing interest in improving wind turbine maintenance scheduling to find an optimal balance between maintenance costs and the risk of failure or unplanned repairs. What remains understudied is the opportunity for wind farms to schedule maintenance in the context of grid conditions and electricity market clearing. This paper contributes to closing this gap by modeling and studying wind farm maintenance planning in a grid and electricity market context. We also review current U.S. practice of wind farm maintenance scheduling, which motivates this paper and its models. Our focus is the derivation of a strategic maintenance planning problem, alongside an efficient solution approach, in which the wind farm operator aims to submit a derated wind farm capacity such that the resulting market clearing and electricity prices maximize its profit while ensuring that all required maintenance can be performed. We derive and study both deterministic and stochastic versions of the model, with the latter considering environmental and operational uncertainties. We conduct numerical experiments using the IEEE RTS 96-bus testbed with real-world offshore wind farm data and investigate the roles of farm and turbine size, as well as forecast quality. We observe an alignment between strategic and grid-serving maintenance planning and find that wind farms can improve their bottom line with low impact on system costs.

Motivation: 在分布式动态协调中，控制器阶次的选择存在权衡：低阶节省状态但可能导致稳态误差，高阶改善跟踪但增加负担。本文旨在消除这一设计选择困境。

Abstract: We address a structural tradeoff in distributed dynamic coordination: when the target complexity is unknown, a low controller order saves states but may leave a persistent tracking error, whereas a high order improves tracking but may burden every agent with unnecessary dynamics. To remove this choice without resorting to computationally more involved nonlinear feedback or chattering-prone nonsmooth feedback, we develop an order-adaptive distributed integral controller (OADIC). Specifically, we start with proportional feedback and add integral states only when locally measurable relative errors show that the current order is inadequate. Meanwhile, we organize the candidate controllers in a nested form, thereby preserving the existing states and gains and maintaining continuous control inputs during order transitions. To provide a theoretical basis for this design, we first characterize the consistency of prescribed relative displacements on the augmented agent--target graph. Next, we construct gains that stabilize all admissible fixed-order subsystems and establish a uniform input-to-state stability bound for the variable-dimension closed loop. Furthermore, we prove that OADIC rejects every insufficient order after finitely many decision intervals and explicitly bound the rejection time of the critical order.

</details>


### [78] [A Modular IoT-Enabled Remote Laboratory Platform for Hybrid Energy System Research and Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00815)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升工程教育和科研中对物理设备的远程访问能力，解决传统实验室在时空限制下的不足，特别是在可再生能源与储能系统教学与实验中的应用需求。

Method: 设计并实现了一个基于工业PLC和IoT网关的三接口远程实验平台，集成可再生能源模拟器、电池储能和可编程负载；通过Talk2M VPN云实现安全通信，采用分层能量管理算法，对比本地与远程执行下的系统性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，在相同风速和辐照条件下，本地与远程运行的能量平衡差异较小，典型通信延迟约为100毫秒，验证了系统的可靠性与实时性。

Conclusion: 该平台能够有效支持控制与能源系统工程领域的研究级远程实验和项目式学习，具有良好的教学与科研应用前景。

Abstract: Remote laboratory systems improve accessibility in engineering education and research by enabling Internet-based interaction with physical equipment. This paper presents a modular IoT-enabled remote laboratory platform for hybrid energy system studies, combining renewable energy emulators, battery storage, and programmable loads within a three-interface architecture based on a web HMI, TIA Portal, and MATLAB/Simulink, all connected through a Talk2M VPN cloud. An industrial PLC and IoT gateway provide deterministic local control as well as secure remote access and monitoring. A hierarchical energy-management algorithm is validated by comparing local and remote executions under identical wind and irradiance profiles. The results show small differences in the energy balances of the renewable sources, battery, and load, while typical communication delays are on the order of 100 ms. Consequently, the platform supports research-grade remote experimentation and project-based learning in control and energy systems engineering.

</details>


### [79] [A Three-phase and Single-phase Compatible Dual-Mode EV On-Board Charger with Integrated Active Power Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00822)
*Bitan Joydhar,Shimul K. Dam*

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于电动汽车的双模式车载充电器，可通过单个继电器开关和小电容实现有源功率解耦，显著减小直流链路电容体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了满足电动汽车对三相高功率充电和单相供电兼容性的需求，同时解决单相充电时直流链路电容过大的问题。

Method: 提出一种新型双模式OBC拓扑结构，利用一个额外的继电器开关和小容量电容实现有源功率解耦（APD），抑制直流链路中的二倍频电流。

Result: 仿真验证了该拓扑在不同工况下的有效性，单相模式下直流链路电容减少了一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的双模式OBC兼具三相与单相充电能力，且通过APD技术大幅降低电容需求，有助于提升功率密度和系统集成度。

Abstract: Onboard charger (OBC) is essential part of Electric Vehicle (EV). High-performance EVs are preferring three phase charging to achieve higher power level. However, the ability to charge from a single phase supply is also required. A dualmode OBC for EVs is proposed, which is capable of operating from both three-phase supply and single-phase supply. The single phase charging comes with the requirement of bulky DC link capacitance due to double frequency current in DC link. The proposed topology eliminates this by achieving Active Power Decoupling (APD) using only one additional relay switch and a small capacitor. The proposed topology is verified under different conditions in a detailed simulation, which shows more than an order of magnitude reduction in DC link capacitance during single phase operation.

</details>


### [80] [Low-Complexity Control Under Input Saturation and Performance Constraints: A Bidirectional Modification Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00827)
*Jinyu Ni,Xiucai Huang,George A. Rovithakis,Yamin Yan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需模型、逼近和避免复杂性爆炸的控制方案，用于解决输入饱和和性能约束下的高阶不确定强耦合MIMO非线性系统的输出跟踪控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高阶不确定强耦合MIMO非线性系统在输入饱和和性能约束下的输出跟踪控制难题，现有方法存在模型依赖、逼近复杂性和分析困难等问题，因此需要一种更高效且稳定的控制策略。

Method: 构建了一个双向修正机制，能够在输入饱和时放松约束，在饱和结束后加速恢复原始约束，并在稳态无饱和时收紧约束以提升性能；同时提出了一种新的稳定性分析框架，确保闭环信号有界并满足修正后的约束条件。

Result: 所提方法实现了对高阶不确定强耦合系统的有效输出跟踪控制，仿真结果验证了该方法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 该文提出的控制方案结合双向修正机制和新型稳定性分析框架，能够有效处理输入饱和与性能约束，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: This article addresses the output tracking control problem for a class of high-order uncertain highly-coupled MIMO nonlinear systems subject to input saturation and performance constraints. To resolve the problem, a bidirectional modification mechanism is constructed, which is able to not only relax the constraints when saturation occurs to alleviate potential conflict, but also accelerate the recovery of original constraints after saturation ceases, and further tighten the constraints to enhance control performance if saturation remains inactive at the steadystate phase. Based on the mechanism, a model-, approximationand complexity-explosion-free control scheme is proposed. To bypass the obstacle in Lyapunov analysis, a novel stability analysis framework is developed, which, given that two parameter selection conditions are met, ensures satisfaction of modified constraints and boundedness of all closed-loop signals. Simulation results validate the effectiveness and superiority of the methodology.

</details>


### [81] [Lightweight CFR-Based Modulation Adaptation in a Real-Time MIMO-OFDM SDR Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00903)
*Luca Borst,Maryam Ansarifard,Ankith Vinayachandran,Kishor C. Joshi,George Exarchakos*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在实时MIMO-OFDM系统中使用信道频率响应（CFR）幅度特征进行调制选择的链路自适应方法，相比传统SNR指标显著提升了分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统链路自适应依赖于SNR等标量指标，无法充分利用信道信息；而现有高精度方法复杂度高，难以实时实现，因此需要一种兼具高性能与低复杂度的替代方案。

Method: 基于USRP测试平台收集87,817个空中传输（OTA）样本，提取基站端上行导频的CFR特征；采用决策树（DT）、随机森林（RF）和K近邻（KNN）分类器，分别评估SNR、CFR及其组合特征在调制识别中的性能，并将轻量级DT模型集成至LabVIEW C Node实现实时推断。

Result: 仅使用SNR的分类器准确率为35%-42%，而基于CFR特征的DT、RF和KNN分别达到73.6%、81.4%和80.0%；RF在接近10% BLER可靠性阈值时仍保持82.8%的准确率；深度为7的DT（含123个叶节点）可成功部署于实时系统。

Conclusion: 基站侧的紧凑型CFR描述符相比传统SNR能提供更强的判别能力，且适用于轻量级软件定义无线电（SDR）实现，是实现高效实时链路自适应的可行方案。

Abstract: Conventional link adaptation typically relies on scalar link-quality indicators such as signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), while richer channel state information (CSI) can improve adaptation at the cost of higher processing complexity. This paper investigates a compact alternative for modulation selection in a real-time multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) system using channel frequency response (CFR) magnitude descriptors. A dataset of 87,817 over-the-air (OTA) samples is collected using a USRP-based testbed, with CFR measurements extracted at the base station (BS) from received uplink pilots. Decision tree (DT), random forest (RF), and k-nearest neighbours (KNN) classifiers are evaluated using BS-side SNR, CFR features, and their combination. SNR-only classifiers achieve 35%-42% test accuracy, whereas CFR-only features achieve 73.6%, 81.4%, and 80.0% for DT, RF, and KNN, respectively. CFR-based performance is maintained near the 10% BLER reliability thresholds, with RF reaching 82.8%. A depth-7 DT with 123 leaves is further integrated into the LabVIEW C Node for real-time inference. The results show that compact BS-side CFR descriptors provide more discriminative information than the available scalar BS-side SNR while remaining suitable for lightweight SDR implementation.

</details>


### [82] [Geometric Fixed-Time Sliding Mode Control for Constrained Attitude Tracking on $\mathrm{SO}(3)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01211)
*Saumitra Barman,Shashi Ranjan Kumar,Rohit Gupta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在多姿态指向约束和外部干扰的情况下，基于SO(3)流形的航天器姿态跟踪控制问题，提出了一种内蕴于SO(3)的势函数与固定时间滑模控制律，实现了在规避禁飞区域的同时快速、全局收敛的姿态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统姿态控制方法难以处理姿态约束和拓扑障碍，且无法保证全局快速收敛，因此需要在SO(3)流形上构建能规避禁飞区域并实现固定时间收敛的几何控制方法。

Method: 在SO(3)上构造具有唯一非退化极小值的内蕴姿态势函数，并通过黎曼梯度定义固定时间非奇异滑模面，结合滑模控制设计几何控制律，利用李群微分几何分析其凸性与收敛性。

Result: 所提势函数在允许姿态子集内具有局部强凸性，控制律确保系统状态始终保持在允许区域内，并在固定时间内收敛至平衡点的小邻域内。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了带姿态约束和扰动的航天器姿态跟踪问题，具备良好的几何特性、避障能力与固定时间收敛性能。

Abstract: This paper studies constrained spacecraft attitude tracking on the Riemannian configuration manifold $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ in the presence of multiple attitude pointing constraints and matched external disturbances. To address this, an attitude potential function is proposed intrinsically on $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, and its key properties are established using intrinsic geometric analysis. Under mild conditions, the potential function is shown to admit a unique nondegenerate minimum at the desired attitude over the admissible subset of $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, defined by excluding the forbidden attitude regions as well as a measure-zero set, thereby ensuring a well-posed constrained attitude tracking problem. A Riemannian Hessian analysis shows that the Hessian of the potential function is locally uniform positive definite in an open neighborhood of the desired attitude, thereby establishing local strong convexity. A nonsingular fixed-time geometric sliding manifold is proposed using the Riemannian gradient of the potential function, leading to a geometric fixed-time sliding-mode-based constrained attitude control law. It is shown that, for every initial attitude in the admissible subset, the closed-loop state trajectory evolves on $\mathrm{SO}(3)\times\mathbb{R}^3$, with the attitude remaining in the admissible subset throughout the maneuver, while the state converges to a sufficiently small compact neighborhood of the desired equilibrium in a prescribed fixed time. Numerical simulations validate the proposed control approach and illustrate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [83] [Constructive Port-Hamiltonian Energy Shaping Design of Dispatchable Virtual Oscillators in Grid-Forming Converters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01349)
*Lu Gao,Lihui Yang,Feng Ji,Dong Liu,Longze Kou*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于端口-哈密顿（PH）系统的统一框架，用于电网形成控制，通过构建包含幅值恢复和功率分配的统一形变哈密顿函数，实现了几乎全局渐近稳定性和局部指数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可调度虚拟振荡器控制（dVOC）缺乏统一的能量解释，导致幅值调节、同步和功率调度耦合严重，难以实现稳定的能量管理。

Method: 将外环动态建模为耗散PH系统，利用具有饱和非线性的逻辑型径向调节实现与二次能量存储兼容的梯度分解，构造统一的形变哈密顿函数，并通过径向增益匹配推导出保证稳定性的参数不等式。

Result: 提出了显式的闭式参数不等式，确保几乎全局渐近稳定和局部指数收敛；通过调节功率误差加权系数消除低电压潮流解，保证唯一高电压平衡点的存在性，并通过Hessian奇异性条件给出临界权重阈值。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功将幅值调节、同步和功率调度统一于一个能量框架下，提升了系统稳定性与收敛性能，仿真验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Port-Hamiltonian (PH) theory offers a passivity-based framework for grid-forming control, yet conventional dispatchable virtual oscillator control (dVOC) does not naturally admit a dissipative PH realization, since its amplitude egulation, synchronization, and power dispatch are inherently coupled without a unified energy interpretation. This paper formulates the outer-loop dynamics as a dissipative PH system, thereby unifying amplitude regulation, synchronization, and power dispatch within one energy structure. The formulation rests on the key property that logistic-type radial regulation, characterized by an inherent saturation-like nonlinearity, permits an exact gradient decomposition compatible with the quadratic energy storage. On this basis, a unified shaped Hamiltonian is constructed, which encapsulates both amplitude restoration and power dispatch. Radial gain matching derived from this Hamiltonian yields explicit closed-form arameter inequalities that guarantee almost-global asymptotic stability and local exponential convergence. Moreover, tuning the power-error weighting coefficient actively shapes the energy landscape, thereby eliminating the undesirable low-voltage power-flow solution from the stationary set and ensuring convergence to the desired high-voltage equilibrium point. The Hessian singularity condition further provides the critical weight threshold that guarantees uniqueness of the high-voltage equilibrium. Numerical simulations validate the proposed method.

</details>


### [84] [Exact Risk-Complexity Laws for Projective Boundaries in Scenario Optimization and Distribution-Free Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01355)
*Giuseppe C. Calafiore*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为“适当投影边界方案”的框架，用于分析场景优化和共形预测中的条件违反风险分布，揭示了观测边界大小随机时的精确分布规律，并指出仅依赖观测复杂度的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有方法中，条件违反风险通常假定服从精确的贝塔分布，但当边界大小随机时，这一假设不再成立，需要更普适的理论解释。

Method: 引入“适当投影边界方案”概念，结合边界映射与接受集，通过跨样本复杂度剖面刻画条件风险分布，并推导出稳定与变化剖面对应的分布形式。

Result: 证明了对于任意适当投影边界方案，条件违反风险的分布由其跨样本复杂度剖面决定；稳定剖面恢复经典贝塔律，变化剖面则产生精确的修正项，并涵盖多种已有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了多种分布无关认证方法，提供了对条件风险更精细的刻画，同时表明仅使用观测复杂度不足以获得可靠保证，需考虑复杂度的动态变化。

Abstract: Scenario optimization, conformal prediction, and related distribution-free certification methods use finite samples to construct decisions or prediction sets with violation-risk guarantees for fresh observations. In several classical settings, the conditional violation risk follows an exact beta law, whose tail has a beta-binomial representation and whose parameter is a support, calibration, or compression dimension. This paper identifies the deterministic boundary mechanism behind these formulas and derives the corresponding law when the observed boundary size is random. A decision rule is represented by an acceptance set for future observations, together with a boundary map selecting the sample points responsible for that set. The resulting pair is called a {\em proper projective boundary scheme} when held-out samples are accepted precisely if the full-sample boundary is retained, and accepted non-boundary samples can be deleted without changing that boundary. For every such scheme, the conditional law of the violation risk given the observed boundary size is determined by the boundary's cross-sample complexity profile. A stable profile yields the usual beta law, whereas a varying profile produces an exact profile correction. The framework covers scalar order-statistic calibration, support-reconstructive scenario programs, cascaded support-removal certificates, coordinatewise envelopes, and Pareto-frontier calibration with vector scores. It also yields conditional probabilistic certificates and a no-go result explaining why observed complexity alone is insufficient.

</details>


### [85] [Equivalent DQ Sequence-Domain Model of Unbalanced Three-Phase Passive Elements for Power Converter Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01396)
*Air\án Franc\és,Luis Saz,Rafael Castillo,Dionisio Ramirez,Javier Uceda*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了不平衡三相无源元件的等效dq序域模型的推导方法，结合解析和直观图形方法，并通过实验验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统dq0模型假设各相阻抗相同，在实际阻抗不对称时会产生二次谐波，限制了其应用，因此需要更精确的模型来处理不平衡情况。

Method: 利用Fortescue定理将不对称三相信号分解为对称分量，并推导出考虑零序与正负序相互作用的等效dq序域模型，同时采用解析法和图形化方法进行分析。

Result: 成功建立了能反映不平衡三相无源元件特性的dq序域模型，且实验结果验证了该模型的准确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型能够有效处理三相系统中的不平衡问题，克服了传统dq0模型的局限性，为逆变器和整流器控制器的设计提供了更精确的理论基础。

Abstract: Equivalent dq0 models of three-phase passive impedances are widely used for the design of three-phase inverter and rectifier controllers in the synchronous frame. However, these equivalent models assume that the impedance in each phase are the same, which is not always applicable. The fact that the impedance in each phase is different, generates second order harmonic content in the dq0 components, which hinders the advantages of the dq0 transformation. To avoid the second harmonic in these scenarios, it is possible to use the Fortescue's theorem to represent any asymmetrical set of three-phase signals as a linear combination of three symmetrical sequences. In the literature, only a few works have attempted to derive the equivalent model of unbalanced three-phase passive elements in the sequence domain, and none is able to account for the interaction between the zero sequence and the positive and negative sequences. This work presents the derivation of the equivalent dq sequence-domain model of unbalanced three-phase passive elements both analytically and with an intuitive graphical approach. Experimental results are shown to validate the proposed models.

</details>


### [86] [Blackbox Small-Signal Modeling of Grid-Connected Inverters in Asymmetrical Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01411)
*Airan Frances,Dionisio Ramirez,Javier Uceda*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在不对称条件下表示并网商用变流器动态行为的序域黑箱建模新结构，并通过实验验证了其在三相逆变器电压暂降期间的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于电力电子系统在不对称条件下的动态交互可能导致电能质量问题，且元件的精确电气模型通常难以获取，因此需要有效的黑箱建模方法来表征商用变流器的行为。

Method: 提出一种基于序列域的新建模结构，用于描述不对称条件下并网三相变流器的动态特性，并通过实验数据对模型进行辨识与验证。

Result: 在三相逆变器上进行了实验测试，成功识别出其黑箱模型，并在不对称电压 sag 条件下验证了模型的良好性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的序域黑箱建模方法能够有效表征商用三相并网变流器在不对称工况下的动态行为，适用于系统级分析和电能质量评估。

Abstract: Power electronic converters are envisaged to be key enablers of modern electric power distribution systems. Grid-connected three-phase inverters are widely used in Smart Grids and microgrids, but also in standard grids. They provide controllability and dynamic decoupling capabilities, which are fundamental in the integration of renewable sources and storage systems and in specialized applications such as FACTS. Nevertheless, power electronics-based systems can exhibit dynamic interactions, which may lead to power quality issues. Although the electrical model of each element of these systems is important for their system-level analysis, they are rarely available. Blackbox modeling strategies are useful for obtaining behavioral models of commercial electronic converters. Most blackbox modeling strategies are focused on dc-dc electronic converters; however, some works have extended these concepts to three-phase converters by means of the dq framework, under the assumption of symmetrical and balanced conditions. This work proposes a new structure in the sequence domain to represent the dynamic behavior of grid-connected commercial converters in asymmetrical conditions. Experimental tests have been performed on a three-phase inverter to identify its blackbox model and its performance has been validated during an asymmetric voltage sag.

</details>


### [87] [Stability of Droop-Controlled Low-Frequency Transmission Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01571)
*Rafael Castillo-Sierra,Giri Venkataramanan,Dionisio Ramirez*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了采用下垂控制策略的低频交流输电系统的小信号稳定性，通过特征值分析探讨了控制器参数、运行频率、线路参数和运行点对系统稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高低频交流输电系统的稳定性和可靠性，研究不同参数对其小信号稳定性的影响至关重要。

Method: 采用特征值分析方法，分析下垂增益、运行频率、输电线路参数和运行点对系统稳定性的影响。

Result: 结果表明，系统的动态特性主要由AC/AC变换器的下垂增益之和决定，同时线路长度、R/X比、运行频率和电压也影响系统稳定性。

Conclusion: 下垂增益之和是影响系统稳定性的关键因素，其他参数如线路长度、R/X比、运行频率和电压也需要综合考虑以优化系统设计。

Abstract: Low-Frequency AC (LFAC) transmission systems employing power converters are being considered for a varity of applications. This work studies the small-signal stability of an LFAC transmission line controlled by the Droop Control Strategy. Eigenvalue Analysis is used to determine how the controller droop gains, operating frequency, transmission line parameters, and the operating point affect system stability. The results show that the overall system's dynamic is governed by the sum of the droop gains of the AC/AC converters. Analytical results that give insights on how the different system's parameters affect the critical stability point are presented. The results indicate that system stability is affected by the line's length, the line's R/X ratio, operating frequency and voltage.

</details>

### [88] [Fuzzy local reduced order models (fl-ROMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00031)
*Antonio Colanera*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模糊c均值聚类的模糊局部降阶模型（fl-ROM），通过连续的隶属权重实现局部动力学的平滑耦合，有效提升了非线性动力系统在复杂流形和状态转换下的建模精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统通常演化在几何复杂的低维流形上，单一全局降阶模型难以准确逼近；此前提出的量化局部降阶模型（ql-ROM）在簇边界处因硬切换导致不连续性和振荡，影响精度与稳定性。

Method: 采用模糊c均值聚类构建模糊局部降阶模型（fl-ROM），并行推进各局部降阶状态，通过连续的隶属权重融合预测结果，实现局部动力学的平滑‘单位分解’耦合；同时引入伪贝叶斯信息准则指导模糊设定下聚类数的选择。

Result: 在二维Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程的周期、行波、准周期和混沌等不同动力学状态下进行验证，结果表明：局部ROM优于全局ROM，而fl-ROM相比sharp formulation具有更平滑且通常更精确的预测；在混沌情形下，fl-ROM在短期预测精度与长期统计特性之间达到最佳平衡。

Conclusion: fl-ROM为具有状态转移和复杂吸引子几何结构的非线性动力系统提供了一个有效、鲁棒且可解释的降阶建模框架。

Abstract: Nonlinear dynamical systems often evolve on low-dimensional manifolds with intricate geometry, making them difficult to approximate with a single global reduced-order model (ROM). To address this, we previously introduced quantized local reduced-order models (ql-ROMs), which partition the solution manifold into $K$ clusters and construct local ROMs around the corresponding centroids. In the sharp formulation, discontinuous switching between clusters can induce discontinuities, spurious oscillations, and loss of accuracy near cluster boundaries. Here, we propose a fuzzy extension of ql-ROMs based on fuzzy c-means clustering. The resulting fuzzy local ROM (fl-ROM) advances all local reduced states in parallel and combines their predictions through continuous membership weights, yielding a smooth partition-of-unity coupling of the local dynamics. We also introduce a pseudo-Bayesian information criterion to guide the selection of the number of clusters in the fuzzy setting. The methodology is assessed on the two-dimensional Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation in periodic, travelling-wave-like, quasi-periodic, and chaotic regimes. In all cases, the local ROMs outperform the global ROM, while the fl-ROM provides smoother and generally more accurate predictions than the sharp formulation. In the chaotic regime, where long-time trajectory tracking is inherently limited, the fl-ROM achieves the best compromise between short-time predictive accuracy and long-term statistics. These results show that fl-ROM provides an effective, robust, and interpretable framework for reduced-order modeling of nonlinear dynamical systems with regime transitions and complex attractor geometry.

</details>


### [89] [Predicting Phase Ordering in Chaotic Maps and Coupled Map Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00983)
*Shiva Dixit,Swati Chauhan,Manish Dev Shrimali*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于参数感知储备池计算（PARC）的数据驱动机器学习框架，用于预测耦合逻辑映射系统中序参量的动力学行为，成功预测了从同相到反相的相变过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用机器学习方法在系统参数变化时提前预测耦合混沌系统中的集体有序相变，尤其是在传统方法难以处理的危机点附近。

Method: 采用参数感知储备池计算（PARC）框架，利用危机前的时间序列数据训练储备池模型；对于一维逻辑映射，重建整个分岔图；对于二维耦合映射格子（CML），利用空间均匀性，单个储备池训练后复制到所有格点进行预测。

Result: 成功重建了逻辑映射的分岔图并预测了方向性序参量M(μ)在危机点后的无序转变；在CML中准确预测了在μ≈3.82处从同相同步到反相簇态的转变，相相干度θ从≈1降至≈0。

Conclusion: PARC框架能够有效预测混沌耦合系统中跨越危机点的集体相变，展示了数据驱动方法在复杂动力系统序参量预测中的潜力。

Abstract: Coupled logistic maps exhibit collective ordering of their directional phases. As the system parameter varies, the directional phases can undergo a transition from an in-phase state to an anti-phase state, while the individual map trajectories remain chaotic. In this work, we propose a data-driven machine learning (ML) framework based on parameter-aware reservoir computing (PARC) to predict order-parameter dynamics in two representative systems: a logistic map and a two-dimensional coupled map lattice (CML). For the logistic map, the reservoir is trained using only pre-crisis time series data at bifurcation parameter $μ$ values below the attractor-merging crisis ($μ_0 = 3.6786$). The trained reservoir reconstructs the full bifurcation diagram and correctly predicts the transition in the directional order parameter $M(μ)$, from an ordered state ($M \approx 0$) to a disordered state ($M \neq 0$) across the crisis point. For the CML, we exploit the spatial homogeneity of the lattice: a single reservoir is trained on the dynamics of one representative lattice site and is then replicated across all $L^2$ sites during prediction, where $L$=50. The replicated reservoir correctly predicts the transition from in-phase synchronization ($θ\approx 1$) to anti-phase clustered states ($θ\approx 0$) at $μ\approx 3.82$, where $θ$ quantifies phase coherence across lattice sites.

</details>


### [90] [Spatiotemporal Chaos with Extended Spatial Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01424)
*A. Raj,M. R. Paul*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维非线性映射格子中协变李雅普诺夫向量（CLVs）的谱特性，提出了一种不依赖具体映射函数、仅基于耦合结构预测CLV谱和李雅普诺夫指数谱的理论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大尺度空间耦合系统中混沌动力学的结构特征，特别是CLVs的空间谱如何受耦合方式影响。

Method: 利用协变李雅普诺夫向量（CLVs）分析系统，并基于耦合矩阵的本征向量建立理论模型，推导CLVs的空间功率谱结构。

Result: 发现CLVs可分为两个区域：对应最大李雅普诺夫指数的第一区域具有高度纠缠且含大尺度结构的CLVs，其波数随索引线性增加；第二区域则表现出多尺度混合。两区域分界索引约等于系统的动力学分形维数。

Conclusion: CLVs的谱结构主要由耦合矩阵决定，可通过对耦合矩阵本征向量的分析普遍预测，揭示了高维混沌系统中尺度分离与维度之间的内在联系。

Abstract: The chaotic dynamics of a large one-dimensional lattice of nonlinear maps with a spatial coupling of increasing extent is explored using the covariant Lyapunov vectors (CLVs). A theoretical approach is developed that predicts the spectral variation of the CLVs and the spectrum of Lyapunov exponents. The theoretical approach is independent of the details of the mapping function and requires only a description of the lattice coupling. The general structure of the spatial power spectrum of the CLVs is determined using only the eigenvectors of the coupling matrix. This yields a decomposition of the CLVs into two regimes. The first regime, corresponding to the largest Lyapunov exponents, contains highly entangled CLVs with significant contributions from large scale spatial structures and aligns with the sorted eigenvectors, which occur in pairs with wavenumbers that increase linearly with their index. The second regime contains significant mixing of length scales with increasing index. The index separating these two regimes is approximately equal to the fractal dimension of the dynamics.

</details>


### [91] [Fifth-Harmonic Feedback Controls the Existence of a Third-Harmonic Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01508)
*Kanchan Sarkar*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了在弱振幅下谐波零点的不可达性，指出五次谐波虽然微弱，但在特定条件下可通过鞍结分岔导致平衡态消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成谐波中精确零点的形成机制及其在弱振幅下的可达性限制。

Method: 通过分析阻尼振子的基频源平衡与吸收损耗引起的相位亏损，结合轨道运动和同伦加权方法研究谐波平衡的闭合条件。

Result: 发现五次谐波幅度仅为基频的1.8×10⁻³，但其加权低于0.48时可引发鞍结分岔，使追踪的解对消失。

Conclusion: 弱振幅下由于主导通道阶数不足（O(F⁷)而非O(F³)），闭合条件无法满足，导致精确零点不可达。

Abstract: An exact zero of a generated harmonic needs two real cancellations; a damped oscillator's leading fundamental-source balance supplies one. Absorber loss leaves a phase deficit of fixed sign, amplitude independent at leading order, closed instead by the orbit's own $5Ω$ motion returning to $3Ω$ at weight $|Δ_1|^4$. Closure is therefore unreachable at weak amplitude: the channel supplying it ranks at $O(F^7)$, not at the leading $O(F^3)$. The fifth harmonic is $1.8\times10^{-3}$ of the relative-coordinate fundamental, yet a balance homotopy weighting it below $0.48$ annihilates the tracked pair in a saddle-node.

</details>

### [92] [How well is surface ocean carbon represented in observations and ocean models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00133)
*Viviana Acquaviva,Romina Wild,Alessandro Laio,Amanda R. Fay,Thea H. Heimdal,Galen A. McKinley*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于数据流形的内在维度和可微信息不平衡的通用框架，用于量化复杂地球物理数据集的信息内容和表示质量，并应用于表面海洋碳数据的表示优化与模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解和评估复杂地球物理数据（如海洋碳观测和模型）的信息含量与表示质量，需要一种能够量化数据复杂性并比较不同数据源之间信息结构的方法。

Method: 利用数据流形的内在维度和可微信息不平衡理论，构建一个量化信息内容的框架，并应用于SOCCAT观测数据和全球海洋生物地球化学模型（GOBMs），比较其变量重要性、区域表现及时间稳定性。

Result: 发现当前GOBMs未能完全捕捉SOCCAT观测数据的空间复杂性，但在变量排序和相对重要性上基本正确；在某些区域（如南大洋）表示精度较低，且过去二十年未显著改进；提出的最优表示可提升基于距离的机器学习模型性能，并提出了两种新指标用于构建更准确的加权估计集成。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估和改进地球系统模型提供了新的信息理论工具，有助于优化数据表示、提升预测能力，并支持多模型集成的定量比较。

Abstract: We introduce a general framework for quantifying the information content and representation quality of complex geophysical datasets based on the intrinsic dimension and differentiable information imbalance of data manifolds. We use it to derive and compare optimal representations of surface ocean carbon in the SOCAT database of observations and in global ocean biogeochemistry models (GOBMs) and to assess the robustness of the information we can extract from existing data. We find that within the most widely used feature set, the complexity of the data space of SOCAT observations is not fully captured by GOBMs, but the ranking and relative importance of variables learned through GOBMs are substantially correct. We observe that the learned representation of ocean carbon is less accurate in some regions, including the Southern Ocean, but doesn't appear to have evolved significantly over the last two decades. Finally, we show how the optimal representations can be used to improve the skill of distance-based machine learning models and demonstrate it for ocean carbon, and we propose two new metrics to compare models and observations that can be used to build more accurate weighted ensembles of estimates.

</details>


### [93] [Reconciling the Lack of a Robust Anvil Cloud Amount Response to Warming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00429)
*Zachary McGraw,Blaž Gasparini,Wouter Mol,Aiko Voigt*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 该研究挑战了“稳定性-云砧假说”，指出静力稳定性的增加并非由动力过程导致云砧减少，而是源于高空空气密度降低在气压坐标系中的数学表现，RCEMIP模拟证实其与云砧量无显著关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验热带云砧云量随变暖减少的“稳定性-云砧假说”是否具有理论和模拟依据。

Method: 通过解析推导揭示静力稳定性与上升速度对地表变暖的敏感性源于高空空气密度下降，并利用RCEMIP模拟集合验证该机制及云砧响应。

Result: 发现静力稳定性的增加是空气密度减少在气压坐标中的体现，不伴随云砧量、对流输送到云砧层的水汽或成云效率的显著变化。

Conclusion: 静力稳定性增加并不系统性地抑制云砧形成，基本物理论证不能支持云砧量随变暖必然减少的观点。

Abstract: The stability-iris hypothesis proposes that tropical anvil cloud amount decreases with warming as anvils rise into environments of greater static stability. Here we show that there is no clear theoretical or simulated link between increasing static stability and anvil amount. We demonstrate analytically that identified sensitivities of static stability and pressure velocity to surface warming arise from decreasing air density aloft, which enters pressure-coordinate formulations but does not represent a process that dynamically constrains anvil formation. An ensemble of RCEMIP simulations verifies that the stability increase is robust yet arises from the air density reduction, and hence is not accompanied by a robust response in anvil amount, convectively driven water convergence into anvil levels, or the efficiency with which this convergence produces cloudiness. Overall, these findings dispel the expectation of a systematic reduction in anvil amount with warming arising from basic physical arguments.

</details>


### [94] [A Checklist to assess the energy and carbon impacts of ML/AI applications in Earth System Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00847)
*Filippo Dainelli,Amirpasha Mozaffari,Marina Castaño,Aina Gaya i Àvila,Lluís Palma Garcia,Alessio Melli,Oscar Dimdore Miles,Amanda Duarte*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一份针对机器学习与地球系统科学应用的实用清单，旨在帮助研究人员评估和减少其项目对环境的影响，并提供了估算能耗和碳足迹的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习和人工智能在气候、天气和地球系统建模中的广泛应用，如何在日常研究中实现伦理和可持续发展的原则成为一个挑战。

Method: 通过整合现有文献中的讨论，构建一个围绕模型开发流程各阶段的实用检查清单，并提供相关的能耗与碳排放评估指标。

Result: 提供了一个结构化的工具，帮助研究人员在实际工作中落实可持续性原则，并引用具体文献中的示例和建议以支持每一项决策。

Conclusion: 该检查清单有助于弥合可持续AI的愿景与科研实践之间的差距，推动更环保的机器学习研究。

Abstract: As machine learning and artificial intelligence find their way into nearly every aspect of climate, weather, and Earth system modeling, it is worth pausing to consider what our design decisions imply for the science and for the computational resources we consume. A growing body of literature addresses the ethical and sustainable development of ML/AI, yet translating these principles into day-to-day research practice remains a challenge as most of best practices are dispersed across multiple studies and commentaries. Here, we distill these discussions into a practical checklist that ML/AI and Earth system science practitioners can use to assess and reduce the environmental footprint of their own applications, organised around the successive stages of the model development pipeline. We complement the checklist with a selection of metrics drawn from the literature for estimating the energy consumption and carbon footprint of a project. For each question, we point to concrete examples and actionable suggestions from recent literature, aiming to bridge the gap between aspirational principles and the decisions researchers face at every stage of the development cycle.

</details>


### [95] [A Sensor-Adaptive Incremental Learning Framework for Artifact Detection in Satellite Precipitation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01514)
*Andres F. Monsalve,Hernan A. Moreno,Christian D. Kummerow*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练计算机视觉模型的异常检测框架，用于识别全球卫星降雨数据中的伪影，结合少量人工标注数据，在SSMI和SSMIS数据上验证了方法的有效性，具备良好的可解释性和迭代优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着小型卫星的快速发展，私营部门提供的卫星降水产品迅速增加，但缺乏相应的数据验证手段，亟需一种能在数据发布前实时检测异常的可靠工具。

Method: 利用预训练的计算机视觉模型，结合稀疏的人工标注数据，构建了一个异常检测框架，并应用于SSMI和SSMIS卫星的微波成像数据，以识别包含伪影的轨道数据。

Result: 该方法能够有效区分正常与含有伪影的卫星轨道数据，性能与现有最先进的在轨方法相当，并具备可解释性以及根据误报或漏报进行迭代优化的能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的异常检测框架为卫星降水产品的质量控制提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，有助于提升近实时遥感数据的可信度和应用价值。

Abstract: Historically, retrieving rainfall data from satellite imagery has been the domain of space agencies. However, in recent years, the development of cheaper, more compact satellites (SmallSats) capable of detecting rainfall proxies has led to a significant increase in private-sector initiatives for satellite launch and surface precipitation products. This rapid growth has yet to be matched by data validation efforts. Consequently, the need for a robust tool to detect anomalies in near-real-time data before it is disseminated to the public has become critical. In this paper, we present the development of an anomaly-detection system to identify artifacts in global satellite-based rainfall products. The developed framework leverages pre-trained computer vision models and incorporates scarce human-labeled data to detect specific anomalies. Our proposed anomaly detection strategy is tested on data from the Special Sensor Microwave Imager (SSMI) and the Special Sensor Microwave Imager/Sounder (SSMIS). Results demonstrate the efficacy of our approach at separating regular orbits from artifact-containing orbits for each satellite, with performance comparable to state-of-the-art in-place methods. Additionally, the framework offers explainability and the capacity for iterative refinement following false-positive or false-negative classifications.

</details>

### [96] [Optical free space extreme learning machine for the implementation of emergent complex systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00933)
*Elena Moreno,Fernando Soldevila,Daniel Torrent*

Main category: nlin.CG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于光学极端学习机的自由空间系统，用于模拟多种细胞自动机，具有可编程、低成本、低功耗和易搭建的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望设计一种物理上遵循细胞自动机规则演化的新型模拟计算平台，以降低功耗和延迟，而非仅在软件层面实现。

Method: 利用空间光调制器编码系统演化规则，并通过相干波传播执行计算，构建自由空间中的光学极端学习机。

Result: 成功实现了对基本细胞自动机、康威生命游戏和二维图灵机等多种复杂计算系统的模拟。

Conclusion: 该系统是一种简单、完全可编程、高效且易于实现的细胞自动机模拟平台，展示了光学计算在复杂系统仿真中的潜力。

Abstract: Cellular automata conform a set of computational models which evolve with a reduced set of simple rules, yet still are able to show extremely complex emergent phenomena such as fractals and universal computation. Despite their apparent simplicity, they have shown great potential in simulating natural systems and solving challenging computational tasks such as classification and image generation. Instead of implementing cellular automata purely at the software level, it is desirable to design novel analog computing platforms that physically evolve following the automata's underlying rules, thereby reducing power requirements and latency. Here, we introduce an optical extreme learning machine for the simulation of a wide range of cellular automata. Our system operates in free space, and uses a spatial light modulator to encode the evolution rules of the system, while coherent wave propagation performs the corresponding computations. Our results demonstrate a simple, fully-programmable, cost and power efficient, and easy to build and align platform for the implementation of a wide range of complex computational systems such as elementary cellular automata, Conway's Game of Life, and two-dimensional Turing machines.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Bayesian Tracking of a Diffusing Target in Two and Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00144)
*Ewan McCulloch,Adam Nahum*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了在噪声分布式传感器阵列下扩散目标的贝叶斯跟踪问题，通过映射到KPZ增长与移动缺陷模型，分析了不同维度下的相变行为，发现在d=2时存在去钉扎相变，d=3时可能出现无检测或错误检测两种失败模式，三相交汇于一个类似Nishimori的多临界点，并指出传统ε展开无法描述某些异常临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在高维空间中对扩散目标进行贝叶斯跟踪的性能极限及相变结构，特别是在模型误设条件下的跟踪失败机制。

Method: 结合理论分析、数值模拟和弱耦合重正化群（RG）方法，将贝叶斯跟踪问题映射到KPZ界面增长与移动缺陷或定向聚合物钉扎问题，研究其相图与临界行为。

Result: 在d=2时发现去钉扎相变，贝叶斯最优推断始终成功，但过度自信的次优推断会导致失败；在d=3时存在三种相：成功跟踪、无检测（后验概率离域）和错误检测（后验概率局域但在错误位置），分别对应Edwards-Wilkinson和KPZ统计，三相交于Nishimori型多临界点；模型误设可导致进入不同失败相；部分相变具有非标准临界行为，ε展开失效。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯跟踪的成功与否不仅取决于维度和噪声水平，还强烈依赖于推断策略是否最优，特别是模型置信度的设定至关重要；该框架揭示了与表面增长和定向聚合物相关的广泛相变现象中的新临界特性。

Abstract: We study Bayesian tracking of a diffusing target monitored by a noisy distributed sensor array. Building on an earlier mapping to KPZ growth with a moving defect (or an equivalent directed polymer pinning problem) we determine the phase structure, beyond the previously-studied one-dimensional case, for both Bayes-optimal and suboptimal inference. In $d=2$, theoretical analysis and numerical simulations both give a depinning transition between a successful tracking phase and a failure phase. Weak-coupling RG shows that Bayes-optimal tracking is always successful in $d=2$, but failure can arise from overconfident (suboptimal) inference. In $d=3$, tracking can succeed, or can fail in two distinct ways: the posterior probability distribution may delocalize (no detection), or may become sharply localized, but at the wrong position (a false detection). The two possibilities correspond to Edwards-Wilkinson or Kardar-Parisi-Zhang statistics for the log-posterior. The three phases meet at a Nishimori-like multicritical point on a Bayes-optimal line in a two-parameter phase diagram. (Model misspecification alone can drive depinning into either unpinned phase: underconfidence gives diffuse failure, while overconfidence gives localized-but-wrong failure.) We analyze the transitions between the various phases numerically and with renormalization group arguments. We show that some of these have unusual critical behavior, which the conventional $ε$ expansion fails to describe. Recent rigorous results for directed polymers indicate an alternative scenario. Many of our results, including a scaling relation for exponents at pinning transitions and results for RG flows, are relevant to other phase transitions that involve surface growth or directed polymers in 2+1D or 3+1D.

</details>


### [98] [Nonlinear Fluctuating Hydrodynamics from Interacting Noisy Quantum Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00159)
*Alexios Christopoulos,João Costa,Stefano Scopa,Jacopo De Nardis,Zala Lenarčič,Denis Bernard,Tony Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从微观林德布拉德动力学出发，推导出边界驱动XXZ自旋链在体相去相作用下的宏观涨落理论（MFT），揭示了相互作用量子多体系统中非平衡稳态的普适性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找相互作用量子多体系统中非平衡稳态的普遍刻画是统计物理的核心难题之一。

Method: 基于边界驱动XXZ自旋链与体相去相的林德布拉德动力学，推导出有效的经典宏观涨落理论（MFT），提取密度依赖的扩散系数与迁移率。

Result: 成功计算了稳态密度分布、长程关联和电流的全计数统计，并与张量网络模拟高度一致。

Conclusion: 噪声量子多体系统可实现真正相互作用的扩散物质普适类，MFT为这类系统提供了强大而普适的理论框架。

Abstract: A universal characterization of non-equilibrium steady states in interacting quantum many-body systems remains one of the central challenges of statistical physics. Here, we address this problem for a paradigmatic model of diffusive interacting quantum matter---the boundary-driven XXZ spin chain with bulk dephasing---and derive, directly from its microscopic Lindblad dynamics, an emergent classical Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (MFT) governing its large-scale fluctuations. Crucially, the resulting hydrodynamics carries a density-dependent diffusivity and mobility as the fingerprint of interactions. This effective description enables the exact computation of the stationary density profile, long-range correlations, and the full counting statistics of the current, in excellent agreement with tensor-network simulations. Our work demonstrates that noisy quantum many-body systems can realize the universality class of genuinely interacting diffusive matter, beyond the constant-diffusivity class of the symmetric simple exclusion process, and establishes MFT as a powerful universal framework for interacting diffusive quantum systems.

</details>


### [99] [Duality between the level statistics of Hermitian and non-Hermitian random matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00162)
*Ze Chen,Zhenyu Xiao,Yifei Liu,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现了厄米与非厄米随机矩阵在大N极限下的精确对偶性，建立了非厄米版本的Dyson三重道路，并通过解析方法得到了普适的能级统计结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索复杂量子系统的统计描述，特别是非厄米系统中的对称性作用及其与厄米系统之间的深层联系。

Method: 利用随机矩阵理论，通过对称性分类和费米子非线性σ模型的解析延拓，建立厄米与非厄米系统之间的对偶关系。

Result: 得到了三类Wigner-Dyson和七类Altland-Zirnbauer非标准类中非厄米随机矩阵的普适本征值关联函数和谱密度，部分结果此前仅数值已知，现获得解析解并经数值对角化验证。

Conclusion: 揭示了厄米与非厄米随机矩阵理论之间存在深刻的对应关系，提出了由转置对称性组织的非厄米三重道路，为理解开放和耗散量子系统提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Random matrix theory describes complex quantum systems statistically, with symmetry as its organizing principle. We uncover an exact duality between the level statistics of Hermitian and non-Hermitian random matrices in the large-$N$ (matrix size) limit. It acts class by class: the two replica partition functions, given by fermionic nonlinear $σ$ models, are related by analytic continuation. Applied to the three Wigner--Dyson classes, the duality yields the universal bulk eigenvalue pair-correlation functions of non-Hermitian random matrices. This establishes a non-Hermitian counterpart of Dyson's threefold way, organized by transposition symmetry: generic complex, complex symmetric, and complex self-dual matrices. The dissipative spectral form factors of these classes follow in closed form as well. Applied to the seven nonstandard Altland--Zirnbauer classes, the duality yields the exact spectral densities near the origin, the non-Hermitian hard-edge statistics. Most of these statistics were previously known only numerically. Exact diagonalization confirms the analytical predictions, and physical models demonstrate their universality. We expect these results to be the tip of a deeper correspondence between Hermitian and non-Hermitian random matrix theory.

</details>


### [100] [Exact joint eigenvalue densities of non-Hermitian random matrices are Calogero scattering states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00164)
*Zhenyu Xiao,Ze Chen,Yifei Liu,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文解决了具有转置对称性的非厄米矩阵在任意尺寸下的精确联合本征值密度问题，发现其与Calogero模型的散射态波函数相关，并揭示了其不同于库仑气体的独特统计特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定随机矩阵理论中精确的联合本征值密度长期以来是一个核心难题，特别是对于具有转置对称性的非厄米矩阵，现有方法难以处理其结构复杂性。

Method: 通过结合随机矩阵理论与可积系统（Calogero模型）的方法，推导出复对称和复自对偶矩阵的联合本征值密度表达式，并分析其统计性质如复间距分布和两点谱关联函数。

Result: 得到了任意尺寸下非厄米转置对称矩阵的精确联合本征值密度，发现其形式包含Vandermonde因子以外的结构，对应于Calogero模型的散射态波函数；进一步计算显示其能级间距分布和谱关联具有幂律尾特征，不同于传统的库仑气体行为。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，非厄米转置对称矩阵的本征值统计可通过可积模型描述，揭示了随机矩阵、可积性与对称性之间的深层联系，拓展了对传统随机矩阵系综的理解。

Abstract: Determining exact joint eigenvalue densities is central to random matrix theory. We solve this long-standing problem for non-Hermitian matrices with transposition symmetry (complex symmetric and complex self-dual) at arbitrary matrix size. Up to a Vandermonde factor, they are scattering-state wave functions of the Calogero model, a line of particles interacting through an inverse-square potential, with the coupling strength set by the symmetry. In contrast to many previously known joint densities, the densities cannot be written as a gas of eigenvalues with pairwise interactions. We further compute the complex level spacing distributions and two-point spectral correlation functions, which carry power-law tails, absent in a Coulomb gas. Our results shed light on the interplay among random matrices, integrability, and symmetry.

</details>


### [101] [A Human-AI Theorem Connecting Spontaneous and Field-Induced Mechanisms of Collective Behavior in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00322)
*Weiguo Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由人工智能与人类协作发现的定理，建立了统计物理中零场与外场调控下集体行为之间的联系，并证明了该方法在多种自旋模型中的普适性，暗示AI参与系统性科研可能催生自主科学突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人工智能是否能在超出人类主动假设范围的情况下生成科学假设，并研究如何组织人机协作以促进此类科学突破的发生。

Method: 通过构建温度无关的哈密顿量映射，将具有非均匀近邻和次近邻相互作用的O(n)向量开链模型转化为仅含近邻相互作用和轴向单自旋势的简化模型，并推广至Potts自旋体系。

Result: 证明了一个普适性定理：任意非均匀J1-J2型O(n)开链在微观上等价于一个带有轴向场的简化近邻模型；特例下实现了经典J1-J2模型到J-h场模型的映射，并获得了所有q≥2的J1-J2 Potts链的闭式精确解。

Conclusion: AI在持续参与理论推导过程中提出了超越人类直觉的关键映射思路，表明系统性的人机协同研究有助于激发AI自主产生科学洞见，为未来科学研究范式提供了新方向。

Abstract: Can an artificial intelligence (AI) generate a scientific hypothesis outside a human collaborator's active hypothesis space (AHS), and can human-AI research be organized to make such breakthroughs more likely? We document such a case while proving a theorem that connects two basic organizing mechanisms of statistical physics: collective behavior arising in zero field from competing interactions and that induced or controlled by an external field. A zero-field $O(n)$-vector open chain with arbitrary inhomogeneous nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor interaction functions $U_i(S_i\cdot{S}_{i+1})$ and $V_i(S_i\cdot{S}_{i+2})$ is microscopically, via a temperature-independent mapping at the Hamiltonian level, equivalent to a simpler $O(n)$ open chain with nearest-neighbor interaction $V_i( σ_i\cdot σ_{i+1})$ and axial single-spin potential $U_i(σ_i^z)$ for every integer $n\ge1$ and every system size $L\ge1$. The homogeneous linear specialization maps the foundational frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ model onto the canonical $J$-$h$ field model---with $n=1,2,3$ being the Ising, XY, and Heisenberg classical spin models, respectively. An analogous theorem holds when the continuous $O(n)$ spins are replaced by the $q$-state Potts spins with the standard Potts interaction, implying a closed-form exact solution of the $J_1$-$J_2$ Potts open chain for every $q\ge2$ and every $L\ge1$. The emergence of the theorems from sustained human-AI collaboration suggests that involving AI throughout a systematic research program may incubate autonomous scientific breakthroughs.

</details>


### [102] [Parafermions in plain sight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00545)
*Siu A. Chin,A. Chaudhary*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文表明，对于具有离散能谱的势场，理想玻色子和费米子在ξ=-1/m时的插值会导致零温基态能量对应于m个费米子占据单一量子态，且此时巨正则配分函数可能源于真实的para统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在离散能谱下理想玻色子与费米子插值行为的物理意义，并理解其与parastatistics的关系。

Method: 通过分析在特定ξ值下的插值行为，研究零温极限下基态能量的表现，并探讨其与parastatistics中巨正则配分函数的联系。

Result: 发现当ξ=-1/m时，系统表现出m个费米子占据同一量子态的特性，且该结果可由真正的parastatistics导出。

Conclusion: 理想玻色-费米插值在特定条件下可对应于真实的parastatistics行为，揭示了多体量子统计中新的物理图像。

Abstract: We show that for any potential with a discrete energy spectrum, the well-known interpolation between ideal boson and fermion partition functions at discrete values of $ξ=-1/m$ yielded zero temperature ground state energies corresponding to $m$ fermions occupying a single quantum state. The grand canonical partition function in this case can be a result from genuine parastatistics.

</details>


### [103] [Feedback-Enhanced Quantum Metrology and Clock Precision under Thermodynamic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00622)
*Jincheng Lu,Chen Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在开放量子系统中，通过单向跳跃反馈如何将连续监测的量子跃迁转化为热力学资源，并提出了改进的热力学不确定性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索反馈控制如何影响开放量子系统的热力学性能和测量精度，特别是在不改变平均热流的情况下提升参数估计和时钟精度。

Method: 通过将反馈映射纳入倾斜生成器，构建了带有单向跳跃反馈的全计数统计框架，并推导出反馈修正的热力学不确定性关系。

Result: 发现反馈可以增强储层参数估计和时钟精度，且精度提升不受储层熵产生单独限制；信息熵产生补偿了缺失的成本。双量子点系统验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 反馈不仅可作为热力学资源，还能通过信息熵产生实现更高精度的传感与时间保持，扩展了传统热力学不确定性的界限。

Abstract: Feedback can convert continuously monitored quantum jumps into a thermodynamic resource. We formulate full counting statistics for open quantum systems under unital jump feedback by incorporating the feedback maps into the tilted generator. The resulting trajectory ensemble determines both current fluctuations and the Fisher information of the measurement record. We show that feedback can enhance reservoir-parameter estimation and clock precision without necessarily changing average thermodynamic currents. This enhanced precision is not bounded by reservoir entropy production alone. By embedding the reduced dynamics in an enlarged measurement-feedback process, we derive a feedback-modified thermodynamic uncertainty relation in which the information entropy production of the feedback apparatus supplies the missing cost. A charge-monitored double quantum dot illustrates the framework: jump-conditioned feedback improves thermometry and chemical-potential sensing, and stabilizes a quantum clock defined by output-current ticks.

</details>


### [104] [Strengthened second law for periodic processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00639)
*Jake Schaefer,Ben Ansbacher,Jan Korbel,David H. Wolpert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一个与物理模型无关的周期性过程熵产生的下界，强化了热力学第二定律在周期性过程中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在周期性驱动下的物理系统熵产生的一般规律，不依赖于具体的动力学模型假设。

Method: 通过初始分布p0在重复映射G作用下的演化，推导出任意周期过程熵产生的普适下界。

Result: 得到了一个严格强于传统热力学第二定律的熵产下界，适用于不满足CTMC、局域细致平衡等典型条件的系统。

Conclusion: 该结果为周期性驱动系统提供了更普适的热力学约束，可应用于Curie-Weiss模型、确定性有限自动机乃至通用计算系统。

Abstract: Many physical systems evolve under periodic driving: the same control protocol is applied again and again, even though the state of the system itself need not return to where it started after each cycle. We derive a physics-independent lower bound on the entropy production of \emph{any} periodic process modeled by the evolution of an initial distribution $p_0$ by a repeated application of the same map $G$. This is a strict strengthening of the second law of thermodynamics for periodic processes. It does not require that the single-period dynamics arise from a CTMC, satisfy (local) detailed balance, or be subject to other typical restrictions. We discuss its application to spins in the Curie-Weiss model and Deterministic Finite Automata, with possible extensions to other uniform computers.

</details>


### [105] [BKT-like Correlation Scaling and Twist Responses in a One-Dimensional Fractional $U(1)$ Ginzburg--Landau Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00721)
*Hitomi Endo,Michikazu Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了一维分数阶U(1) Ginzburg-Landau模型在σ=1时的BKT类相变行为，发现其关联函数呈现类似BKT标度但无普适螺旋模量跳跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一维系统中是否存在类似BKT的相变行为，尤其是在具有非平凡色散关系的情况下。

Method: 通过随机Gross-Pitaevskii动力学采样平衡吉布斯测度，并分析关联函数、无量纲比值、有效指数和扭转响应。

Result: 低温下关联函数呈代数衰减且指数随温度变化，高温下呈现r^{-2}尾部；关联比和Binder比在(T-T_BKT)(log L)^2变量下坍缩，T_BKT≈0.35；扭转响应不产生有限螺旋模量，线性响应随系统尺寸增长，而尖锐扭转响应按L^{-η(T)}缩放。

Conclusion: 该模型在关联标度上表现出BKT-like行为，但由于缺乏普适的螺旋模量跃变，与传统二维XY模型中的BKT跃迁有所不同。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional fractional $U(1)$ Ginzburg--Landau model whose quadratic part has Fourier multiplier $|k|^σ$, focusing on the marginal case $σ=1$. This dispersion yields logarithmic spin-wave fluctuations, suggesting BKT-like behavior despite the one-dimensional setting. We sample the equilibrium Gibbs measure using stochastic Gross--Pitaevskii dynamics and analyze correlation functions, dimensionless ratios, effective exponents, and twist responses. The correlation function shows a low-temperature algebraic branch with a temperature-dependent exponent, while the high-temperature regime exhibits a nonlocal-kernel-induced tail consistent with $C(r)\sim r^{-2}$. The correlation and Binder ratios are nearly size independent at low temperature and collapse with the BKT-type variable $(T-T_{\rm BKT})(\log L)^2$; finite-size effects set in around $T\simeq0.35\text{--}0.4$, consistent with $T_{\rm BKT}\simeq0.35$. Unlike the two-dimensional XY model, twist responses do not yield a finite helicity modulus: the ordinary linear-response quantity grows with system size, whereas the cusp twist response scales as $L^{-η(T)}$, like the squared zero-mode order parameter. Thus, the transition is BKT-like in correlation scaling, but lacks a universal helicity-modulus jump.

</details>


### [106] [Kibble--Zurek Mechanism and Defect Freezing in Imbalanced-Pairing Kitaev Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00971)
*R. Jafari,Alireza Akbari,Shukhrat Mardonov,A. Langari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了在非平衡配对Kitaev模型中跨越临界点和例外点的驱动动力学，发现配对不平衡参数正负对准粒子谱、相变边界及缺陷密度标度行为有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米系统中配对不平衡对量子相变和动力学行为的影响，特别是在临界点和例外点附近的标度规律。

Method: 采用波函数归一化方法和双正交框架，分析一维和二维非平衡配对Kitaev模型在淬火过程中的缺陷生成行为。

Result: 正配对不平衡下，缺陷密度遵循常规或扩展的Kibble-Zurek标度；负配对不平衡导致时间反演对称性破缺和复能谱，在例外点处标度行为依赖于所用框架，且出现缺陷冻结现象。

Conclusion: 尽管负配对不平衡导致绝热性破坏和缺陷冻结，但超出背景的额外缺陷仍遵循Kibble-Zurek标度律，表明其标度行为具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We investigate driven dynamics across critical and exceptional points in the one- and two-dimensional imbalanced-pairing Kitaev models using both the wave-function normalization approach and the biorthogonal framework. For a positive pairing imbalance parameter, the quasiparticle spectrum remains real, and a pairing imbalance neither shifts the equilibrium phase boundaries nor generates imaginary eigenenergies. In this regime, the defect density follows the conventional Kibble--Zurek scaling in one dimension and the extended Kibble--Zurek scaling, arising from a gapless manifold, in two dimensions within both frameworks. The corresponding scaling exponents are therefore governed by those of the Hermitian transition. For a negative pairing imbalance parameter, time-reversal symmetry is broken, the quasiparticle spectrum develops complex eigenvalues, and the gap closes at exceptional points. For ramps ending at an exceptional point, the defect density follows the modified Kibble--Zurek scaling in the wave-function normalization approach, whereas it obeys the conventional Kibble--Zurek scaling in the biorthogonal framework. When the ramp traverses the time-reversal-symmetry-broken region, a finite density of defects remains even in the adiabatic limit, leading to defect freezing in both frameworks. Although this frozen background indicates a breakdown of adiabaticity, the excess defects generated on top of this background continue to obey the conventional Kibble--Zurek scaling in one dimension and the extended Kibble--Zurek scaling in two dimensions.

</details>


### [107] [Statistical Language Competition Model with Dynamic Edge Weighting on a Random Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01078)
*Somyaranjan Chakra,Mohit Anand Madhesia,Shradha Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究通过扩展Abrams-Strogatz模型，提出了一种基于Erdős-Rényi随机网络的语言竞争动力学计算模型，引入动态边权重机制和基于加权多数规则的代理切换机制，揭示了语言 dominance 与共存的相界，并发现孤立少数群体簇导致的异常持续区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更真实地模拟现实社会中语言竞争的复杂性，本文旨在改进经典模型，引入动态社交网络结构和局部交互规则，以探索语言共存与消亡的临界条件。

Method: 采用Erdős-Rényi随机网络构建语言竞争模型，引入两个新机制：一是动态边权重机制，通过增量Δ增强少数语种使用者之间的连接；二是基于加权多数规则的概率型agent切换机制。通过多尺度仿真分析相变边界、集群行为及噪声影响。

Result: 在(p, Δ)参数空间中识别出语言 dominance 与共存的相边界；发现了位于预测 dominance 区域内的异常持续区，归因于孤立少数语种集群的形成；规模分析显示平均集群大小随网络尺寸N增大而减小，且相边界随随机噪声增加而扩散。

Conclusion: 动态社会连接和局部共识机制对语言竞争结果具有显著影响，少数群体可通过形成紧密子群实现非稳态持久，该机制为语言保护提供了新的理论支持。未来可扩展至三语模型及异质声望/波动性设置以增强现实适用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a computational study of language competition dynamics on Erdős--Rényi random networks, extending the foundational Abrams--Strogatz model through two novel contributions: (i) a dynamic edge-weighting mechanism that reinforces social ties between co-minority speakers by an additive increment $Δ$, and (ii) a probabilistic agent-based framework governing language switching via a weighted majority rule. Phase boundaries separating the dominance and coexistence regimes are identified across a two-dimensional parameter space $(p, Δ)$, where $p$ denotes the network connectivity probability. We further characterise anomalous persistence zones within predicted dominance regions, attributing them to the formation of isolated minority speaker clusters. Scaling study across network sizes $N \in \{50, 100, 250, 500, 1000\}$ reveal that average cluster size decreases with $N$ and that phase boundaries diffuse with increasing stochastic noise. Finally, we discuss extensions to a tripartite bilingual model and heterogeneous prestige/volatility to more faithfully capture real sociolinguistic contact scenarios.

</details>


### [108] [Nonmonotonic control of pattern formation by chemotaxis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01127)
*Mintu Karmakar,Abhik Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了化学趋向性在两类反应-扩散系统中对模式形成的影响，发现其强度可导致非单调依赖关系和再入相变，并引发新的不稳定性及斑点与条纹模式之间的形态转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索化学趋向性在反应-扩散系统中如何影响模式形成，特别是其强度变化可能带来的复杂动力学行为。

Method: 通过理论分析和数值模拟研究具有化学趋向性的两物种反应-扩散系统的模式形成机制。

Result: 发现化学趋向性可导致模式形成的非单调依赖性、再入相变、新类型的不稳定性以及斑点与条纹模式之间的转换。

Conclusion: 化学趋向性在调控生物系统中空间模式的形成中起着关键作用，且其强度是控制模式类型和稳定性的有效参数。

Abstract: We investigate pattern formation in generic two-species reaction--diffusion systems with chemotaxis. We show that chemotaxis can give rise to a striking nonmonotonic dependence of pattern formation on its strength, opening the possibility of re-entrant transitions between patterned and homogeneous states controlled by chemotaxis. In certain regimes, chemotaxis also induces additional heretofore unexplored instabilities. Furthermore, varying the strength of chemotaxis can drive morphological transitions between spot and stripe patterns.

</details>


### [109] [Dynamical phase transitions for single particles in the semiclassical and weak noise limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01197)
*Norayr Asriyan,Jan Meibohm,Vasco Cavina,Massimiliano Esposito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一描述孤立量子粒子的幺正演化和经典布朗粒子耗散弛豫中动力学相变的理论框架，基于动力学生成函数，在半经典和弱噪声极限下揭示了费舍尔零点在复时间平面上的凝聚行为，并建立了量子与经典动力学相变之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立量子与经典系统中有限时间动力学相变的统一描述，揭示单粒子量子系统中也能出现类似多体系统中的动力学量子相变。

Method: 通过引入动力学生成函数，在半经典和弱噪声极限下分析费舍尔零点在复时间平面的分布及其对物理轴的交叉，利用返回轨迹的竞争机制解释相变产生。

Result: 发现费舍尔零点在复时间平面凝聚并导致动力学相变；建立了量子与经典动力学相变的直接联系，提出了统一的基于轨迹的序参量，可表示为经典关联函数或序列量子弱值。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了量子与经典动力学相变的统一描述，表明单粒子量子系统也可展现动力学量子相变，且半经典极限起到了类似热力学极限的作用。

Abstract: We present a unifying description of dynamical phase transitions in the unitary evolution of an isolated quantum particle and the dissipative relaxation of a classical Brownian particle, based on a dynamical generat- ing function. In the semiclassical and weak-noise limits, Fisher zeros of this function condense in the complex- time plane and reach the corresponding physical axes, producing dynamical phase transitions through a competition between return trajectories. This establishes a direct connection between quantum and classical (finite-time) dynamical phase transitions, where the semiclassical limit plays the role of the thermodynamic limit. In particular, the established link naturally provides a classical version of the Loschmidt amplitude and shows how dynamical quantum phase transitions, typically associated with isolated many-body systems, can arise in a single-particle quantum system. The dynamical phases are distinguished by a unifying, trajectory- based order parameter, realized as a classical correlation function and a sequential quantum weak value.

</details>


### [110] [An exact and fast solution of the inverse Regularized Optimal Transport problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01278)
*Dario Mazzilli,Riccardo Piombo,Lorenzo Buffa,Aurelio Patelli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过单次双中心化操作直接从观测的传输计划中恢复真实成本矩阵的闭式解法，无需迭代优化，并可推广到更广泛的传输模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决熵正则化最优传输逆问题中存在的成本矩阵因加性规范自由度而导致的不可识别性问题。

Method: 引入一种精确的双中心化操作来消除规范自由度，从而直接以闭式形式恢复成本矩阵；在已知少量真实成本值时，联合估计温度参数并提供可靠性诊断。

Result: 实现了对真实成本矩阵的精确恢复，无需迭代优化；可同时估计正则化温度参数，并扩展至一类基于可逆关系的广义传输模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为最优传输逆问题提供了高效、解析的解决方案，具有广泛适用性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Optimal transport describes the most efficient way to move mass between two distributions, given a cost matrix for moving mass between each pair of locations. Entropic optimal transport, solved via the Sinkhorn algorithm, is a widely used regularized version of this problem. Its inverse problem asks the opposite question: given an observed transport plan, what cost matrix produced it? This is difficult because the cost is identifiable only up to an additive gauge freedom. Here we show that this freedom can be fixed exactly by a single double-centering operation applied to the observed plan, yielding the true cost matrix in closed form, with no iterative optimization required. When a modest number of true cost entries are known, the same approach lets us jointly estimate the temperature parameter controlling the entropic regularization, together with a diagnostic for the reliability of this estimate. We further show that the method is not specific to the entropic optimal transport, but extends to a broader class of transport models defined by an invertible relation between cost and plan.

</details>


### [111] [Pole-Zero Geometry, Model Reduction, and Identifiability in Sensory Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01329)
*Gunn Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了感觉适应系统中低阶系统辨识的局限性，发现高阶自适应系统在不同拟合方法下会跨越二阶极点边界，导致模型约化结果不一致。通过两态谱分析，揭示了隐藏状态与交叉扩散在精确适应和不可逆性中的作用，并提出了约化模型在生物适应动力学中的解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在感觉适应过程中，传统的低阶系统辨识方法为何可能产生定性错误，以及如何理解模型约化过程中的非不变性问题。

Method: 采用高阶自适应系统的数学建模，结合矩匹配与有限窗口拟合方法分析极点分类的变化；利用两态随机模型研究谱可识别性与适应机制的关系，并推导不可逆性与隐藏交叉扩散的关联。

Result: 发现同一真实高阶系统在不同约化方法下可被归类为实极点或复极点（ρ_moment=4.50，ρ_window=3.31），表明极点分类依赖于约化方式；揭示在无自松弛的隐藏状态下交叉扩散项从谱中消失，恰好对应积分记忆极限下的精确适应；显示高斯路径空间不可逆性仍依赖于隐藏交叉扩散，即使宏观变量无法观测到该效应；并给出漂移项的下界估计。

Conclusion: 传递函数的不变量、约化依赖性质与隐藏状态量之间存在本质区别，这为评估适应性生物动力学低维模型的局限性提供了具体理论框架。

Abstract: Sensory adaptation provides a concrete setting in which low-order system identification can fail qualitatively. We show that one fixed higher-order adaptive system composed entirely of real first-order relaxation modes can be reduced to opposite sides of the second-order pole boundary: low-frequency moment matching gives $ρ_{\rm moment}=4.50$, whereas finite-window fitting gives $ρ_{\rm window}=3.31$, and the inferred pole class changes further with sampling protocol. Thus the real-versus-complex classification of a reduced model is not itself reduction invariant. We then use the general two-state spectrum to connect stochastic identifiability to adaptation: for nontrivial coupling and one-state observation, cross diffusion drops out of the scalar spectrum when the hidden state has no self-relaxation. In the adaptive model, this condition is precisely the integral-memory limit that produces exact adaptation, while leaky memory restores spectral sensitivity. For the exact-adaptation model, the Gaussian path-space irreversibility nevertheless depends on the hidden cross-diffusion channel. Hence $\{H,S_x\}$ does not determine the irreversibility rate. Independently, for a specified all-even reduced two-state drift with $ρ<4$, the drift-only lower bound is $σ\ge τ_x^{-1}(4/ρ-1)$. Published \textit{E.~coli} and \textit{C.~elegans} responses provide biological examples of these limits. The distinction established here between transfer-function invariants, reduction-dependent properties, and hidden-state quantities provides a concrete framework for evaluating the limitations of low-dimensional models of adaptive biological dynamics.

</details>
