### [112] [Green-function Zeros Encode Competing Mott and Charge-ordering Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00124)
*Peizhi Mai,Philip W. Phillips*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过研究扩展的Hubbard模型，提出了一种强关联物质的新范式：格林函数零点的色散由自旋-自旋关联和缺陷动力学共同决定，并在莫特相和棋盘状电荷密度波相之间的转变处表现出不连续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管关联绝缘体中没有低能准粒子极点，但其格林函数仍通过零点保持清晰的动量结构。然而，这些零点的具体物理意义尚不清楚，因此需要深入研究。

Method: 结合解析和数值方法研究扩展的Hubbard模型，分析格林函数零点的色散行为及其与自旋关联和缺陷动力学的关系。

Result: 发现格林函数零点的色散在莫特相和棋盘状电荷密度波相之间的一级相变处发生不连续变化，且该行为对次近邻跃迁具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 格林函数零点的色散编码了强关联导致的有序现象的物理本质，是理解强关联体系中新物态的关键。

Abstract: While correlated insulators are devoid of low-energy quasiparticle poles, their Green functions retain clean momentum structure through zeros. However, precisely what zeros imply is not clear. By studying the extended Hubbard model both analytically and numerically, we establish a new paradigm for strongly correlated matter: the dispersion of Green function zeros is determined by both microscopic spin-spin correlations and defect kinematics. In fact, we find that the dispersion changes discontinuously across the transition between the Mott and the checkerboard charge-density wave phases as is expected for a first-order transition. This physics is robust to the inclusion of further neighbor hopping which simply fine tunes the spin-correlation or charge-defect kinematics. We conclude that it is the {\it dispersion} of the Green-function zeros that encode the physics of ordering resultant from the strong correlations.

</details>


### [113] [Bound states, resonances, and their thermodynamic properties in pseudospin-1 systems with short-range impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00433)
*E. V. Gorbar,Pavlo Sukhachov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了伪自旋-1系统中由短程杂质诱导的束缚态和共振态，发现平带附近的束缚态具有不同于伪自旋-1/2系统的独特性质，其能量随角动量增大而降低，波函数局域于势阱边缘，且热力学量表现出特定行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索伪自旋-1系统中平带附近由短程杂质诱导的束缚态与共振态的特殊物理特性，揭示多组分费米子结构对量子态的影响。

Method: 采用圆形势阱模型描述短程杂质，结合安德森模型分析无序势中独立电子的热力学性质，计算局域态密度及热力学量。

Result: 发现平带衍生的束缚态存在于任意总角动量下，能量随|j|增加而减小，波函数在势阱边缘局域且局域性随|j|增强；熵密度随温度单调上升并饱和，热容呈现单一峰值。

Conclusion: 伪自旋-1系统中平带相关的束缚态展现出独特的角动量依赖性和空间局域特性，显著区别于传统狄拉克系统，为理解平带物理和杂质效应提供了新视角。

Abstract: Bound states and resonances induced by short-range impurities modeled by circular potential wells are analyzed in the vicinity of flat and dispersive bands in gapped and gapless pseudospin-1 systems. We find that the bound and resonant states derived from the flat band show unusual characteristics originating from the multicomponent structure of pseudospin-1 fermions, which are distinct from those for pseudospin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ fermions. Contrary to gapped Dirac systems and unlike bound states in the vicinity of the upper dispersive band, the bound states derived from the flat band occur for any value of the total angular momentum. The energies of these bound states with higher angular momentum $j$ tend to decrease with $|j|$. In addition, it is found that their wave functions are localized at the potential well edge and the localization increases with $|j|$. The signatures of the impurity states in the local density of states are determined. Using the Anderson model for independent electrons in the disorder potential, the thermodynamic potential, entropy density, and heat capacity are obtained. In the regime dominated by bound states derived from the flat band, the entropy density monotonically increases with temperature and saturates, whereas the heat capacity exhibits a single maximum.

</details>


### [114] [Higher-Winding Fractionalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00139)
*Kishore Iyer,Christophe Mora,Daniele Guerci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了高阶涡旋纹理如何通过每单位晶胞多个磁通量量子产生有效的磁场，从而导致在整数填充下出现分数量子反常霍尔态。随着有效磁场的晶格尺度不均匀性增加，系统会发生BKT相变，从分数量子液体转变为介电晶体，并且该相变具有拓扑特征：在通量插入过程中，多体极化定义了一个在分数量子相中非零而在介电晶体中消失的量子化缠绕数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高阶涡旋结构在强关联电子系统中实现分数量子现象的可能性，尤其是在没有分数填充的情况下实现分数量子反常霍尔态。

Method: 通过理论建模分析高阶涡旋产生的非均匀有效磁场对电子态的影响，结合Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) 相变理论和拓扑响应（如通量插入下的多体极化）来识别不同物态及其拓扑性质。

Result: 发现随着有效磁场空间不均匀性的增强，系统经历BKT型相变，从具有非零量化缠绕数的分数量子液体转变为缠绕数为零的介电晶体。

Conclusion: 高阶涡旋可作为实现分数量子现象的新机制，其诱导的拓扑相变可通过多体极化的量子化行为进行探测，为在整数填充系统中实现分数化提供新路径。

Abstract: Higher-winding skyrmion textures can generate emergent magnetic fields with multiple flux quanta per unit cell. This opens an intriguing route toward fractionalization, allowing fractionalized quantum anomalous Hall states to arise even at integer filling of the microscopic unit cell. We show that, in this setting, increasing lattice-scale inhomogeneity of the emergent magnetic field drives a Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) transition between a fractionalized liquid and a crystalline dielectric state. This transition carries a topological signature: under flux insertion, the many-body polarization defines a quantized winding number that is nonzero in the fractionalized phase and vanishes in the dielectric crystal.

</details>


### [115] [Engineering tunable $p$-wave magnetism in antiferromagnetic bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00277)
*Yu-Han Lin,Jin-Wei Dong,Ziqiang Wang,Sen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过对称性调控在双层反铁磁方格系统中实现可调p波磁性的方法，无需自旋轨道耦合即可产生奇宇称自旋劈裂，并通过面内位移连续调控自旋响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找无需自旋轨道耦合即可实现并调控二维系统中p波磁性的新机制。

Method: 基于AB堆叠的双层反铁磁方格模型，引入层间面内相对位移以选择性破缺对称性，并结合有效键调制格点模型与平均场框架下的Hubbard模型进行分析。

Result: 实现了在全补偿磁态中出现奇宇称自旋劈裂，p波自旋劈裂的方向和大小可通过面内位移连续调节；相图显示存在稳定的p波磁性区域，具有共面或手性自旋结构。

Conclusion: 构建了一个最小化的平衡平台，可用于在二维系统中实现和操控p波磁性，为新型自旋电子学器件提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We propose a symmetry-guided route to engineer tunable $p$-wave magnetism in a bilayer system composed of two AB-stacked antiferromagnetic square lattices with their collinear moments nonparallel to each other. We show that an in-plane relative shift between the two layers selectively breaks the symmetries protecting spin degeneracy while preserving time-reversal-related constraints, thereby generating odd-parity spin splitting in a fully compensated magnetic state without spin-orbit coupling. The direction and the magnitude of the resulting $p$-wave spin-splitting, together with the associated spin responses, can be continuously tuned by the in-plane displacement, providing a potential knob for control. We further map the bilayer system onto an effective bond-modulated square lattice and investigate the corresponding Hubbard model within a mean-field framework. The calculated phase diagram reveals extended regions where $p$-wave magnetism with coplanar or chiral spin textures emerges spontaneously. Our results establish a minimal equilibrium platform for realizing and manipulating $p$-wave magnetism in two-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [116] [Self-Healing Diffusion Monte Carlo applied to a simple fermionic model: A critical assessment of the method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01301)
*Michel Caffarel,Manon Pinar,Anthony Scemama*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了自愈合扩散蒙特卡罗（SHDMC）方法在一维周期性边界条件模型中的应用，发现其在标准形式下无法收敛到正确解，改进节点更新准则可部分解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索SHDMC方法在具有部分对称约束节点结构的模型中优化节点的能力，检验其在费米系统模拟中的有效性。

Method: 使用具有一维周期性边界条件和反转对称性的模型，将玻色和费米本征态分别建模为偶态和奇态，并分析SHDMC迭代过程中节点演化动力学行为。

Result: 标准SHDMC在此模型中导致固定节点能量上升且节点收敛至错误值；修改节点更新准则可部分纠正此问题，而一般情况下的收敛可能需要局域基组。

Conclusion: SHDMC并不总能收敛到正确解，其成功依赖于节点区域的精确处理，未来需结合局域基组以实现稳定收敛。

Abstract: We investigate the Self-Healing Diffusion Monte Carlo (SHDMC) method using a one-dimensional model with periodic boundary conditions. An inversion symmetry is introduced to mimic the antisymmetry property of fermionic wavefunctions, with the bosonic and fermionic sectors being modeled by the even and odd eigenstates, respectively. As in realistic fermionic systems, the nodal structure is only partially constrained by symmetry, making this model a non-trivial testbed for nodal optimization algorithms such as SHDMC. We show that the nodal evolution under SHDMC iterations can be cast into a dynamical system exhibiting both attractive and repulsive fixed points. In the standard formulation of SHDMC applied to this model, the fixed-node energy is found to increase upon iteration, and the node converges to a wrong value, indicating that SHDMC does not always converge to the correct solution. We further show that this problem is partially cured by modifying the nodal update criterion to give more importance to the nodal region. Achieving convergence in the general case very likely requires the use of a localized basis set, as is the case for this model.

</details>


### [117] [Correlation-Driven Nonlinear Magnetoelectric Response in an Altermagnet: A Dynamical Mean-Field Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00697)
*Robert Peters,Jun Ōiké*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了强关联反铁磁材料中的光学非线性磁电效应，发现该效应在反铁磁相中存在而在顺磁相中消失，且其频率依赖性反映了自旋分辨的谱结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强关联反铁磁材料中光学非线性磁电效应的起源和特性，寻找有效的探测手段以识别和调控此类有序态。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论，自洽地计算反铁磁序、电子谱和光学非线性响应，避免使用人为设定的自旋劈裂能带模型。

Result: 发现光学非线性磁电效应仅存在于反铁磁相，其频率依赖性揭示了自旋分辨谱特征，并可估算自旋劈裂尺度；降低相互作用强度（接近相变边界）增强响应，而升温则抑制响应并在临界温度以上归零。

Conclusion: 光学非线性磁电效应可作为探测关联反铁磁序的有效手段，通过压力、应变或化学掺杂等调节手段逼近相互作用驱动的相变边界，有望最大化该响应。

Abstract: We investigate the optical nonlinear magnetoelectric effect (NMEE) in a strongly correlated altermagnet using dynamical mean-field theory. Unlike effective band descriptions with an imposed spin splitting, our approach determines the altermagnetic order, electronic spectrum, and optical nonlinear response self-consistently. We find that the NMEE is finite in the altermagnetic phase and vanishes in the paramagnetic phase. Its frequency dependence reflects the spin-resolved spectral structure and provides an estimate of the characteristic altermagnetic spin-splitting scale. Interaction and temperature tuning produce qualitatively different behavior: at low temperature, reducing the interaction strength toward the interaction-driven magnetic phase boundary enhances the response, whereas increasing the temperature suppresses it and drives it to zero above the critical temperature. These results establish the optical NMEE as a probe of correlated altermagnetic order and suggest that tuning parameters such as pressure, strain, or chemical substitution toward an interaction-driven phase boundary may provide a promising route to maximizing the response.

</details>

### [118] [Isospectral potentials with Dirac delta interaction: Constrained Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00056)
*Kumar Abhinav,Biswanath Rath,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了通过不连续超势在超对称构造中包含狄拉克δ函数的量子势，展示了其对离散谱的调控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索包含狄拉克δ函数的量子势在超对称构造中的行为及其对能谱的影响。

Method: 利用自伴延拓施加匹配条件，结合正则化处理不连续超势。

Result: 发现边界条件导致能谱受限和截断，束缚态数量由系统参数决定且需为宇称偶态；特定情况下如谐振子和Rosen-Morse势表现出强限制性谱，而与Calogero型奇异性组合时正则化失效。

Conclusion: 局域奇异性可用于控制和设计量子系统的离散谱，在具有高度局域相互作用的物理系统中有潜在应用。

Abstract: We study quantum potentials containing Dirac delta function (DDF) within its supersymmetric (isospectral) construction via a discontinuous superpotential, instead of being simply an added part. This construction comprises of two independently solvable sectors joined at the singular point by matching conditions imposed by the corresponding self-adjoint extension. Needing careful regularization, these boundary conditions impose independent algebraic constraints on each of the eigenstates, that restrict and truncate the spectrum. The number of surviving bound states, which are required to be parity-even, is thus fixed by the available system parameters rather than by the usual quantization rule. The specific cases of the Harmonic oscillator and the Rosen-Morse potential isospectrally infused with the DDF demonstrate such highly restrictive spectra, while the similar combination of the DDF with a Calogero-type singularity turns out to be largely incompatible as the regularization breaks down. Therefore, localized singularities are capable of controlling and engineering the discrete spectrum of quantum systems, with possible use in myriads of physical systems with very localized interactions.

</details>


### [119] [Encoding Compact U(1) Gauge Fields in Bosonic Modes with GKP Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00167)
*Victor Ale,Tommaso Rainaldi,Enrique Rico,Felix Ringer,George Siopsis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种将紧致格点规范理论精确编码到基于振荡器模式的玻色量子硬件上的方法，通过GKP型稳定子实现紧致性，并在有限压缩下可控制和校正主要误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合紧致格点规范理论（基于角变量和整数电通量）与连续、无界正交分量的玻色量子硬件之间的差距。

Method: 使用一对一编码，在满足高斯定律后，每个剩余规范自由度由单个振荡器模式承载，相互作用由三角门构建，并利用GKP型稳定子实现紧致性；在无限压缩极限下编码是精确的，有限压缩下的主要缺陷表现为可观测量的小幅可计算偏移。

Result: 应用于紧致QED₃，推导出有限压缩下的误差预算，以闭式表征主导误差并展示其可校正性；设计了检测和消除光子损失位移分量的综合征提取协议，并通过一plaquette实例验证了对紧致转子动力学的复现能力，实时谱学结合外推恢复了电荷扇区间的指数小能隙。

Conclusion: 该编码方案有效连接了紧致规范理论与玻色硬件，误差可控且可外推消除，为在量子硬件上模拟规范理论提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Compact lattice gauge theories are formulated in terms of angular variables and integer electric fluxes, while bosonic quantum hardware provides oscillator modes with continuous, unbounded quadratures. We bridge this gap with a one-to-one encoding. After Gauss's law is solved, each remaining gauge degree of freedom is carried by a single oscillator mode, with its interactions built from trigonometric gates, and a Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill (GKP)-type stabilizer provides the compactness that the hardware does not. The encoding becomes exact in the limit of infinite squeezing, and at finite squeezing, the leading imperfections act as small, computable shifts of physical observables rather than uncontrolled leakage. We apply the construction to compact QED$_3$ and derive the error budget at finite squeezing, characterizing the leading errors in closed form, and showing that they can be corrected, subtracted, or extrapolated away. We construct syndrome-extraction protocols that detect and remove the displacement component of photon loss, delimit the noise it does not reach, compare two choices of dynamical variables, and collect the scaling of mode count, gate count, and measurement cost. A one-plaquette example reproduces the exact compact-rotor dynamics, and real-time spectroscopy with controlled extrapolations recovers the exponentially small energy splitting between charge sectors, the seed of the monopole physics of the theory, at the percent level against its exact value.

</details>


### [120] [Entangling capability of coherently controlled quantum processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00168)
*Beyza Aslanbaş,Bedirhan Alkan,Göktuğ Karpat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了通过相干控制量子过程（如量子开关和时间翻转）生成纠缠的能力，推导出多种量子通道下纠缠度的精确表达式，并揭示了在特定条件下即使存在耗散也能实现最大纠缠的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子过程中纠缠生成机制，理解相干控制对量子通道顺序或输入输出方向的影响，探索纠缠能力的极限。

Method: 保留控制比特并将其与目标系统视为二分系统，在纯积态输入下分析输出纠缠；针对量子开关和时间翻转过程，使用解析方法推导纠缠度量，结合几何表征和相干-纠缠守恒关系进行分析。

Result: 得到了qubit幺正操作量子开关的纠缠精确表达式；证明了复合可交换通道无法产生纠缠，但某些耗散通道（如振幅阻尼与比特翻转）组合可生成最大纠缠态；不同稳态布居的广义振幅阻尼通道可产生纠缠；对时间翻转过程也获得了类似结果。

Conclusion: 相干控制量子通道的顺序或方向能有效生成纠缠，即使在有噪声环境下也可能达到最大纠缠，这为量子信息处理中的资源优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We analytically characterize entanglement generation by two paradigmatic coherently controlled quantum processes, the quantum switch and time-flip. Retaining rather than measuring or discarding the control, we treat the control and target as the bipartite system and assume pure product inputs, so that any output entanglement reflects the entangling capability of the process. For the switch of qubit unitaries, we derive exact expressions for entanglement, together with a geometric characterization and a coherence-entanglement conservation relation. We then extend our analysis to binary random unitary, Pauli, amplitude damping, and generalized amplitude damping channels. We prove that switch of channels commuting under composition cannot entangle a separable input. Yet maximal entanglement is possible even with dissipation, e.g., an entanglement-breaking damping channel switched with a bit-flip can transform a product input into a maximally entangled state. For two generalized amplitude damping channels, a common stationary state prevents entanglement, while different stationary populations can enable it. We present an analogous study for the time-flip of unitary and binary random unitary channels. Our findings provide a systematic analysis of how much entanglement can be generated by coherent control of channel order or input-output direction.

</details>


### [121] [Exact learning of quantum noise with tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00169)
*Nicola Pancotti,Vedika Saravanan,Krysta Svore*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种变分框架，直接从量子纠错实验数据中学习噪声模型，结合梯度下降优化，能高效恢复物理上有意义的噪声参数，并实时跟踪设备噪声漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的噪声模型对高性能量子纠错至关重要，但传统表征方法依赖额外实验，成本高且难以实时更新。因此需要一种能直接从纠错运行数据中学习噪声的方法。

Method: 将故障事件概率作为变分参数，通过最小化解码器预测与实际逻辑观测结果之间的二值交叉熵，利用梯度下降进行优化；采用张量网络解码器实现精确的最大似然解码和解析梯度计算。

Result: 在Sycamore处理器的电路级数据上验证，所学噪声模型的逻辑错误率与谷歌独立表征的模型相差仅2%，噪声参数均方误差随训练明显下降，且可实时跟踪合成噪声演化，保持近最优解码性能。

Conclusion: 该方法无需专用表征实验即可学习物理一致且高性能的噪声模型，具备实时适应能力，形式上与解码器无关，可推广至关联噪声场景。

Abstract: Accurate noise models are essential for high-performance quantum error correction, yet characterizing the noise of a quantum device typically requires dedicated experiments. We present a variational framework that learns the noise model directly from quantum-error-correction syndrome and logical-observable data collected during error-corrected memory experiments. The fault-event probabilities are treated as variational parameters and optimized via gradient descent to minimize the binary cross-entropy between the decoder's predictions and experimental logical-observable outcomes. We prove that this objective is principled rather than ad hoc: a sufficiently expressive noise ansatz attains the information-theoretic minimum logical error rate. We instantiate this framework using a tensor-network decoder, which provides exact maximum-likelihood decoding and analytically differentiable gradients with respect to all noise parameters. Using circuit-level data from Google's Sycamore processor, and starting from an uninformed prior, the optimization recovers noise models whose logical error rates agree to within $2\%$ with those of Google's independently characterized detector error model. The mean squared error between the learned and reference noise parameters shows a clear overall decrease throughout training, confirming that the method recovers physically meaningful noise structure, not merely parameters that happen to decode well. We further demonstrate that the optimization can track device drifts in real time via warm-started updates, maintaining near-optimal decoding performance under synthetically evolving noise without the need for re-characterization. The approach is decoder-agnostic in its formulation and naturally extends to correlated noise models.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum Secret Sharing with a Helper and Programmable Access Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00220)
*Eric Chitambar,Sarah Hagen,David W. Kribs,Mike I. Nelson,Andrew Nemec*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种称为“helper QSS”的量子秘密共享变体，引入辅助持有者，并实现了可编程访问结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种新的量子秘密共享方案，使得特定子集可以恢复状态，并实现第三方在分发份额后仍能盲选访问结构。

Method: 提出了helper QSS模型，构建适用于任意数量参与方和系统规模的helper码族，利用量子导引实现盲目的非本地可编程访问结构。

Result: 实现了具有辅助持有者的量子秘密共享方案，支持任意参与方规模的编码，并成功应用量子导引实现第三方独立、盲性选择访问结构。

Conclusion: helper QSS为量子秘密共享提供了更灵活的访问控制机制，可编程访问结构拓展了其在分布式量子计算中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Quantum secret sharing (QSS) is a process in which the state of a quantum system is partitioned into multiple shares, allowing only specific subsets of shareholders to reconstruct the state, while others gain no information about its identity. In this work, we propose a variant of QSS, called ``helper QSS,'' which designates a special shareholder whose participation with any other group of parties is sufficient for recovering the state. We present different families of helper codes that function for an arbitrary number of parties and system sizes. As an application, we introduce the idea of a programmable access structure in QSS, which allows for a third-party programmer to independently choose the access structure of the code even after the shares are delivered to all the shareholders. This choice is made blindly, meaning that the programmer has no information about the secret being encoded, and it is implemented using the nonlocal process of quantum steering.

</details>


### [123] [A Backend-Agnostic MWIS Kernel for Stochastic Unit Commitment with Neutral-Atom Hardware Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01248)
*Jiying Chen,Min Lin,Jingwei Wen,Zhihong Zhang,Chuixiong Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后端无关的计算接口，将随机机组组合的离散决策层编译为最大权重独立集（MWIS）问题，并在QuEra Aquila中性原子量子处理器上实现了端到端的工业调度工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件在解决结构化组合优化方面初现成效，但实际应用仍受限于问题映射和噪声输出恢复两个瓶颈。

Method: 设计一种与后端无关的计算接口，将离散决策层转化为MWIS问题，保留连续调度和可行性恢复在经典计算层，并在绿色氢气调度场景中验证该方法。

Result: 在50节点实例上的15天硬件实验中，经经典优化后的硬件生成解达到或超过精确MWIS的调度边界；扩展至144节点时编码质量稳定，阵列存活率成为主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了通向更大规模问题的硬件兼容计算路径，为探索经典精确优化难以扩展的领域奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum hardware is beginning to address structured combinatorial optimisation, but two steps still block practical use: mapping real operational models onto hardware-compatible instances, and converting noisy hardware output back into feasible decisions. Here we introduce a backend-agnostic computational interface that compiles the discrete decision layer of stochastic unit commitment into a move-based maximum-weight independent set (MWIS) problem, while retaining continuous dispatch and feasibility recovery in the classical computational layer. We validate the approach in a green hydrogen scheduling setting and deploy it on the QuEra Aquila neutral-atom quantum processor. This is the first end-to-end industrial scheduling workflow that connects real operational decisions to programmable neutral-atom hardware through a solver-agnostic MWIS representation. Across a 15-day hardware campaign on 50-node instances, hardware-generated solutions after classical refinement match or exceed the dispatch margins obtained from exact MWIS on every day. When scaling to 144 nodes, encoding quality remains stable, while the probability that the full atom array survives, rather than graph embedding, emerges as the dominant bottleneck to further scaling. Together, these results establish a hardware-compatible computational pathway toward larger problem scales, and lay the groundwork for exploring regimes in which exact classical optimisation may no longer scale efficiently.

</details>


### [124] [Codes for Quantum Secret Sharing with a Helper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00234)
*Eric Chitambar,Sarah Hagen,David W. Kribs,Zhao Ma,Mike I. Nelson,Andrew Nemec*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子秘密共享中的辅助者编码结构，提出了新的编码构造方法，完全刻画了盲辅助稳定子码的结构，并探讨了在不同条件下通过LOCC实现秘密恢复的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解并扩展量子秘密共享中辅助者的角色，特别是当辅助者不持有秘密信息时如何有效协助解码。

Method: 分析QSS辅助码的一般结构，提出新编码构造，利用稳定子形式化方法刻画盲辅助码，并研究LOCC协议下的恢复能力。

Result: 完全表征了单量子比特编码下盲辅助稳定子码的结构；证明此时总能通过从辅助者到目标方的一路LOCC完成恢复；展示了辅助者可通过LOCC授权更大群体恢复秘密；并在每方仅持一个量子比特时给出了所有辅助码的通用形式，发现LOCC恢复仅在特殊情况下可行。

Conclusion: 盲辅助稳定子码在单量子比特编码中具有明确结构且支持高效LOCC恢复，但推广到非稳定子码时LOCC恢复受限，揭示了辅助者在量子秘密共享中的能力与限制。

Abstract: Helper quantum secret sharing is a form of secret sharing defined by its unique access structure. One special fixed party, called the helper, can work together with any other party to fully decode the secret. A blind helper is one who can provide this assistance while not holding any local information about the encoded secret. In this work, we analyze the general structure of QSS helper codes and present new code constructions. We fully characterize the structure of blind helper stabilizer codes and show that for the encoding of a single qubit, recovery is always possible using one-way local operations and classical communication (LOCC) from the helper to the targeted party. Furthermore, we demonstrate how such codes also allow for the helper to target larger subsets of parties by one-way LOCC, enabling them to be authorized to recover the secret. Finally, when each party only holds a qubit, we identify the general form of all helper codes (including non-stabilizer codes) and find that LOCC recovery is only possible in special cases.

</details>


### [125] [Optimization Landscape Geometry in VQE for Frustrated Quantum Spin Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00235)
*Vojtěch Novák,Ivan Zelinka,Swagatam Das,Martin Beseda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过在受控的挫败自旋模型层次结构上基准测试八种经典优化器，探讨了变分量子本征求解（VQE）中的优化性能。研究结合哈密顿-变分电路的景观特性（如局部极小值、梯度、曲率等），分析不同优化器在能量收敛和可达性方面的表现，并考察电路深度对表达能力与优化几何的影响。结果显示，优化器性能显著依赖于模型类型和景观结构，且变分间隙是独立的误差来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在不同挫败自旋模型中，经典优化器在VQE中的实际表现及其与变分电路表达能力和能量景观结构的关系，从而揭示影响量子算法性能的关键因素。

Method: 在从对角伊辛玻璃到横向场伊辛及各向异性海森堡模型的层次结构上，使用匹配的函数评估预算，对八类经典优化器（包括局部、随机梯度、进化、协方差适应和群体智能方法）进行基准测试；采用简单变分电路（如Ry乘积态和浅层Ry-CNOT硬件高效电路），并研究电路深度变化对表达性、可达性和优化几何的影响。

Result: 发现优化器性能随模型层次显著变化，且与能量景观的结构（如局部极小值分布、梯度和曲率）密切相关；增加电路深度可提升表达性和可达性，但可能改变优化难度；变分间隙构成独立于优化过程的误差源。

Conclusion: VQE在挫败自旋模型上的性能由经典优化方法的选择、变分电路的表达能力以及哈密顿-变分景观的几何特征共同决定，需综合考虑三者以提升算法效率。

Abstract: We benchmark eight classical optimizers for exact-statevector VQE calculations on a controlled hierarchy of frustrated spin models, ranging from a diagonal Ising glass to transverse-field Ising and anisotropic Heisenberg models. The benchmark includes local, stochastic-gradient, evolutionary, covariance-adaptation, and swarm-based optimization methods under matched function-evaluation budgets. To understand their performance beyond final energies, we characterize the underlying Hamiltonian--ansatz landscapes in terms of local minima, gradients, curvature, and ground-state reachability. We use simple variational circuits, from an $R_y$ product-state ansatz for the diagonal model to shallow $R_y$--CNOT hardware-efficient circuits for the noncommuting models, and study how increasing circuit depth changes their expressivity, reachability, and optimization geometry. We find that optimizer performance changes substantially across the model hierarchy and is closely connected to landscape structure, while the variational gap represents a separate source of error. These results show how classical optimization, variational expressivity, and landscape geometry jointly determine VQE performance for frustrated spin models.

</details>


### [126] [A nonabelian anyon violates Haag duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01267)
*Daniel Wallick,Henrik Wilming*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了描述单个非阿贝尔任意子的超选择sector违反了Haag对偶性，这等价于量子信息理论中净化唯一性的基本原则。作者通过物理论证和基于sector理论的严格证明，并在Levin-Wen模型中具体实现该结果。此外，文章指出相应的基态尽管是纯态，但不允许量子导引，并且违反近似Haag对偶性，从而推翻了所有能隙基态都满足此条件的猜想。结果表明，在某些能隙物相中，Haag对偶性在整个相空间均失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非阿贝尔任意子对应的超选择sector对Haag对偶性的影响，检验量子信息原理（如净化唯一性）在拓扑序系统中的适用性，并探讨能隙基态是否普遍满足Haag对偶性或其近似形式。

Method: 结合物理直观论证与代数量子场论中的sector理论进行严格数学证明，并以Levin-Wen模型作为具体实例来验证理论结果。

Result: 1. 单个非阿贝尔任意子的超选择sector违反Haag对偶性；2. 相应的基态不支持量子导引；3. 该基态还违反近似Haag对偶性，反例表明并非所有能隙基态都满足该性质。

Conclusion: 存在一类具有拓扑序的能隙物相，在整个相中Haag对偶性均不成立，这对拓扑量子计算和凝聚态中的对偶性假设提出了重要修正。

Abstract: We show that a superselction sector describing a single nonabelian anyon violates Haag duality, which is equivalent to the fundamental quantum-information theoretic principle of uniqueness of purifications. We provide a simple physics argument as well as a rigorous proof using sector theory, and instantiate our result concretely in Levin-Wen models. We also show that the associated ground state does not allow for quantum steering despite being a pure state. Finally, we show that this ground state violates approximate Haag duality, disproving the conjecture that all gapped ground states satisfy this condition. Our result implies that there are gapped phases of matter where Haag duality fails at every point in the phase.

</details>


### [127] [Which Otto Engine Is the Fastest?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00321)
*Idriss Hank Nkouatchoua Ngueya,Marcin Łobejko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了四种不同实现方式的奥托热机，发现尽管动力学机制不同，其功率均可分解为一个与系统内频率和温度相关的普适特征功，以及一个依赖于热浴耦合和驱动方式的实现特异性特征时间。通过比较特征时间，可统一评估不同引擎的性能，并揭示其运行速度受限于冷热浴热化时间之和。此外，引擎分为两类：驱动与热化同时进行的呈现二次标度，交替进行的则呈现线性标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解不同类型热机的速度决定因素，并建立可比较不同实现方式热机性能的统一框架，特别是在保持相同奥托效率的前提下比较其输出功率。

Method: 分析四种具有相同奥托效率但动力学实现不同的奥托引擎，将其功率分解为特征功与特征时间的乘积，进而识别出决定运行速度的关键参数，并基于热化与做功过程的时间安排对引擎进行分类。

Result: 发现所有引擎的最大运行速度受限于冷热浴热化时间之和；功率可分解为普适特征功与实现相关特征时间的乘积；四种引擎可分为两类：同步型（驱动与热化同时）具二次标度，交替型（做功与热化交替）具线性标度。

Conclusion: 不同实现方式的量子奥托引擎在功率表现上具有共通的动力学规律，可通过特征时间统一比较其性能，揭示了热化时间总和是速度上限的决定因素，并提出了基于操作时序的分类框架。

Abstract: A general thermal machine is characterized by distinct time or energy scales: temperature, coupling to the heat baths, internal free dynamics and interactions, and external driving. We address the question: what parameters determine the speed of a given engine, and how can they be used to reliably compare different types of engine? Specifically, we consider four realizations of the Otto engine, each operating with the same Otto efficiency, and aim to characterize and compare their power outputs. The main insight of this paper is that, irrespective of their very different dynamical implementations, the power of each engine can be factorized into a common characteristic work and an implementation-specific characteristic time. The characteristic work depends only on the internal frequencies of the machine and the temperatures, whereas the characteristic time depends on the coupling strength to the baths and on the implementation-specific driving. This observation allows us to compare the power of different engines through their characteristic times and thereby reduce the relevant parameter space to parameters that capture the dynamical aspects of engine performance. Despite different dynamical implementations, this framework reveals simple common features. In all cases, the maximum operating speed is limited by a common timescale given by the sum of the characteristic thermalization times of the hot and cold baths. Moreover, the four engines fall into two distinct asymptotic classes: implementations in which driving and thermalization occur simultaneously exhibit quadratic scaling, whereas those in which work extraction and thermalization alternate exhibit linear scaling. These results expose general dynamical features that determine the operational speed of quantum Otto engines.

</details>


### [128] [Depth-1 expanders on the unitary group and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01605)
*Anurag Anshu,Shankar Balasubramanian,Jonas Haferkamp,Aram W. Harrow,Xinyu Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个常数度和常数能隙的量子扩展器，其每个酉操作可通过深度为1的一维Pauli或CNOT门电路实现，并将其应用于构造满足最优纠缠-能隙关系的一维无挫哈密顿量族，以及检验一类一维体积律纠缠态的流式协议；进一步将该扩展器推广到包含T门和Clifford电路的酉群上，实现了对酉群稠密子群上的有能隙随机行走，改进了前人工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构造高效可实现的量子扩展器，并解决在量子多体系统中实现最优纠缠-能隙关系以及高效验证大面积纠缠态的问题。

Method: 利用深度为1的一维电路构造常数度和常数能隙的量子扩展器，并将其应用于设计无挫哈密顿量和流式验证协议；进一步将扩展器推广至包含T门和Clifford电路的酉群上。

Result: 成功构造出可在浅层电路中实现的量子扩展器；应用该扩展器得到满足S = Θ(Δ^{-1/2})关系的1D哈密顿量族；提出了可检验体积律纠缠态的流式协议；并将扩展器推广至酉群，实现了对酉群稠密子群上的有能隙随机行走，且能隙不依赖于维度。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了高效可实现的量子扩展器及其在多体物理和量子验证中的关键应用，同时在酉群上获得了比先前工作更优的能隙控制结果。

Abstract: We construct a constant-degree and constant-gap quantum expander on $n$ qubits where each unitary can be implemented by a depth-$1$ and 1D circuit of Pauli or CNOT gates. We provide two applications of this expander. First, we use it to construct a family of frustration-free 1D Hamiltonians whose ground states obey the entanglement-gap relation $S = Θ(Δ^{-1/2})$; this is believed to be optimal, but achieving it had been open. Second, we use it to provide a streaming protocol that tests for closeness to a class of 1D volume-law entangled states.
  Moreover, we extend our quantum expander to a constant-degree and constant-gap expander on the unitary group where each unitary is a single $T$ gate, a single $T^{\dagger}$ gate, or a depth-$1$ Clifford circuit. This implies that a random sequence of unitaries from the expander yields a gapped walk on a dense subgroup of the unitary group. This improves upon previous work by Bourgain and Gamburd which did not control the dependence of the gap on the dimension.

</details>


### [129] [Generation of multicomponent Schrödinger cat states in schemes with measurement of Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00343)
*E. A. Nesterova,S. B. Korolev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多模高斯态光子数分辨测量的方案，用于生成粒子数相差四或八的福克态叠加态，并评估了其与多组分薛定谔猫态的保真度及最优生成配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了生成特定粒子数差的福克态叠加态并研究其与多组分薛定谔猫态的相似性。

Method: 利用多模高斯态的光子数分辨测量来实现福克态叠加态的生成，并通过理论分析比较生成态与多组分薛定谔猫态的保真度。

Result: 成功生成了粒子数相差四或八的福克态叠加态，保真度随探测粒子数变化，并找到了最优生成配置。

Conclusion: 该方案能有效生成目标量子态，且在适当配置下具有较高保真度，适用于量子信息处理中的多组分猫态制备。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a scheme for generating superpositions of Fock states whose numbers differ by four or eight. The proposed protocol is based on photon-number-resolving measurements on multimode Gaussian states. We compare of the generated states with multicomponent Schrödinger cat states. We evaluate the fidelity and determine how it scales with the number of detected particles. Furthermore, we derive the optimal configuration for the generation such states.

</details>


### [130] [Spatial correlations of photons interacting via transverse Rydberg blockade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00348)
*Bankim Chandra Das,Daniil Svirskiy,Matthias Metternich,Wolfgang Alt,Sebastian Hofferberth,Ofer Firstenberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在里德堡非线性介质中两个相互作用光子的横向空间动力学，扩展了一维情况下的研究，探讨了里德堡极化激元传播时的纵向和横向关联特性。研究发现，横向关联主要由衍射引起，而纵向关联则受扩散影响，并指出横向关联长度主要由阻塞半径决定，纵向关联长度受限于带宽。通过建立包含高斯光束和非均匀原子密度分布的模型，推导出两个方向上的关联函数定量预测，并将传播方程简化为类薛定谔方程，得到了与全数值结果一致的近似解。研究表明，空间光子关联可直接用于测量阻塞半径，验证了横向里德堡阻塞作为一种独特且可观测的关联机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在里德堡非线性介质中光子间相互作用的空间动态特性，特别是在二维情况下横向与纵向关联的区别，以深化对多光子量子态操控的理解，并提供一种直接测量里德堡阻塞半径的方法。

Method: 构建了一个包含高斯光束和非均匀原子密度分布的理论模型，将光子传播方程约化为一个类薛定谔方程，采用解析近似与全数值模拟相结合的方法，分析了横向和纵向光子关联函数的行为及标度律。

Result: 发现了横向关联由衍射主导、纵向关联由扩散主导的不同机制；得出横向关联长度主要取决于阻塞半径，而纵向关联长度受系统带宽限制；理论预测与数值结果高度一致；证实了空间光子关联可用于直接测量里德堡阻塞半径。

Conclusion: 横向里德堡阻塞是一种独立且可测量的关联机制，空间光子关联为研究强相互作用光子系统和精确测定阻塞半径提供了有效手段。

Abstract: We develop a theory of the transverse spatial dynamics of two interacting photons in a Rydberg nonlinear medium. Extending the well-studied one-dimensional case, we explore both longitudinal and transverse correlations of photons propagating as Rydberg polaritons. We identify distinct behaviors and scaling laws for these correlations, arising from fundamentally different mechanisms in the two directions: diffraction for transverse correlations and diffusion for longitudinal correlations. We develop a model incorporating a Gaussian optical beam and an inhomogeneous atomic density distribution, from which we derive quantitative predictions for correlation functions in both directions. We further show that the propagation equations can be reduced to a single Schrödinger-like equation, allowing approximate solutions that are supported by full numerical results. Our findings indicate that the transverse correlation length is determined primarily by the blockade radius, whereas the longitudinal correlation length is limited by bandwidth. These results establish transverse Rydberg blockade as a distinct and measurable correlation mechanism and show that spatial photon correlations provide a direct means of measuring the blockade radius.

</details>


### [131] [Towards unsupervised representation learning for quantum data: quantum models with inference and generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00372)
*Robin Lorenz,Eric Brunner,Marcello Benedetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从量子数据中进行无监督表示学习的概念与数学框架，利用状态随时间演化映射来定义量子系统中的分解、推断与生成，并揭示了其与纠缠理论中PPT准则的深层联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子传感器、模拟器和网络的发展，未来量子技术可能产生以相干形式存在的量子态数据。传统机器学习中的无监督表示学习依赖于经典概率论的链式法则进行推断与生成，而量子态缺乏通用的标准分解性质，这促使研究者探索适用于量子数据的无监督学习框架。

Method: 引入‘状态随时间演化映射’（state-over-time maps）作为核心工具，定义可见系统与隐含系统之间的联合量子态，并通过该映射建立量子版本的边际态、推断通道与生成通道；提出‘模糊态’（ambiguous states）概念，即存在有效推断或生成分解的量子态，并施加数据扩展一致性条件以确保模型合理性。

Result: 证明非平凡模型必须包含非线性的扩展映射；针对三种典型的状态随时间演化映射，完整刻画了对应的模糊态结构，发现其构成一个与PPT（正部分转置）判据相关的层级结构；特别地，Leifer-Spekkens构造仅对PPT类量子态支持有效的推断与生成，允许存在真正的量子可见-隐变量关联。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子无监督表示学习的基础框架，揭示了量子推断与生成模型的内在限制与可能性，将量子信息理论中的PPT准则与机器学习中的表示学习联系起来，为未来量子机器学习模型的设计提供了理论基础与研究方向。

Abstract: With quantum sensors, simulators and networks emerging, a future of quantum technology may produce quantum states as data---that is, coherently rather than as classical measurement records---thus motivating the study of suitable quantum generalisations of modern machine learning, including the automated, unsupervised extraction of useful representations. Two ingredients are central to the latter: inference, mapping observations to latent representations, and generation, mapping latent states back to synthetic data. Both are related to each other and to joint distributions for training models by the chain-rule of classical probability theory. The fact that quantum states however lack such universal, standard factorisation property thus poses a challenge. Here we develop a conceptual and mathematical framework for unsupervised representation learning from quantum data. Models are joint quantum states over visible and latent systems; state-over-time maps provide a notion of factorisation into a marginal state and inference (generation) channel; models with inference (generation) are ambiguous states---states for which such factorisation obtains---subject to a further consistency condition on extended inference maps as data extension. These stipulations are restrictive: we show that non-trivial models must feature non-linear such maps to the extended space. For three representative state-over-time maps, we completely characterise the ambiguous states, uncovering a hierarchy tied to the positive-partial-transpose (PPT) criterion from entanglement theory. Notably, the Leifer-Spekkens construction supports inference and generation exactly for model classes of PPT states, thus allowing genuinely quantum visible-latent correlations. We also formulate quantum counterparts of exact and approximate inference training, explore weaker notions of data extension and sketch a future research programme.

</details>


### [132] [Fractal dimension predicts quantum kernel collapse in angle-encoded data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00475)
*Ana Paula Appel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出使用相关分形维度D2作为先验的量子比特预算，以避免角编码量子核在特征映射宽度超过数据本征维度时崩溃，并通过实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决角编码量子核在处理表格数据时因特征映射过宽而导致的核崩溃问题。

Method: 引入相关分形维度D2作为量子比特预算标准，采用FD-ASE选择D2个坐标进行编码，并与PCA-95%宽度和其他编码方式对比性能。

Result: 在一层数ZZ保真度核中，使用D2宽度可保持几何活性，而PCA-95%宽度已崩溃；在模拟器和IBM量子硬件上验证了该方法的优越性。

Conclusion: D2是一种有效的先验量子比特预算指标，核的崩溃是映射-数据对和带宽的属性，而非仅由经典表格决定。

Abstract: Angle-encoded quantum kernels on tabular data collapse when the feature map is wider than the intrinsic dimension of the data. We propose the correlation fractal dimension D2 as an a priori qubit budget: encode D2 coordinates chosen by FD-ASE instead of the PCA-95% width or all E attributes. On nine data sets and a statevector simulator (n= 32), a one-layer ZZ fidelity kernel at q=D2 stays geometrically alive while the same kernel at the PCA-95% width has already collapsed. The budget is map-dependent: product-state and IQP maps overshoot it; a second ZZ layer undershoots it. Packed dense-angle and re-uploading encodings still live at the fractal q, but not when PCA-95% features are stacked onto those qubits. Shrinking the angle bandwidth moves the ZZ knee later; stretching it kills the kernel earlier. On IBM Quantum (ibm_fez, 256 shots, n=8) the one-layer ZZ kernel at the fractal width matches the exact kernel (MAE 0.021); past that width both hardware and simulator have collapsed. The ceiling is a property of the map-data pair at a stated bandwidth, not of the classical table alone.

</details>


### [133] [Imaginarity witnessing enhancement via spectral norms of witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00534)
*Yuhang Xie,Yanjun Chu,Yushan Ding,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子虚数性（quantum imaginarity）的检测问题，提出通过已知的虚数性见证算符来改进检测方法。作者推导出在所有自由实量子态上虚数性见证算符最大期望值的严格上界，并基于此将见证算符分为四类，系统分析了其完备性及联合检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更有效地检测量子虚数性这一关键物理资源，需要提升现有见证方法的灵敏度和理论理解。

Method: 利用见证算符的实部谱范数，推导出对实量子态的最大期望值上界，并据此对见证算符进行分类，分析其检测性能与结构特性。

Result: 得到了一个严格的上界表达式，显著增强了虚数性检测能力；将见证算符划分为四类，并对其完备性、联合检测能力和状态识别条件进行了全面分析。

Conclusion: 该研究深化了对量子虚数性检测的基本认识，为理论研究和实验实现提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Quantum imaginarity is an essential physical resource that underpins key functionalities of modern quantum technologies. We improve imaginarity witnessing via the prior knowledge of imaginarity-witness operators. To this end, we derive a rigorous upper bound on the maximum expectation value of an imaginarity witness operator over the set of all free (real) quantum states, which is given by the spectral norm of the real component of the corresponding witness operator. We demonstrate via detailed examples that this bound substantially improves imaginarity detection. We further classify all imaginarity-witness operators into four distinct families based on this bound. For these four witness classes, we perform a comprehensive analysis of their completeness and finite completeness, the joint detection of shared imaginary quantum states by different witnesses, and the conditions for distinct witnesses to identify identical imaginary states. Our results advance the fundamental understanding of imaginarity detection and offer useful insights for both theoretical studies and experimental implementations of quantum imaginarity.

</details>


### [134] [Parametric amplification of continuous-variable entangled state for loss-tolerant quantum distributed sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00671)
*Sijin Li,Wei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于光学参量放大多模纠缠的量子分布式传感方案，利用高增益OPA提升连续变量系统在损耗和探测效率低下时的量子计量灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压缩态在损耗和探测低效下灵敏度受限，难以在实际中实现高性能量子计量，因此需要探索更鲁棒的量子态和传感方案。

Method: 利用压缩态生成双模EPR纠缠态和四模簇态，结合大增益光学参量放大（OPA）构建多模纠缠资源，并通过计算量子费舍尔矩阵分析其在不同损耗条件下的最优灵敏度性能。

Result: 引入大增益OPA后，系统的量子传感灵敏度对损耗和探测低效具有更强的鲁棒性，在几乎所有损耗场景下均显著优于无OPA的传统方案；即使在小或中等增益下也能超越传统方法，并找到了优于单模压缩态的参数范围。

Conclusion: 该研究为在存在损耗和探测不完美的真实环境中实现大规模、高灵敏度的量子计量提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum metrology exploits quantum states to achieve an estimation sensitivity beyond classical limits. In the continuous-variable (CV) regime, the squeezed state has been used to implement deterministic quantum sensing, but the quantum metrology sensitivity of this state is significantly affected by losses or detection inefficiencies, which restrict its applications. In this work, quantum distributed sensing is proposed using optical parametric amplified multimode entanglement generated from squeezed states. It is found that the sensitivity is robust to loss or detection inefficiency when large-gain optical parametric amplification (OPA) is introduced, where a two-mode Einstein--Podolsky--Rosen-entangled state and a four-mode cluster state are exploited for analysis. The quantum sensitivity is greatly improved compared to that without OPA in almost all loss scenarios. The quantum Fisher matrix is calculated for both states to obtain the optimal bound in comparison with our scheme, and it is found that even with a small or moderate OPA gain, quantum sensing can be improved compared with the traditional scheme. The states are also compared with corresponding single-mode squeezed states, finding the parameter ranges where entangled states perform better. This study provides a method for realizing large-scale quantum metrology in real-world applications despite losses or detection inefficiencies.

</details>


### [135] [The Fermionic Cohomology Problem on the Full Fock Space Is $\mathrm{QMA}_1$-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00694)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了费米子上同调的全局问题，证明了在完整福克空间中该问题是QMA₁-完全的，并给出了局部单项式作用下不同模式数的复杂性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究集中在指定粒子数扇区的费米子上同调问题，而全局问题（即在整个福克空间中寻找零能态）尚未被完全理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，明确其计算复杂性。

Method: 通过构造精确的局部单项式微分算符，分析其在完整福克空间中的上同调结构，并利用块链实现技术证明指定度问题的QMA₁-完全性。

Result: 证明了完整福克空间中的费米子上同调问题是QMA₁-完全的，即使每个单项式最多作用于41个模式；同时证明了具有30个模式项的指定度问题也是QMA₁-完全的，且存在一维块链实现。

Conclusion: 费米子上同调的全局问题具有高度计算难度，属于QMA₁-完全类，揭示了其在量子复杂性理论中的重要地位。

Abstract: Fermionic cohomology characterizes the zero-energy states of the supersymmetric Hamiltonian associated with a fermionic differential. Previous work showed that the problem restricted to a particle-number sector specified with the input is $\mathrm{QMA}_1$-hard and belongs to $\mathrm{QMA}$. We study the global problem, in which no sector is specified and cohomology may occur anywhere in the full Fock space. This formulation directly matches the whole-space ground-state question: a specified-degree NO instance may still have zero-energy states in another sector, whereas the global NO promise excludes them across all sectors and their superpositions. We prove that this full-Fock problem is $\mathrm{QMA}_1$-complete for differentials given as exact lists of local monomials, even when each monomial acts on at most $41$ modes. As a companion result, we prove $\mathrm{QMA}_1$-completeness for the specified-degree problem with $30$-mode terms whose hard instances admit a one-dimensional block-chain realization.

</details>


### [136] [Superconducting Flux Memory for Cryogenic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00772)
*Tony X. Zhou,John McFarland,Aruna N. Ramanayaka,Brian Sears,Colin Stack,Aref Fouladi,Robert Smith,Sambarta Rakshit,Zachary A. Stegen,Keith D. Hillaire,Moe Khalil,Robert M. Young,David G. Ferguson,Anthony J. Przybysz,John X. Pryzbysz,Mark Covington,Gregory R Boyd,Jeremy Clark,Aaron Pesetski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了用于超导电路的通量存储器的发展，该技术可在片上超导环路中存储持续电流，为量子比特等超导电路提供通量偏置。研究开发了三种类型的通量存储器，并通过实验验证了其在片上长时间稳定偏置的能力以及在二维结构中实现控制信号复用的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了减少超导电路（如量子比特）控制线路的数量并提高可扩展性，需要一种能够在芯片上持久提供稳定通量偏置的技术，避免依赖外部直流电源。

Method: 设计并实现了三种不同类型的通量存储器，利用片上通量探测器对其进行表征，并测试其在关闭外部直流源后维持通量偏置的能力；进一步提出将通量存储器以二维阵列形式排列以实现控制信号的多路复用。

Result: 成功实现了通量存储器对transmon量子比特的通量偏置，能够将其能态调节至目标频率并在20小时内保持稳定；实验表明外部直流源可关闭，通量偏置仍能维持，且验证了二维布局下控制线数量从N²降低到2N的可行性。

Conclusion: 通量存储器是一种可行且具有扩展性的解决方案，可用于简化超导电路的控制布线，提升系统集成度，并支持未来大规模量子处理器的设计。

Abstract: We report the development of flux memory for use with superconducting circuits. This technology stores persistent currents in superconducting loops on-chip to be used to provide flux biasing for superconducting circuits, like qubits. We developed three types of flux memory and draw comparisons among them for circuit design. We demonstrate the utility of flux memory by using an in-situ flux detector and characterize each approach and further demonstrate that once flux is set in a memory cell, benchtop DC control sources can be powered off, leaving the on-chip flux bias in place. We propose that flux memory can be arranged in a two-dimensional configuration to multiplex control signals and reduce how line counts scale (N^2 devices -> 2N control lines), and our experimental results pave the path to the proposed scalability. We demonstrate the use of flux memory to flux bias a transmon qubit and show the tunability of the qubit state to a target frequency which remained stable on-chip for 20 hours.

</details>


### [137] [High-Rank Encoding Can Improve Approximate Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00778)
*Bikun Li,Liang Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过引入编码过程中的内在随机性，高秩编码器可提升量子纠错码的纠缠保真度，突破传统纯态编码的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子编码将纯逻辑态映射为纯编码态，但这种限制可能牺牲性能，本文旨在探索更一般的混合编码策略以提升纠错能力。

Method: 分析秩一编码器的性能损失界限，联合优化编码与恢复操作，并构造一类需要高秩编码器才能接近完美恢复的噪声模型。

Result: 证明在接近完美恢复时，使用高秩编码器（将纯输入映射为混合编码态）可实现更高的纠缠保真度，且优势在小噪声扰动下仍可保持。

Conclusion: 允许编码过程中产生混合态可超越传统纯态编码的性能极限，是实现最优量子纠错的重要途径。

Abstract: Conventional quantum-code constructions encode pure logical states as pure code states, but this restriction can sacrifice performance. We show that intrinsic encoding randomness can improve optimal entanglement fidelity. We bound the loss from imposing a rank-one encoder and prove it is at most quadratic near perfect recovery after joint optimization. The optimized advantage survives small noise perturbations. An explicit noise family requires higher-rank encoders arbitrarily close to perfect recovery, with every optimal encoder mapping pure inputs to mixed code states.

</details>


### [138] [Thermal screening and critical scaling of quantum energy teleportation in a harmonic chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00928)
*Taisanul Haque*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在有限温度下一维谐振子链中量子能量传输的高斯态理论，通过优化测量-反馈协议，揭示了提取能量受热关联长度控制，并在临界极限下得到精确的渐近规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在有限温度下量子系统中如何通过局域测量和反馈实现能量传输，并理解其在临界和非临界区域的行为。

Method: 基于吉布斯态的高斯态框架，利用热二点函数描述优化的测量-反馈协议，并结合解析分析与数值模拟研究单站点协议下的能量提取行为。

Result: 发现提取的能量由单一关联函数主导，随距离呈指数屏蔽，受热关联长度控制；在零温临界极限下，能量按距离的四次方衰减（E~d^{-4}）；压缩高斯测量可调节提取系数，p-压缩增强能量提取，q-压缩抑制之。

Conclusion: 量子能量传输在有限温度下具有普适的尺度行为，其长程特性由热关联长度决定，而压缩测量可用于调控传输效率但不改变远距离的标度律。

Abstract: We develop a finite-temperature Gaussian-state formulation of quantum energy teleportation in the one-dimensional harmonic chain. For Gibbs states, the optimized measurement-feedback protocol reduces to thermal two-point functions. In the single-site protocol, the extracted energy is governed by a single correlator, which makes the thermodynamic and near-critical limits analytically tractable. At fixed finite temperature, the extracted energy is exponentially screened with distance and is controlled by a thermal correlation length for both critical and non-critical $α$. In the zero-temperature critical limit taken after the thermodynamic limit, we derive the exact asymptotic law $E_{\mathrm{ext}}\sim d^{-4}$. Numerical results confirm both regimes and resolve the crossover responsible for the apparent drift of the effective decay exponent at intermediate distances. We also analyze squeezed Gaussian measurements and show that they modify the extraction prefactor: $p-$squeezing enhances the extracted energy, whereas $q-$squeezing suppresses it, without changing the large-distance scaling.

</details>


### [139] [Quantum-Based Optimization of Gas Throughput in Natural Gas Transmission Networks Under Hydraulic Constraints Using QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00825)
*Alex Ben Ishay,Yuval Eyal,Yuval Cohen,Nati Erez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于量子近似优化算法（QAOA）的图优化方法，用于在满足Panhandle-B水力方程约束下最大化天然气传输网络的吞吐量，并通过Classiq平台实现QUBO建模，在模拟器和IonQ Forte-1量子处理器上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然气传输网络优化问题具有高复杂性，传统方法计算代价高昂，难以扩展；因此需要探索量子计算等新型计算范式以有效求解该类组合优化问题。

Method: 将节点压力分配问题转化为离散搜索问题，构建包含目标函数与物理约束惩罚项的成本哈密顿量，进而转化为QUBO形式，适用于QAOA求解，并在Classiq平台上进行建模与仿真，最终在IonQ Forte-1离子阱量子处理器上执行简化实例。

Result: 模拟实验中QAOA成功恢复最大吞吐量运行点，与经典方法结果一致；在仅使用p=2层电路的硬件实测中，结果仍能紧邻经典最优解（误差在一个压力离散步长内），且电路深度远低于模拟所需的p=30层。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即使使用较浅深度的QAOA电路，也能获得有意义且物理有效的天然气网络优化结果，为近期量子设备应用于实际能源系统优化提供了端到端的概念验证。

Abstract: Maximizing gas throughput in transmission networks under hydraulic and operational constraints is a combinatorial problem whose complexity grows exponentially with network size, making it computationally intensive to solve exactly. This paper addresses the graph-based optimization problem by optimizing nodal-pressure assignments under the Panhandle-B hydraulic equation. By framing the problem as a search over discretized nodal-pressure assignments coupled with a cost Hamiltonian that encodes both the delivery objective and physical-constraint penalties, we establish a unified formulation suitable for the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). The mathematical model is adapted to a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) formulation and implemented using the Classiq quantum software platform. In simulator-based experiments, QAOA recovered the maximum-throughput valid operating point, consistent with classical exhaustive evaluation and classical hydraulic simulation reference solutions. A distinctive contribution of this work is the end-to-end execution of a reduced problem instance on the IonQ Forte-1 trapped-ion quantum processor. Remarkably, the hardware implementation used only $p=2$ QAOA layers, substantially fewer than the $p=30$ layers used in the simulator-based study. Despite this significant reduction in circuit depth, the QPU produced physically valid and interpretable candidate solutions that bracketed the continuous classical optimum, with each located within one pressure-discretization step of it. These results demonstrate that meaningful gas-network optimization behavior can be obtained using considerably shallower QAOA circuits than initially expected and provide an end-to-end proof of concept for near-term quantum-assisted gas-network optimization.

</details>


### [140] [Information geometry of non-equilibrium quantum states: Mixed metric structures on an extended information manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00977)
*Kouji Kashiwa,Hidefumi Matsuda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种信息几何框架，用于描述横场伊辛链模型中非平衡动力学下的量子态演化，重点研究量子费舍信息度量的混合分量 $g_{ht}$ 及其与纠缠熵和Loschmidt回波的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过信息几何方法理解非平衡量子系统中的动力学演化行为，特别是参数变化与时间演化之间的几何关系。

Method: 利用横场伊辛链模型作为平台，构建以哈密顿参数 $h$ 和实时间 $t$ 为坐标的扩展信息流形，分析量子费舍信息度量的混合分量 $g_{ht}$ 及相关几何观测量。

Result: 揭示了几何观测量（如演化速度和方向对齐）与纠缠熵、Loschmidt回波之间存在显著关联，表明信息几何可有效刻画非平衡动力学特征。

Conclusion: 信息几何框架为研究非平衡量子动力学提供了新的视角，$g_{ht}$ 等几何量可作为表征量子态演化的有效工具。

Abstract: We discuss an information-geometric framework for characterizing quantum states in non-equilibrium dynamics. Using the transverse-field Ising chain model as a laboratory, we investigate the quantum Fisher information metric with particular emphasis on the mixed metric component $g_{ht}$ and related geometric observables, where $h$ is a controllable post-quench Hamiltonian parameter and $t$ is real time. The framework treats $h$ and $t$ as coordinates of an extended information manifold $(h,t)$. The geometric observables characterize the speed of evolution on the manifold and the alignment between the temporal and parameter-deformation directions. Correlations between these geometric quantities and two widely used measures of non-equilibrium dynamics, the entanglement entropy and the Loschmidt echo, are analyzed.

</details>


### [141] [Ontic and epistemic states in the theory of spacetime-local beables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00848)
*Nathan Argaman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在贝尔定理限制下，如何通过引入“回溯因果”（retrocausal）或非局域实在变量来构建量子纠缠的模型。提出了一种广义的局域性框架，用以描述基于本体态和认知态的玩具模型，并解释波函数坍缩与 Born 规则的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于贝尔定理排除了局域因果理论描述量子现象的可能性，作者试图探索替代路径，如回溯因果或放宽因果结构，以建立更直观且广泛适用的量子力学诠释框架。

Method: 提出一个通用理论框架，区分本体态（ontic states）与认知态（epistemic states），并通过局部中介的随机玩具模型实现量子纠缠的描述；分析其与量子态及Born规则的对应关系。

Result: 成功构建了一类符合广义局域性的玩具模型，其预测通过本体态和认知态表达时展现出与Born规则显著相似的形式；并为波函数坍缩提供了清晰的信息论解释。

Conclusion: 在回溯因果或宽松因果结构下，有可能发展出一种既保持局域性又可解释量子现象的新范式，该框架为重构量子力学提供了有希望的方向。

Abstract: Bell's theorem rules out developing a locally causal theory to describe quantum phenomena. Many take this to imply that any model of quantum entanglement must employ variables (called beables by Bell) which follow nonlocal rules, even though signaling is local. The alternative is to adopt an all-at-once (block universe) approach, with beables which may depend on both past and future inputs, even though signaling is causal. Within this lenient-causality approach (a.k.a. retrocausal), simple cases of entanglement have been successfully described by locally mediated stochastic toy models, i.e.,~toy models which are local in a sense which generalizes Bell's local causality. Developing a widely applicable reformulation of quantum mechanics along these lines is a grand challenge. This work presents a general framework for such models and theories, and identifies the corresponding ontic and epistemic states. The epistemic state is closely analogous to the quantum state, yielding an explanation for the collapse of the wavefunction. In the case of the models of the framework, it is clear what the information is about. The expression for the empirically verifiable predictions of the models in terms of the ontic and epistemic states displays remarkable parallels to the Born rule. A toy-model example is discussed.

</details>


### [142] [Observable- and state-selective prethermalization and bounds on prethermal lifetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01606)
*C. L. Sriram,Soumya Kanti Pal,Lea F. Santos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了预热化现象，指出即使存在能量尺度分离，也不一定保证预热平台的出现，并揭示了可观测量和初态对预热行为的影响机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在具有能量尺度分离的系统中，为何某些可观测量会表现出预热行为而其他则不会。

Method: 通过分析接近全排列对称极限的系统，结合长程相互作用自旋模型进行说明，并证明Loschmidt回波为有界可观测量的预热寿命提供下界。

Result: 发现预热平台的出现不仅依赖于哈密顿量，还依赖于可观测量和初始状态；并证明了Loschmidt回波可作为预热寿命的下界。

Conclusion: 预热化的出现具有选择性，其机制由哈密顿量、可观测量和初态共同决定，结果具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Prethermalization describes long-lived intermediate regimes that precede equilibrium and can dominate experimentally accessible dynamics. Here, we show that a separation of spectral energy scales, despite giving rise to a hierarchy of dynamical timescales, does not by itself guarantee the appearance of a prethermal plateau. Under the same Hamiltonian, some observables may exhibit prethermal behavior, while others relax directly toward equilibrium. We uncover the mechanism governing this selectivity, showing that the emergence of a prethermal plateau depends not only on the Hamiltonian, but also on the observable and the initial state. Our results apply broadly to systems close to a fully permutation-symmetric limit and are illustrated with a long-range interacting spin model. We further prove that the Loschmidt echo provides a lower bound on the prethermal lifetime of any bounded observable whenever the prethermal and exact dynamics are unitary.

</details>


### [143] [Structure-Aware Placement and Routing of Multi-Controlled Toffoli on Bivariate Bicycle Code Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00852)
*Anik Basu Bhaumik,Suman Dutta,Siyi Wang,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多控制Toffoli门在双变量自行车码（BB码）容错架构上的高效映射方法，利用最优Toffoli深度分解和定向放置策略，显著减少了模块间指令数量，并验证了电路的实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多控制Toffoli（MCT）门是量子电路设计中的基本单元，但在新兴的BB码容错架构上，其高效分解与映射仍面临编译瓶颈，尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用最优Toffoli深度的MCT分解方案，通过外部魔法态工厂直接注入|CCZ⟩态，并设计一种利用MCT分解二叉树结构的定向放置策略，以共置交互子树，减少模块间通信。同时评估不同拓扑中工厂布局的影响。

Result: 相比朴素的顺序首次适应放置，模块间指令数最多减少16.02%；在网格布局下相较线性架构最多减少23.7%。通过bicycle_numerics工具评估，验证了编译后电路的逻辑错误率和执行可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作为MCT门在BB码等模块化容错量子架构上的高效实现提供了实用化路径，显著降低了通信开销，提升了编译效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: The multi-controlled Toffoli (MCT) gate is a fundamental primitive in quantum circuit design, with applications in quantum arithmetic, cryptanalysis, and algorithmic implementations. Being a high-level logical operation, the efficient decomposition of MCT gates into lower-level netlists has remained a major optimization challenge for decades. While emerging quantum error-correcting codes such as bivariate bicycle (BB) codes drastically reduce fault-tolerance overhead, realizing non-Clifford circuits on modular BB-code architectures introduces complex compilation bottlenecks governed by inter-module routing, factory density, and layout. Consequently, the mapping of MCT gates onto BB-code architectures remains relatively unexplored.
  In this paper, we overcome these challenges by mapping optimal-Toffoli-depth MCT decompositions (Dutta et al., PRA, 2025) onto BB-code-based fault-tolerant architectures via direct $\lvert \mathrm{CCZ} \rangle$ state injection from an external magic state factory. We introduce a targeted placement strategy that exploits the binary-tree structure of MCT decompositions to co-locate interacting subtrees. This approach reduces inter-module instruction counts by up to $\mathbf{16.02}\%$ compared to a naive sequential first-fit placement. We also evaluate the impact of factory placement across different topologies, demonstrating that grid-based layouts yield up to a $\mathbf{23.7}\%$ reduction in inter-module instructions relative to linear architectures (Yoder et al., arXiv, 2025). Finally, we validate the practical viability of our compiled circuits by analyzing aggregate execution errors and logical failure probabilities using the bicycle-ISA error estimator bicycle_numerics provided by the Qiskit community, https://github.com/qiskit-community/bicycle-architecture-compiler.

</details>


### [144] [Towards Natural Gas Contract Selection via Quantum-Guided Independent Set Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00881)
*Vivek Dixit,Vaibhaw Kumar,Kentaro Ohno,Alberto Maldonado Romo,Larry Bowden*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子-经典混合框架，用于在噪声量子硬件限制下求解大规模最大独立集（MIS）问题，以优化天然气运输合同的选择。该方法结合了迭代图约简与量子引导优化，在多个基准和合成实例上取得了接近最优的近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然气运输合同选择问题具有高度复杂性，随着候选合同数量增加，搜索空间急剧膨胀，传统方法难以高效求解。需要一种能够在当前量子硬件条件下处理大规模实例的混合求解方法。

Method: 将合同兼容性问题抽象为最大独立集（MIS）问题，采用量子-经典混合框架：通过经典算法迭代约简图规模，再利用量子算法在简化后的子问题中进行优化求解。

Result: 在QOBLIB的15个基准实例中，平均近似比达到0.996，其中14个获得最优解；在含最多900个合同的6个合成实例中，平均近似比为0.989，4个达到最优解。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合框架能有效处理工业规模的合同兼容性问题，为在当前量子设备上解决实际优化问题提供了可行路径，并可作为两阶段筛选流程的第一步。

Abstract: Selecting mutually compatible natural gas transportation contracts is a practically important optimization task in which operators must choose from many candidate agreements subject to temporal, infrastructural, and flow-related constraints. As the number of candidates grows, the resulting search space becomes difficult to explore exhaustively. We study a pairwise abstraction of this task, formulated as a Maximum Clique problem on a contract-compatibility graph, or equivalently as a Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem on the complement graph.
  Building on recent work, this paper studies a quantum-classical framework for solving large-scale MIS instances within the limitations of noisy quantum hardware. The approach combines iterative classical graph reduction with quantum-guided optimization to progressively simplify the search space while maintaining high solution quality. This enables large candidate spaces to be reduced to smaller subproblems that are more suitable for execution on current quantum computers.
  We evaluate the approach on fifteen benchmark instances from the Quantum Optimization Benchmarking Library (QOBLIB), obtaining an average approximation ratio of 0.996 and recovering optimal solutions for fourteen instances, including graphs with up to 186 vertices. We further evaluate the algorithm on six synthetic pairwise contract-compatibility graphs containing up to 900 contracts, where the proposed method achieves an average approximation ratio of 0.989 and obtains optimal solutions in four cases. These experiments demonstrate the ability of the hybrid MIS solver to reduce industrially motivated graphs. The pairwise abstraction serves as the first step of a two-stage screening procedure that narrows the candidate contracts to a smaller set of mutually compatible ones, which can then be verified against pipeline-capacity constraints.

</details>


### [145] [Robust CZ gate against flux line memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00939)
*Yao Song,Xiu-Hao Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过优化通量脉冲来抑制误差生成器并减少门操作后残余通量线状态的鲁棒CZ门设计方法，显著提高了重复门序列中的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真度的CZ门对于超导量子处理器至关重要，但基于通量调谐的实现对脉冲失真敏感。传统预失真方法在存在通量线记忆效应时失效，导致重复CZ门的保真度迅速下降。

Method: 将通量线失真建模为与量子系统耦合的状态经典执行器的动力学，并利用一阶Dyson展开推导由初始通量线状态变化引起的误差生成器，进而优化通量脉冲以抑制这些生成器。

Result: 在一阶模型中实现了预期的鲁棒性平台；在更实用的三阶模型中，优化后的CZ脉冲达到平均保真度99.998%，抑制了所有一阶误差生成器，并使残余通量线状态接近零；在无额外等待时间的情况下，十门序列的平均保真度达99.97%，序列失真率比基线降低约2300倍。

Conclusion: 显式考虑通量线记忆效应能够在重复门序列中维持高保真度CZ操作，解决了传统预失真方法的关键局限性。

Abstract: High-fidelity CZ gates are central to superconducting quantum processors, but implementations based on flux tuning are still sensitive to pulse distortion. Conventional predistortion, typically used for an isolated gate, can recover the desired flux at the chip but it fails if the flux line memory exists, causing the fidelity of repeated CZ gates to drop rapidly. To address this issue, we model the flux line distortion as the dynamics of a stateful classical actuator coupled to a quantum system. Using a first-order Dyson expansion, we derive the error generators induced by variations in the initial flux-line state. We then design a robust CZ gate by optimizing the flux pulse to suppress these generators and minimize the residual flux line state at the gate exit. A one-pole flux line model shows the expected first-order robustness plateau. For a more practical three-pole model, the optimized CZ pulse achieves $F_{\mathrm{avg}}=99.998\%$, suppresses all first-order error generators, and brings the residual flux line state close to zero. With no additional waiting time between gates, our robust pulse achieves $F_{\mathrm{avg}}=99.97\%$ for the complete ten-gate sequence and reduces the sequence infidelity by a factor of about $2.3\times10^{3}$ relative to the baseline under the same predistortion protocol. These results show that explicitly accounting for flux line memory maintains high-fidelity CZ operation across repeated gate sequences and addresses a key limitation of conventional predistortion.

</details>


### [146] [Uniform Hiding of Haar Block Transpose Gram Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01008)
*Hongru Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Haar干涉仪中使用等量压缩的主动输入进行高斯玻色采样时，通过复对称转置Gram矩阵分配无碰撞概率的问题，并给出了与复高斯转置Gram定律之间的总变差距离的显式误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高斯玻色采样的复杂性论证提供随机矩阵替换部分，支持其计算硬度的理论基础。

Method: 结合密集区域下的中心圆形正交系综得分分析与矩形相对熵界限，证明了总变差距离的显式误差界。

Result: 得到了误差界是输出模式数的二次函数、反比于干涉仪大小，且在压缩输入数上一致的有限总变差比较结果。

Conclusion: 该结果为高斯玻色采样在特定条件下的硬度提供了严格的随机矩阵理论支持。

Abstract: Gaussian boson sampling with equally squeezed active inputs assigns collision free probabilities through a complex symmetric transpose Gram matrix formed from a rectangular block of a Haar interferometer. We prove a finite total variation comparison with the corresponding complex Gaussian transpose Gram law. The error bound is explicit, quadratic in the number of selected output modes, inversely proportional to the interferometer size, and uniform in the number of squeezed active inputs. The proof combines a centered circular orthogonal ensemble score analysis in the dense regime with a rectangular relative entropy bound. This result supplies a random matrix replacement component of Gaussian boson sampling hardness arguments.

</details>


### [147] [Nonlocal wavefront shaping through complex media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01017)
*Yanis Trouyet,Neelan Gounden,Pedro Ornelas,Patrick Cameron,Andrew Forbes,Hugo Defienne*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过空间纠缠光子对实现了非局域波前整形，利用未与散射介质相互作用的光子对其纠缠伙伴的畸变进行相位校正，成功恢复了初始空间关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波前整形技术依赖于位于散射光路中的主动校正元件，限制了其在紧凑成像系统中的应用，因此需要一种非局域的波前调控方法以提升系统集成性和适用性。

Method: 采用空间纠缠的光子对，通过对其中一个光子施加相位校正，补偿其纠缠伙伴在穿过散射介质时产生的畸变，从而实现非局域波前整形。

Result: 实验上成功验证了非局域波前整形的有效性，能够恢复因散射而退化的空间相关性。

Conclusion: 该方法在物理上将波前校正与散射介质解耦，为在紧凑及难以接触的系统中实现复杂介质成像提供了新途径。

Abstract: Wavefront shaping is a key technique for mitigating scattering in complex media, enabling advanced imaging and optical communication. Yet existing approaches are inherently local, requiring active correction elements - such as spatial light modulators or deformable mirrors - to lie directly in the optical path of the scattered light, which limits their integration into compact imaging systems. Here, we experimentally demonstrate nonlocal wavefront shaping using spatially entangled photon pairs. By applying a phase correction to a photon that never interacts with the scattering medium, we compensate for the distortions experienced by its entangled partner and restore their initial spatial correlations. Our approach physically decouples the wavefront correction from the scattering medium, paving the way for imaging through complex media in compact and otherwise inaccessible systems.

</details>


### [148] [Photonic fusion operations transform partial distinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01019)
*S. N. van den Hoven,G. B. Lamers,J. J. Renema*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了光子融合操作在量子信息处理中如何改变部分可区分性，发现不同融合协议、预示结果和误差模型会导致显著不同的可区分性变换，并指出在评估光子融合协议时应同时考虑可区分性的动态变化和整体成功概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨现有光子融合操作在部分可区分性方面的差异及其影响，以改进量子信息处理中的资源利用效率和保真度。

Method: 分析不同融合协议在不同预示结果和误差模型下对部分可区分性的变换行为，提出一种改进的融合协议并评估其性能。

Result: 发现在集体资源态误差模型下，修改后的融合协议能平均提高剩余态的保真度；但多数成功的融合结果伴随可区分性退化。

Conclusion: 部分可区分性在干涉和测量过程中是动态变化的，应在评估光子融合协议时与成功概率一同考虑。

Abstract: Photonic fusion operations are a central primitive in photonic quantum information processing and are commonly characterized by their success probability and resource cost. Here, we identify an additional way in which different implementations of fusion operations differ, namely in how they transform partial distinguishability. We consider distinguishability originating both from imperfect entangled resource states and from imperfect single photons. We find that different successful fusion events can induce markedly different distinguishability transformations depending on the fusion protocol, heralding outcome, and error model. Under a collective resource-state error model, we identify a modification to an existing fusion protocol that improves the fidelity of the remaining state on average. Generally, however, these transformations adversely affect distinguishability, and many successful fusion outcomes are accompanied by distinguishability degradation. Our results show that partial distinguishability is dynamically shaped by interference and measurements and should be considered alongside overall fusion success probability when evaluating photonic fusion protocols.

</details>


### [149] [Tangent fermions can restore vacuum stability of discrete-time Dirac models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01029)
*C. W. J. Beenakker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于正切色散关系的替代离散化方案，通过Cayley变换实现Floquet本征值到无界能量的映射，在保持单一狄拉克锥的同时解决了传统Dirac量子行走中布里渊区角部的真空不稳定性问题，并在计算施温格效应时重现了连续极限下的单锥结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统Dirac量子行走中由于2μ质量隙导致的布里渊区中心和角部真空不稳定问题，避免Gupta与Short方法中为消除不稳定性而引入的费米子倍增问题。

Method: 采用基于正切而非正弦色散关系的离散化方案，利用Cayley变换将Floquet本征值从单位圆映射到实数能量轴，并比较标量势通过Hermitian生成元耦合与分裂算符耦合两种方式对布里渊区角部贡献的影响。

Result: 新方案实现了单一狄拉克锥下的真空稳定；在计算施温格效应时，粒子-空穴对产生率与连续情形下单个狄拉克锥的结果一致；当标量势通过Hermitian生成元耦合时，布里渊区角部被精确解耦；若采用分裂算符以保持晶格上的严格规范不变性，角部贡献随晶格常数平方趋于零，优于传统Dirac量子行走中角部不稳定性在连续极限下仍存留的表现。

Conclusion: 基于正切色散和Cayley变换的离散化方案为构建稳定的、无费米子倍增的1+1维量子行走模型提供了有效路径，兼具物理准确性与数值模拟优势。

Abstract: The Dirac quantum walk (a 1+1 dimensional space-time discretization of the Dirac equation) has a $2μ$ mass gap both at the center and at the corner of the quasi-energy-momentum Brillouin zone. Gupta and Short recently noted [Quantum 9, 1845 (2025)] that the Dirac vacuum can create a particle-hole pair at the zone corner with the release of an energy $2μ$ to the environment. They removed this vacuum instability at the expense of fermion doubling, the appearance of a second low-energy Dirac cone. Here we show that an alternative discretization scheme, with a tangent rather than a sine dispersion relation, offers stability while retaining a single Dirac cone. The key step is the Cayley transformation from the unit circle of Floquet eigenvalues $e^{-i\varepsilon}$ to the real line of unbounded energies $E=2\tan(\varepsilon/2)$. We compute the Schwinger effect (particle-hole pair creation in a uniform electric field) for tangent fermions and show that the pair-production rate agrees with the continuum result for a single Dirac cone. The zone corner is exactly decoupled if the scalar potential is coupled through the Hermitian generator of the quantum map. If it is coupled as a split operator, in order to preserve exact gauge invariance on the lattice, the corner does contribute - with a weight that vanishes quadratically with the lattice constants, in contrast to the Dirac quantum walk where the zone-corner instability survives the continuum limit.

</details>


### [150] [Scattering and Tunneling in Real Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01066)
*Firdevs Karakus,Daniil Stepanenko,Igor Volovich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了实量子力学中的演化方程、散射和隧穿现象，证明了实凯勒空间上的Stone定理类比，推导出兼具正交性和辛性质的演化算符，并表明在所考虑的势类下，实量子力学的微分散射截面和隧穿概率与复量子力学结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索实量子力学框架下的基本动力学和物理可观测量，验证其与标准复量子力学在散射和隧穿等关键现象上的一致性。

Method: 通过建立实希尔伯特空间上的演化方程，利用实凯勒结构证明Stone型定理，引入实波算符构建散射理论，并对特定势类计算散射截面和隧穿概率。

Result: 得到了保持正交性和辛结构的演化算符；建立了基于实波算符的散射理论；证明了实量子力学中微分散射截面和隧穿概率与复量子力学完全相同。

Conclusion: 实量子力学在散射和隧穿问题上能复现复量子力学的关键物理结果，支持其实用性和理论自洽性。

Abstract: We establish the general form of the evolution equation and study scattering and tunneling in real quantum mechanics. We prove an analogue of Stone's theorem for a real Kahler space and derive the corresponding general evolution equation, whose evolution operator is both orthogonal and symplectic. We formulate scattering theory in terms of real wave operators and the associated S-matrix. For the class of potentials considered, we show that the differential scattering cross section is equivalent to that obtained in standard complex quantum mechanics. We also show that the tunneling probability in real quantum mechanics is identical to that in complex quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [151] [A continuous-mode quantum-optical representation of finite strong-field laser pulses in free space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01157)
*Szabolcs Hack,Attila Czirják,Péter Földi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种能量一致的量子光学表示方法，用于自由空间中的有限近轴激光脉冲，结合连续模式描述，统一处理相干和非经典强场驱动，并通过实验可测参数建立与量子态的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对自由空间中有限激光脉冲的精确量子描述，特别是在强场物理中统一处理相干与非经典光场的需求。

Method: 基于平面波量子化构建横向矢量模式约化，利用频率依赖的相干态位移α(ω)描述脉冲，并引入正常与反常协方差核来刻画非经典脉冲的量子态。

Result: 实现了由实测脉冲能量和解析信号确定相干脉冲的量子态；对于非经典脉冲，需额外关联信息才能唯一确定状态，并提出了g^(2)诊断压缩脉冲的方法及频域离散化的相敏收敛准则。

Conclusion: 该框架为自由空间实测脉冲与量子光学计算之间提供了能量一致且实用的接口，适用于从经典到非经典强场的统一描述。

Abstract: We formulate an energy-consistent quantum-optical representation of finite paraxial laser pulses in free space. The formulation is based on a continuous-mode description and provides a step toward a unified treatment of coherent and nonclassical strong-field drivers. Starting from plane-wave quantization, we construct a transverse vector-mode reduction in which the selected mode can reproduce an arbitrary normalized spatial--polarization profile. For a coherent pulse, the measured pulse energy and full laboratory analytic signal determine the frequency-dependent coherent-state displacement $(α(ω))$, without introducing a physical quantization volume. For nonclassical pulses, the same first-order field data and total energy do not uniquely specify the quantum state. Additional correlation information or model assumptions are required. We incorporate this information through normal and anomalous covariance kernels and discuss $g^{(2)}$-based diagnostics of squeezed pulses. We also derive a phase-sensitive convergence criterion for numerical frequency-bin discretizations. An exactly solvable continuum-mode free-electron application shows that the coherent displacement reproduces the semiclassical mean trajectory, whereas the field covariances determine the electron wave packet width. The framework offers a convenient, energy-consistent interface between measured free-space pulses and quantum-optical calculations.

</details>


### [152] [Quantum time-flip beats adaptive metrology: Asymptotic benefit, activation, and unsimulability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01175)
*Gaurang Agrawal,Pritam Halder,Aditi Sen De*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间方向不确定性的量子计量策略（ITDM），利用time-flip操作在有限和渐近情况下均超越传统并行、自适应及因果叠加策略的量子Fisher信息极限，并实现了海森堡标度，揭示了时间方向不确定性作为量子计量中真正资源的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计量中，并行、自适应和因果叠加策略在渐近极限下性能趋于一致，限制了进一步提升精度的可能性。本文旨在探索是否存在能够突破这一渐近等价性的新型量子策略。

Method: 采用半定规划方法，严格分析time-flip（TF）辅助协议在参数估计中的性能，计算其可达到的量子Fisher信息（QFI），并与并行、自适应及因果叠加策略进行对比。同时研究一类Pauli噪声通道下的标度行为。

Result: TF辅助协议在有限和渐近多个信道使用下，其QFI严格超过所有传统策略所能达到的最大值；在特定Pauli噪声通道中，ITDM实现海森堡标度，而传统策略仅能维持标准标度，此现象称为计量激活。此外，证明了time-flip和转置超映射无法通过任何有限次查询的传统量子电路或因果叠加策略模拟。

Conclusion: 时间方向的不确定性是一种真正的量子资源，能够在量子计量中提供不可替代的优势，打破传统策略的渐近性能瓶颈，为高精度参数估计提供了新途径。

Abstract: In quantum metrology, adaptive and causal-superposition strategies are proven to be beneficial over parallel schemes for a finite number of channel uses, but their advantages disappear in the asymptotic limit. We show that quantum operations with indefinite time direction, specifically, time-flip (TF)-assisted strategies, referred to as indefinite time-directed metrology (ITDM), can overcome this asymptotic equivalence. Using semidefinite programming, we rigorously demonstrate that TF-assisted protocols can achieve quantum Fisher information (QFI) strictly exceeding the maximum value attainable by parallel, adaptive, and causal-superposition strategies, both for finite and asymptotically many channel uses. Moreover, we identify a class of Pauli noise channels for which ITDM achieves Heisenberg scaling, while all parallel, adaptive, and causal-superposition strategies remain restricted to standard scaling. We call this phenomenon as metrological activation. Interestingly, this activation can be used to exhibit that the quantum time-flip and transposition supermaps cannot be simulated by conventional quantum circuits or causal-superposition strategies using any finite number of channel queries, thereby establishing indefinite time direction as a genuine resource for quantum metrology.

</details>


### [153] [Unbiased sampling from Boltzmann distributions with noisy energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01204)
*Iwo Sanderski,Gian Gentinetta,Giuseppe Carleo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的泊松乘积估计器，用于无偏估计玻尔兹曼权重，适用于高噪声环境下的采样和变分量子电路优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在能量只能通过随机估计获得的情况下（如机器学习分子势、变分蒙特卡洛或量子计算），传统方法因噪声导致采样分布偏差，需要改进。

Method: 引入泊松乘积估计器，该估计器无需噪声方差信息，仅需能量估计的上界，即可实现无偏且非负的玻尔兹曼权重估计。

Result: 在最小基组下恢复了H3+的基态能量，并通过采样映射了变分能量景观。

Conclusion: 该方法在高噪声下依然高效，克服了现有方法对噪声方差依赖和计算不可行的问题。

Abstract: Sampling from the Boltzmann distribution is central to computational physics, yet hard when the energy is known only through a stochastic estimate, such as with machine-learned molecular potentials, in variational Monte Carlo, or on quantum computers, because a noisy energy biases the sampled distribution. The penalty method of Ceperley and Dewing corrects this but requires the noise variance and becomes intractable when it is large. We introduce the Poisson product estimator, an unbiased, non-negative estimator of the Boltzmann weight that only needs an upper bound on the energy estimator and remains efficient at high noise. Using it to optimize a variational quantum circuit gradient-free, we recover the $H_3^+$ ground-state energy in a minimal basis and, by sampling rather than following a single trajectory, also map the variational energy landscape.

</details>


### [154] [Terrestrial Gravitational Wave Detection with Atom Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01227)
*Michael Werner,Ashkan Alibabaei,Naceur Gaaloul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了地面原子干涉仪（AIF）探测引力波（GW）的相位响应，提出了一个修正项，并通过数值模拟优化了实验几何参数，开发了开源Python算法用于模拟探测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高地面原子干涉仪在中频段探测引力波的能力，需精确建模其相位响应并优化实验设计，同时填补现有文献中数值模拟工具的空白。

Method: 通过解析推导得到地面探测器的引力波相位响应公式，并发现了一个新的修正项；使用自研的开源Python算法进行数值验证和模拟，分析最优几何参数下的探测方案。

Result: 发现了现有文献未包含的额外相位响应项，确认其重要性；确定了地面AIF实验的最佳几何配置；实现了首个公开可用的数值模拟框架，可用于噪声和复杂引力背景的建模。
