Conclusion: 该工作改进了对地面原子干涉仪探测引力波的理论理解，提供了关键的数值工具，推动了中频引力波及某些暗物质候选者的实验探测发展。

Abstract: Atom interferometers (AIFs) are highly precise inertial sensors and are considered promising instruments for the detection of gravitational waves (GWs) and certain dark matter (DM) candidates in the mid-frequency band. While GW detection with AIFs was initially proposed for space-based experiments with baselines spanning thousands of kilometers, recent developments suggest that terrestrial setups with baselines of at least 100 meters may also be capable of detecting GWs. In this work, we analytically derive the GW phase response formula of a ground detector, find an additional term compared to the existing literature and check it our findings numerically. Based on this treatment, we analyze the optimal geometric parameters for earth-bound AIF experiments. Subsequently, we numerically simulate GW detection schemes for these optimized interferometers using an open-source Python algorithm. Numerical simulations of these schemes have are not available to the community, yet crucial for the accurate modeling of noise and non-trivial gravitational backgrounds.

</details>


### [155] [Anticoncentration and entanglement in Gaussian boson sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01241)
*Laura Shou,Adam Ehrenberg,Yu-Xin Wang,Joseph T. Iosue,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯玻色采样中的反集中性和纠缠性，推导了对称高斯积的hafnian的二阶矩闭式表达式，并得到了Rényi-α Page曲线的闭式表达式，同时讨论了输入压缩参数不等时的平均情况熵曲线性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解高斯玻色采样在不同参数下的经典计算难易程度，需要深入分析其反集中性和纠缠特性。

Method: 通过推导hafnian的二阶矩和Rényi-α熵的闭式表达式，结合随机矩阵理论与量子信息工具，分析反集中相变和纠缠结构。

Result: 得到了hafnian二阶矩的解析表达式，确定了反集中性的相变点；给出了Rényi-α Page曲线的闭式解；证明了在不等压缩参数下Rényi-2熵的单调性估计。

Conclusion: 反集中性和纠缠可通过解析方法精确刻画，且不等输入压缩情形可由等压缩结果推广，深化了对高斯玻色采样复杂性的理解。

Abstract: Anticoncentration and entanglement are two properties used to study classical hardness or easiness of Gaussian boson sampling in varying regimes. In this paper, we consider three directions concerning anticoncentration and entanglement in Gaussian boson sampling: (1) We derive closed-form expressions for the second moment of hafnians of symmetric Gaussian products, and use this to precisely locate the anticoncentration transition as a function of the number of squeezed input modes. (2) We derive closed-form expressions for the Rényi-$α$ Page curves for Gaussian boson sampling. (3) We study unequal input squeezing parameters $(s_i)_i$, and prove estimates as well as monotonicity of the average-case Rényi-2 entropy Page curve in terms of the magnitudes $|s_i|$, which allow for extending equal squeezing results to unequal squeezing.

</details>


### [156] [DF-SQD: Deterministic Fields for Sampling-Based Quantum Diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01264)
*Kushagra Agarwal,Anupama Ray*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DF-SQD的混合量子-经典算法，通过确定性辅助场电路和选择性组态相互作用，在浅层电路和较少采样次数下实现了比传统SQD方法更优的能级估计精度与效率，适用于当前含噪声量子设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在当前含噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）设备上，需要一种兼具采样效率、误差缓解能力和收敛证据的量子对角化方法，以提升对分子哈密顿量本征能量和本征态的估计精度。

Method: DF-SQD利用双因子分解（double-factorization）选择的叶子节点生成确定性辅助场量子电路，用于提议占据数构型；随后通过选择性组态相互作用（selected CI）对原始活性空间哈密顿量进行经典对角化，并用于指导后续采样轮次的中心化更新。

Result: 在N2分子（32比特）和[Fe2S2(SCH3)4]2-铁硫簇（40比特）系统上，DF-SQD相比标准SQD方法在更小的子空间（减小11.23%）下实现45倍更高的精度，并在硬件实验中以3.75倍更少的采样次数获得更优的子空间恢复效果；在相同50M维度下相对误差降低24.5%。

Conclusion: DF-SQD是一种高效、准确且具备样本效率与目标误差缓解能力的采样量子对角化方法，能够在当前量子硬件上使用浅层电路发现更优结果，并展现出经验性收敛行为。

Abstract: Sampling-based quantum diagonalization method exploits Quantum-centric supercomputing platforms to sample bitstrings for Hamiltonian projection on a quantum computer, and then classically diagonalize the Hamiltonian to estimate the eigenvalues and eigenvectors. In current quantum devices an algorithm is useful when shallow quantum circuits with error mitigation support can discover better results while having either a proof of convergence or some method to explain trust in experiment. In this paper, we introduce DF-SQD, a hybrid algorithm that derives deterministic auxiliary-field circuits from selected double-factorization leaves of the two-electron tensor. The circuits propose occupation-number configurations, while selected configuration interaction evaluates the original active-space Hamiltonian and can recentre subsequent proposal rounds. On N2 (32 qubits; 6-31G basis) and a 40-qubit [Fe2S2(SCH3)4]2- active-space Hamiltonian, we show that DF-SQD improves the energy obtained from sampled determinant spaces while using shallow number-preserving circuits in both simulator and hardware runs. For N2, DF-SQD is 45x more accurate with a 11.23\% smaller subspace, and due to its ability to sample better bitstrings at lesser shots it is 2.93x faster than SQD in quantum devices. For the iron-sulfur cluster, DF-SQD generated a subspace dimension of 221M with 400K shots, while SQD needed 1.5M shots to generate a 238M subspace, thus we have better subspace recovery evident from the hardware at 3.75x reduced shots. At a matched 50M subspace dimension, DF-SQD is 1.32x more accurate (achieves a 24.5\% relative error reduction over standard SQD). So overall, our method is able to discover better results with shallower circuits, is sample efficient, uses configuration recovery (so has targeted error mitigation) and we have empirical convergence observation.

</details>


### [157] [Dynamical regimes of QAOA gradient response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01280)
*Zarin Shakibaei,Alexander Schnell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于动力学表示的QAOA参数空间分析方法，揭示了梯度景观在不同电路深度和调度结构下的普适组织特征，并发现近最优解位于一个中间动力学区域，该区域在原生参数空间中随系统规模增大而压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解QAOA在不同电路参数和问题规模下的可训练性，需要一种能够区分参数特异性特征与整体电路动力学变化的分析框架。

Method: 引入了基于范数加权层强度和代价-混合器不平衡的动力学表示法，并通过MaxCut问题的精确状态模拟分析梯度景观的结构。

Result: 发现梯度景观在动力学变量下具有跨深度和调度结构的粗粒度组织，近最优解位于中间动力学区域；均匀调度能捕捉该区域的大致位置，非均匀调度主要改变其精细结构；随着系统规模增大，该区域在原生参数空间中被压缩。

Conclusion: 该动力学框架有助于分离QAOA有用动力学的持久性与其在原生参数化中的可访问性，为跨尺度理解QAOA可训练性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Characterizing the trainability of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) requires understanding how its gradient landscape changes across circuit parameters and problem size. Yet these gradients are usually described in terms of the native QAOA angles, making it difficult to distinguish parameter specific features from broader changes in the underlying circuit dynamics. Here we introduce a dynamical representation of the QAOA parameter space based on a norm-weighted layer strength and a cost--mixer imbalance, separating the overall scale of the evolution from the relative contribution of the two generators.
  Using exact-state simulations of MaxCut, we find that the gradient landscape exhibits a coarse organization in these dynamical variables that persists across changes in circuit depth and schedule structure, while the finer interference pattern remains schedule dependent. Near-optimal solutions do not simply coincide with the largest local gradients, but instead occupy a distinct intermediate dynamical regime. Uniform schedules recover the broad location of this regime, whereas nonuniform schedules mainly reorganize its fine structure. Across the system sizes studied, near-optimal solution regions remain extended in the dynamical representation while their preimages in the native QAOA angles become substantially compressed at larger sizes. These results separate the persistence of useful QAOA dynamics from their accessibility in the native parameterization, and provide a dynamical framework for interpreting QAOA trainability across circuit and problem scales.

</details>


### [158] [A Scalable Multi-Protocol Platform for Quantum Key Distribution Simulation with Rigorous Statistical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01297)
*Anuj Rathore,Kartick Sutradhar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的量子密钥分发（QKD）仿真平台，集成了BB84、B92、E91和BBM92四种基础协议，基于Python/Qiskit引擎，并支持重复性统计分析与远程访问，实现了系统化的协议比较和可重现的结果评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QKD仿真工具碎片化、仅支持单一协议且缺乏可重复的统计结果，限制了协议间的系统比较和实际部署评估，因此需要一个统一、开放且具备完整损伤建模的仿真平台。

Method: 在统一的Python/Qiskit后端上实现四种QKD协议（BB84、B92、E91、BBM92），引入共享的光纤衰减、器件损耗、偏振漂移及可配置的截获-重发窃听模型；通过桌面（Tkinter/Matplotlib）和Web（React/Node.js）两种前端接口提供本地与远程访问；所有结果基于20次独立运行（每次10000个量子比特）的统计分析，报告均值、标准差和95%置信区间。

Result: 在25公里光纤链路下，BB84平均密钥率最高（160,045 Hz），其次为BBM92（80,023 Hz）、E91（52,815 Hz）和B92（40,011 Hz），顺序与模拟的筛选效率一致；在E91协议中，无窃听时CHSH S参数平均为2.12，有窃听时降至1.58，验证了基于贝尔不等式的入侵检测能力。

Conclusion: 该统一仿真平台有效支持多协议比较与可重复性研究，提升了QKD教学、研究与工程评估的效率，展示了结合统计严谨性与用户可访问性的仿真框架潜力。

Abstract: Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) offers information- theoretically secure key establishment grounded in the laws of quantum physics, yet its practical reach is limited by the prohibitive cost of photonic hardware and the fragmented nature of existing simulation tools. Most simulators support only a single protocol and report results from individual stochastic runs, making systematic protocol comparison and reproducible statistical inference difficult. This paper presents a unified QKD simulation platform that implements four foundational protocols BB84, B92, E91, and BBM92 within a single Python/Qiskit engine. A shared impairment model covers fiber attenuation, source and detector losses, po- larization drift, and configurable intercept-resend eavesdropping. The platform is accessible through two independent interfaces that share the same backend: a desktop application (Tkinter, Matplotlib) for local experimentation and a browser-based web client (React, Node.js/Express) for zero-install remote access. All reported results are drawn from repeated-run studies (20 independent runs, 10000 qubits each), with mean, standard deviation, and 95% confidence intervals stated throughout. At a 25 km fiber link, BB84 achieves the highest mean key-rate of 160,045 Hz, followed by BBM92 (80023 Hz), E91 (52815 Hz), and B92 (40011 Hz) ordering that tracks simulation-derived sifting efficiencies precisely. Under the E91 protocol, the CHSH S-statistic averages 2.12 at baseline and falls to 1.58 when an eavesdropper is activated, demonstrating Bell-inequality-based intrusion detection independent of QBER

</details>


### [159] [Emerging Personas and Tools for Hybrid Quantum-HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01299)
*Eun-Kyung Lee,Jessie Yu,Claudio Carvalho,Yoonho Park,Marcelo Amaral,Tim Osborne,Woong Shin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子中心超级计算（QCSC）环境中的六种不同角色（persona），分析了他们在操作数据方面的需求，强调了在量子计算与高性能计算融合背景下统一数据表示和分析框架的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子处理器单元（QPUs）与高性能计算（HPC）系统的集成日益增强，出现了新的软件、操作和基础设施挑战，亟需理解不同用户角色及其数据需求以支持混合量子-经典工作负载。

Method: 通过识别和表征QCSC环境中的六类典型用户角色，分析各角色的职责、使用工具及其对操作数据的不同需求。

Result: 明确了六种关键角色，特别是出现在量子与HPC系统交界处的新兴角色，并梳理了他们在数据访问、工具链和操作流程方面的差异与需求。

Conclusion: 定义用户角色及其数据需求为构建统一的数据表示和分析框架奠定了基础，有助于推动量子-HPC生态系统的发展和协作。

Abstract: Quantum computing is entering an era where quantum processing units (QPUs) with tens to hundreds of qubits are increasingly integrated with high-performance computing (HPC) systems to support hybrid quantum-classical workloads. This paradigm, often referred to as Quantum-Centric SuperComputing (QCSC), introduces new operational, software, and infrastructure challenges. In this paper, we identify and characterize six distinct personas involved in QCSC environments. Particular attention is given to emerging roles that arise at the interface between quantum and HPC systems. We analyze the responsibilities and tools associated with each persona and examine their differing requirements for operational data. Consistent data representations and analytics frameworks will be required to support diverse users across the quantum-HPC ecosystem. By defining personas and their data needs, we provide a foundation for future discussions in this critical area.

</details>


### [160] [Completing or Refusing Low-Dimensional Records of Structured Quantum Circuits: Measurement Loss, Compression Loss, and Hardware Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01303)
*Gunhee Cho,Juhee Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了结构化量子电路中由于记录低维化导致的控制依赖信息丢失问题，提出通过Hellinger残差和Fisher度量来量化信息损失，并证明了一个有限库完备性定理以判断是否可消除该损失。实验验证了方法在真实设备上的有效性，包括减少校准开销和检测信息损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化量子电路中，低维记录常用于简化数据表示，但会丢失对电路参数响应不同的控制依赖信息。本文旨在不依赖参数化噪声模型或低维统计族的情况下，评估这种信息损失并提供可执行的判定方法。

Method: 利用量子Fisher信息界定预测量灵敏度，使用Fisher度量描述测量与记录所能达到的灵敏度；引入Hellinger残差衡量记录所去除的响应变化，其局部二次项为全结果得分的条件协方差；提出有限库完备性定理，结合解析控制芽和Rees赋值进行理论分析，并通过IBM和IQM的真实设备实验验证方法。

Result: 证明了有限库完备性定理，表明可通过有限步骤判断是否存在保持所有指定响应的记录；三比特计算区分了测量损失与记录损失；IBM实验显示校准 shots 减少33.3%且满足非劣效性标准；IQM数据显示记录级漂移显著低于全分布漂移，但在35/36种设置中仍存在显著残差。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从状态-测量-记录链的信息保留理论框架，提供了可执行的完成或拒绝判据，验证了在特定电路上的有效故障检测能力，但未确立通用压缩性能或设备量子Fisher信息的普适性。

Abstract: Structured quantum circuits are often summarized by low-dimensional records. Records lose control-dependent information when they merge outcomes whose probabilities respond differently to circuit parameters. We assess this loss without a parametric hardware-noise model or low-dimensional statistical family. Quantum Fisher information bounds premeasurement sensitivity; Fisher metrics of measurements and records describe device-attainable sensitivity. A Hellinger residual measures the response removed by a record, while its local quadratic term is the conditional covariance of the full-outcome score. Simultaneous confidence bounds support approval, refusal, or deferral.
  We prove a finite-library completion theorem: executable augmentations terminate with either a record preserving every declared response or proof that no library augmentation removes the loss. For an analytic control germ, integral closures characterize preservation along every analytic control arc, and finitely many Rees valuations detect failure. This state--measurement--record chain connects an all-arc criterion to executable completion or refusal, an attainable-Fisher local kernel criterion, and finite-sample decisions.
  A three-qubit calculation separates measurement loss from record loss. IBM experiments on Kingston and Marrakesh test decisions in fixed-particle-number and GHZ families, including negative controls. An end-to-end Kingston experiment reduces calibration shots by 33.3% while meeting prespecified noninferiority criteria on two held-out objectives. In two-epoch IQM Garnet data, median record-level drift is 0.0797 times full-distribution drift, but a significant residual remains in 35 of 36 settings. These experiments validate failure detection on the tested circuits; they do not establish universal compression performance or device quantum Fisher information.

</details>

Safford,D. Saha,A. E. Saia,D. I. Santiago,R. Schwartz,A. Schwarzkopf,M. Scott-Jones,S. L. Sendelbach,S. J. Shapiro,A. N. Sharma,M. E. Sherwin,A. Sierakowski,J. N. Sills,R. Simha,N. Siwak,J. Smith,C. Tokarchik,N. Tralshawala,D. Tran,S. Tyler,L. Upton,G. Van Dyke,J. Vannucci,K. Vempati,J. S. Vogel,R. Vyas,M. Wagner,P. Warner,N. Washington,B. Way,M. A. Wayne,D. Wehella-Gamage,M. E. Weippert,T. Weitzel,J. Wenner,L. White,S. Whitsitt,A. Wilkinson,R. Willey,I. Yandow,J. Yang,L. Yu,F. Yumiceva,V. Zheng,R. Zimmerman,C. Zinn,J. Ziskind*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了无需微波的新型超导量子比特制备与读出方法，保真度超过99%，耗时仅10纳秒，显著提升量子计算系统的速度与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的超导量子比特制备与读出依赖精细调谐的微波信号和约100纳秒的测量时间，限制了系统的可扩展性和性能，亟需更快速、高保真且可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 采用无需微波的新方法，结合量子通量参数放大器（quantum flux parametrons）实现快速放大，完成量子到数字信号的高速转换。

Result: 实现了超过99%的保真度，制备与读出操作均仅需10纳秒，全量子到数字转换在15纳秒内完成，比现有微波技术快一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该技术具备高速、高保真和可扩展的优势，兼容现有的超导数字控制系统，为未来大规模量子计算系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fast, high-fidelity, scalable state preparation and measurement is critical to the realization of a quantum computing system. The state-of-the-art methods for preparation and readout of superconducting qubits require finely tuned microwave signals and ~100 ns of measurement time, which are major obstacles to the scalability and performance of superconducting quantum computers. Here, we have demonstrated novel, microwave-free methods for both preparation and readout of superconducting qubits with >99% fidelity in only 10 ns for either operation while maintaining qubit coherence. This technology is compatible with scalable superconducting digital control systems, and using quantum flux parametrons for amplification, we demonstrated full quantum-to-digital conversion in only 15 ns, which is an order of magnitude faster than state-of-the-art microwave-based techniques.

</details>


### [162] [Quantum phase transitions of the Cavity Heisenberg spin-chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01340)
*Lv-Ting Gong,Shao-Fan He,Fu-Quan Dou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合量子信息度量与半经典固定点分析，揭示了腔海森堡自旋链模型中基态和激发态量子相变及遍历-非遍历转变的机制，发现了一个介于正常相和超辐射相之间的变形相，并阐明了激发态量子相变与遍历性破缺之间的谱结构关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子临界性与遍历性破缺在复杂量子多体系统中的相互作用，是理解光-物质强耦合系统中相变行为的关键挑战。

Method: 采用量子信息度量（如参与比、多分形分析）结合半经典固定点分析，研究腔海森堡自旋链模型中的基态与激发态量子相变，并通过能级统计分析遍历-非遍历转变。

Result: 发现了介于正常相与超辐射相之间的新变形相，其态密度呈现对数非解析性和两个额外的跳跃不连续；揭示了激发态量子相变与遍历-非遍历转变之间的谱结构联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为光-物质量子多体系统中的相结构与谱特征提供了统一的刻画框架，有助于理解量子临界性与遍历性破缺的共存与竞争机制。

Abstract: The interplay between quantum criticality and ergodicity breaking constitutes a central challenge in complex quantum many-body systems. Here, we investigate ground-state quantum phase transitions (QPTs) and excited-state quantum phase transitions (ESQPTs), as well as ergodic-nonergodic transition in the cavity Heisenberg spin-chain (CHS) model. By combining quantum information measures with semiclassical fixed-point analysis, we identify a deformed phase that interpolates between the normal and superradiant phases, featuring a logarithmic nonanalyticity in the density of states (DoS) and two additional jump discontinuities. We further elucidate the spectral-structure mechanism underlying the connection between ESQPTs and the ergodic--nonergodic transition (ENET) via level statistics, participation ratios, and multifractal analysis. Our results provide a general framework for characterizing phase structures and spectral features in light--matter quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [163] [Local Gaussian bounds on the non-destructive discrimination of two-mode squeezed states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01373)
*Mi-Jung So,James Moran,Youngrong Lim,Mahn-Soo Choi,Hyukjoon Kwon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在连续变量量子系统中使用局域高斯测量对双模压缩真空态进行非破坏性区分的方法，分析了区分成功率与态保真度之间的权衡关系，并提出了一个在数值上最优的协议。此外，研究还表明利用预先共享的纠缠资源可以超越标准局域高斯测量的性能极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子测量通常是破坏性的，限制了对量子态的重复利用。本文旨在将有限维系统中已知的信息获取与扰动之间的权衡关系推广到无限维连续变量系统，实现对纠缠态的非破坏性区分。

Method: 采用局域高斯测量方法对两个双模压缩真空态进行非破坏性区分，分析成功率与输出态相对于初始态保真度之间的权衡关系，并通过数值优化寻找最优协议；进一步引入预共享纠缠资源以提升性能。

Result: 构建了一个基于局域高斯测量的最优非破坏性区分协议，明确了成功率与保真度之间的权衡关系；发现利用预共享纠缠可突破标准局域高斯测量的性能上限。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将信息获取与状态扰动之间的基本权衡从有限维系统推广至连续变量系统，为连续变量量子信息处理中的非破坏性测量提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: Typical measurement setups in quantum systems are destructive, meaning that states are irretrievably altered after measurement. In this work, we analyse non-destructive discrimination of two two-mode squeezed vacuum states using local Gaussian measurements. We investigate a tradeoff relation between the success probability of discrimination and the fidelity of the resulting state with the initial state, and construct a protocol given by local Gaussian measurements, which is optimal within our numerically explored class. We also extend to the case where we allow for additional pre-shared entanglement, and show that this regime allows us to exceed the standard local Gaussian bound for the fidelity-success probability tradeoff. Our work provides a natural extension of the tradeoff between information gain and disturbance in entangled-state discrimination, previously established for finite-dimensional quantum systems, to infinite-dimensional continuous-variable systems.

</details>


### [164] [Exact Virtual Channel Programming with Vanishing Excess Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01419)
*Mingrui Jing,Mengbo Guo,Hongshun Yao,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对有限维量子信道的精确重构协议，证明了单次采样开销随系统维度二次增长的最优性，并揭示了量子程序存储与经典采样之间的定量权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于有限维物理处理器无法精确编程连续的酉信道族，本文旨在量化这一限制，并探索在物理可实现条件下对量子信道进行精确重构的可能性。

Method: 构建了一种与目标无关的精确重构协议，结合确定性基于端口的量子隐形传态和修正去极化失真的拟分解方法；通过将低开销重构协议映射为未知酉门的物理学习器，并利用局部量子估计推导出最优下界。

Result: 证明了任意d维信道的最优单副本采样开销随d²增长，并得出了固定维度下超额开销随相同Choi态数量成反比消失的精确规律；给出了上界和下界的匹配系数。

Conclusion: 将普适的不可编程定理转化为量子程序存储与经典采样之间的定量权衡，其主导成本反映了局部可学习的酉自由度数量。

Abstract: A finite-dimensional physical processor cannot exactly program a continuous family of distinct unitary channels. We show that this obstruction becomes quantitative when the target channel is stored in a normalized Choi state and its output observables are reconstructed by sampling physical channels and classically post-processing their measurement outcomes. For arbitrary $d$-dimensional channels, we construct a target-independent exact reconstruction protocol and prove the optimal one-copy sampling overhead, which grows quadratically with system dimension. We further prove the sharp fixed-$d$ law that the excess overhead vanishes inversely with the number of identical Choi programs. The upper bound combines deterministic port-based teleportation with a quasi-decomposition that corrects its depolarizing distortion. The converse maps any low-overhead reconstruction protocol to a physical learner of unknown unitaries and uses local quantum estimation to recover the same leading coefficient. These results recast the universal no-programming obstruction as a quantitative trade-off between quantum program memory and classical sampling, with a leading cost that reflects the locally learnable unitary degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [165] [Verifiable quantum advantage in extremely low depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01448)
*Alexandru Gheorghiu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可由浅层量子电路解决、但对经典算法困难且可由经典计算机高效验证的采样问题，实现了无需中途测量或反馈的低深度量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 展示浅层量子电路在特定计算任务上的优越性，并推动实用化量子优势的实现。

Method: 基于LWE的单轮量子性证明进行编译，构造适用于极低深度量子电路（QNC^0[log log] 和 QAC^0）的采样方案，并引入加强版自适应硬核比特性质。

Result: 提出了可在极低深度量子电路上实现的量子采样方案，其安全性基于格密码中的知识假设和加强的自适应硬核比特性质，且无需中途中断测量或经典反馈。

Conclusion: 浅层量子电路已具备执行某些经典难解且可高效验证任务的能力，表明即使没有复杂控制操作，也能实现量子优势。

Abstract: We give a sampling problem that is solvable by shallow quantum circuits, hard for polynomial-time classical algorithms under lattice-based assumptions, and efficiently verifiable by a classical computer. The quantum sampler admits two implementations: one uses log-logarithmic-depth quantum circuits with one- and two-qubit gates, i.e., $\mathsf{QNC}^0[\log\log]$ circuits, while the other uses constant-depth quantum circuits with unbounded fan-in gates, i.e., $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuits. Our construction can be seen as compiling the Learning with Errors (LWE)-based single-round proof of quantumness of Arabadjieva et al. (2025) to very low depth. The price paid for this compilation is the reliance on less standard, though well-motivated, assumptions: in addition to the lattice knowledge assumption used by Arabadjieva et al. (2025), we require a strengthened variant of the adaptive-hardcore-bit property of LWE, for which we provide supporting evidence. Unlike previous low-depth proofs of quantumness, the quantum computation here requires no mid-circuit measurements or feed-forward: it consists only of running a shallow circuit and sampling from its output distribution. This shows that shallow quantum circuits have sufficient structure to solve certain classically hard tasks whose solutions can be verified efficiently.

</details>


### [166] [Quantum Weighted Moving Average for Predicting Limit Order Book Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01524)
*Matthias Kamm,Dinh-Long Vu,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子加权移动平均（QWMA）模型，用于预测基于限价订单簿（LOB）数据的金融价格趋势，在FI-2010和中国A股数据集上表现接近最优经典模型，虽未展示量子优势，但表明量子部分可有效聚焦时间序列中最具预测性的部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算机在多变量金融时间序列预测中的潜在应用价值，特别是在处理高维、高频金融数据时是否能超越经典模型。

Method: 提出量子加权移动平均（QWMA）模型，结合经典预处理（特征与时间维度归一化）和基于线性组合酉算子的量子层，将经典数据以酉变换形式嵌入量子电路，并在真实金融数据集上进行评估。

Result: QWMA模型在FI-2010和中国A股数据集上的表现接近当前最佳经典模型水平，量子部分能够学习并关注时间序列中最具预测能力的部分；同时研究了多种变体（如类指数加权形式），但未发现明确的量子优势。

Conclusion: 尽管尚未实现量子优势，但该混合经典-量子框架具有足够的表达能力，可用于金融时间序列预测，为未来探索量子机器学习在金融领域的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Can quantum computers be useful for forecasting multivariate financial time series? In this work, we consider the problem of predicting price trends from limit order book (LOB) data. After identifying key components of classical models, we introduce the quantum weighted moving average (QWMA) model. The two main building blocks are, first, classically preprocessing via normalization of both feature and temporal dimensions and, second, a linear combination of unitaries-based layer for unitarily-embedded classical data. The models are evaluated on the FI-2010 benchmark dataset and a second dataset of China A-share stocks. While we do not present evidence of quantum advantage, the combined classical-quantum model demonstrates performances close to the best classical models. The quantum part of the model is expressive enough to focus on the most predictive parts of the time series. Several specializations of the QWMA model are considered, in particular a variant related to the widely-used exponential moving average (EMA). We give consideration to the limitations of the methods and the datasets.

</details>


### [167] [Irreducibility and regularisation properties of Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01547)
*Franco Fagnola,Federico Girotti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续变量量子系统上高斯量子马尔可夫半群（GQMS）的正则性和不可约性，提出了自然的正则性概念，并通过代数条件刻画其平滑效应与不可约性，揭示了不可约性在量子情形下强于经典情况中的正则化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高斯量子马尔可夫半群的正则性和不可约性对于分析连续变量量子系统的动力学行为至关重要，现有理论缺乏统一的代数刻画框架。

Method: 通过引入适用于该设定的算子正则性概念，结合漂移矩阵和量子扩散矩阵的代数条件，建立与量子线性系统可控性理论及无退相干子系统结构的联系，并提出量子版本的Hörmander条件来刻画不可约性。

Result: 得到了多个等价的不可约性判据，发现量子情形下不可约性强于正则化条件，这与经典情况不同；同时建立了用于分析这些性质的代数框架。

Conclusion: 本文为研究可约高斯量子马尔可夫半群及更一般的连续变量系统上的量子马尔可夫半群奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study regularisation and irreducibility properties of Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups (GQMSs) acting on continuous-variable quantum systems. We first identify a natural notion of regularity for operators in this setting, which allows us to formulate and characterise the smoothing effects of GQMSs in terms of algebraic conditions involving the drift and quantum diffusion matrices. These conditions establish a connection with the controllability theory of quantum linear systems and with the structure of decoherence-free subsystems.
  We then characterise irreducibility through several equivalent algebraic criteria: one formulated in terms of the drift and quantum diffusion matrices, one in terms of the operators appearing in the generalised GKLS representation of the generator, and a third given by a quantum analogue of Hörmander's condition. A central and somewhat surprising consequence is that, in contrast with the classical case, irreducibility is strictly stronger than conditions ensuring regularisation. Our results provide an algebraic framework for analysing these properties and lay the groundwork for a broader study of reducible Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups and, more generally, more general relevant quantum Markov semigroups on continuous-variable systems.

</details>

### [168] [Foreknowledge and Free Will Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00075)
*Eliyahu Zvi Engelberg*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过现代物理学中的因果顺序和物理决定性概念，重新审视了预知与自由意志之间的传统矛盾。利用相对论中对类空间隔事件测量顺序的相对性，证明了一个观察者可以在某个参考系中拥有对未来自由事件结果的知识，表明知识获取并不依赖于决定该事件的物理条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统上关于预知与自由意志不相容的问题，探讨在现代物理框架下（特别是相对论和量子纠缠）是否可以存在对未来事件的非决定性知识。

Method: 基于狭义相对论中不同参考系下测量事件时间顺序可逆的特点，结合量子纠缠系统中测量结果的强关联性，分析一个测量结果是否能在保持其‘自由’的同时被另一个时空分离的观察者所知晓。

Result: 发现在某些参考系中，观察者可以获得对尚未在其过去光锥内的测量事件结果的确定知识，而该事件仍满足‘自由’（即未被局部历史决定）的条件。

Conclusion: 认识论上的预知可以独立于物理决定性存在，因此预知与自由并非必然冲突；这为理解知识、因果性和自由意志的关系提供了新的物理视角。

Abstract: The apparent incompatibility between foreknowledge and free will is traditionally formulated in terms of the implication of knowledge of a future event for the freedom of that event. This paper reexamines the problem using the notions of causal order and physical determination provided by modern physics. I consider measurements of two entangled particles at spacelike-separated events, where a measurement outcome is taken to be free if it is not determined by the previous history of the part of the universe accessible to it. Relativity allows the temporal order of the two measurements to be reversed between reference frames while preserving their causal independence. Quantum correlations nevertheless allow an observer at one measurement event to know the outcome at the other. I show that there is, therefore, a frame in which an observer possesses foreknowledge of a measurement outcome that remains free in this sense. The argument demonstrates that epistemic access to a future event need not be grounded in physical conditions that determine it.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Ordinal Gates, Cardinal Bets: Matching LLM Confidence to the Financial Decision Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00187)
*Rayansh Singh,Sara Rezaeimanesh*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: LLM置信度分数的决策价值依赖于下游操作者和暴露控制器，单独使用重校准或尺度调整不可行；实验表明，置信度映射与其对应的尺度需匹配使用，才能显著提升组合收益，且该效果在不同控制策略下有所差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM置信度分数在金融应用中如何被下游系统正确利用，揭示当前单调重校准方法的局限性，并强调置信度转换与控制器联合评估的重要性。

Method: 在FactSet新闻数据上测试9个开源LLM，使用2021年数据拟合置信度映射和尺度参数，在2022–2023年样本外评估不同映射与尺度组合对确定性等价收益（CER）的影响，比较固定与自适应波动率控制下的表现。

Result: 单独转移尺度会降低8/9模型的CER并导致风险目标偏差；匹配原始映射与对应尺度使年化CER提升9.2个百分点（p<0.001），排除最大贡献模型后仍有5.5个百分点提升；但在自适应控制器下增量收益降至1.6个百分点，且存在显著交互效应；滚动窗口结果也持续显示正向交互作用。

Conclusion: 置信度变换必须与下游控制器联合设计和评估，不能孤立优化，否则可能损害实际决策性能。

Abstract: LLM confidence scores are not independently deployable objects: their decision value depends on the downstream operator and exposure controller that consume them. Monotone recalibration cannot change a coverage-matched rank-based gate, whereas position sizing consumes score magnitude, so changing a confidence map can invalidate a scale fitted to the previous score distribution. We test this on FactSet news for Nasdaq-100 equities, fitting maps and scales on 2021 and evaluating nine open-weight LLMs out-of-sample on 2022--2023. Cross-applying raw and correctness maps with independently fitted scales shows that the two components are not portable alone: scale transfer reduces certainty-equivalent return (CER) in $8/9$ models and produces large risk-target errors. Matching each map with its fitted scale improves ensemble CER by $9.2$ percentage points per year under frozen-scale control ($p<0.001$), and the effect remains significant when the single largest-contributing model is excluded ($+5.5$pp/yr), so it is not driven by one case. Under an identical adaptive-volatility controller, however, the incremental effect falls to $+1.6$pp/yr, with a significant controller interaction. Annual walk-forward effects are smaller, although map--scale interaction remains positive in every fold. Confidence transformations should therefore be evaluated jointly with the downstream controllers that consume them.

</details>


### [170] [Lightweight Adaptation of EEG Foundation Models for Stroke Motor Imagery Decoding: Domain Shift and Subject-Level Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00282)
*Anh T. Nguyen,Zihua Sun,Michelle J. Johnson*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究评估了低秩适应（LoRA）在将预训练的EEG基础模型迁移到中风患者运动想象解码任务中的有效性，发现尽管在健康数据上表现良好，但模型在病理EEG上的迁移需针对性适应，且个体差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于基于健康人群训练的运动想象脑电图（EEG）解码模型可能无法可靠地迁移到中风患者的病理EEG信号上，因此需要探索有效的域适应方法以提升临床应用潜力。

Method: 采用低秩适应（LoRA）技术对三种预训练EEG基础模型（LaBraM-base、REVE-base、REVE-large）进行微调，并在PhysioNet EEGMMIDB和包含30名中风患者的UET175数据集上进行五折交叉验证与留一受试者交叉验证（LOOCV），比较冻结主干仅训练头部层的基线方法与LoRA的性能。

Result: 在EEGMMIDB上，LoRA提升了LaBraM-base和REVE-base的准确率至0.822和0.957；在UET175上，所有仅训练头部的模型表现接近随机水平，而使用LoRA后，REVE-base达到0.847±0.194的准确率并优于更大的REVE-large（0.806±0.178）。最强配置（REVE-base + LoRA）在LOOCV中平均准确率达0.952，但个体准确率从0.586到1.000不等。从健康数据集到中风数据集的零样本迁移表现仍接近随机水平（0.464±0.072）。

Conclusion: 健康人群上的高性能并不保证能迁移到中风患者的EEG解码任务中。将EEG基础模型应用于康复脑机接口时，必须包含目标域适应以及对时间信息性、空间敏感性和生理可分性的个体化评估。

Abstract: Motor imagery (MI) electroencephalography (EEG) decoding could support post-stroke rehabilitation, but models developed on healthy cohorts may not transfer reliably to pathological EEG. We evaluated whether Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) can efficiently adapt three pretrained EEG foundation models (i.e., LaBraM-base, REVE-base, and REVE-large) for binary left- versus right-hand MI decoding. Frozen-backbone head-only baselines and LoRA adaptation were evaluated using subject-wise five-fold cross-validation on the PhysioNet EEG Motor Movement/Imagery Dataset and a binary subset of the UET175 dataset comprising 30 stroke participants. On EEGMMIDB, LoRA increased accuracy to 0.822 for LaBraM-base and 0.957 for REVE-base. On UET175, all head-only models performed near chance. With LoRA, LaBraM-base remained near chance (0.499$\pm$0.009), whereas REVE-base reached 0.847$\pm$0.194 and outperformed REVE-large (0.806$\pm$0.178), indicating that increased model capacity alone did not improve stroke-domain adaptation. The strongest stroke configuration, REVE-base LoRA, was further evaluated using within-cohort leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOOCV), showing 0.952 mean accuracy, but subject-wise accuracy ranged from 0.586 to 1.000, revealing a small low-performing tail. Zero-shot transfer from EEGMMIDB to UET175 remained near chance (0.464$\pm$0.072). These findings show that healthy-benchmark performance does not ensure transfer to stroke EEG. Translation of EEG foundation models to pseudo-online or real-time rehabilitation BCIs should therefore include target-domain adaptation and subject-level assessment of temporal informativeness, spatial sensitivity, and physiological discriminability.

</details>


### [171] [Hidden relationships in a document-derived property graph: top-k chunk embeddings and inverse-distance weighting over a dynamically evolving ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00387)
*Bilge Kaan Karamete,Hunter Casten*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出一种无需修改已抽取事实的附加第二阶段方法，通过嵌入和最近邻查询发现文本间潜在实体关联，使用Shepard逆距离加权评分，保证顺序无关性和增量可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型从文本中提取知识图谱时仅捕获明确陈述的事实，导致跨文档的语义相关实体无法连接。

Method: 将每个文档分块并嵌入一次，通过跨已有块的top-k最近邻查询结合实体成员映射生成候选节点对，并采用Shepard逆距离加权与重缩放弦距离度量进行评分，利用无门控累加器确保顺序独立和增量扩展。

Result: 在FalkorDB、Kinetica、ArangoDB和Neo4j上实现，768维和240维嵌入分别保持了92%和72%的边保真度（相对于3072维基线），且top-k计算速度快25倍。

Conclusion: 该方法有效发现跨文档的潜在语义连接，具有高保真度、高效性和系统通用性，支持增量处理且不依赖处理顺序。

Abstract: Large language models extracting knowledge graphs from text capture only explicitly stated facts, often leaving semantically related entities disconnected across documents. We present an additive, engine-neutral second pass that discovers these latent ties without altering extracted facts. Each document is chunked and embedded once; top-k nearest- neighbor queries across existing chunks yield candidate node pairs via entity membership maps. Candidate pairs are scored using Shepard inverse-distance weighting with a rescaled chord distance metric, avoiding the threshold-collapsing flaw of affine cosine scoring behind a k-NN gate. Un-gated per-pair accumulators form a commutative monoid, ensuring the pipeline is strictly order-independent and scales incrementally without recomputing prior documents. Implemented across FalkorDB, Kinetica, ArangoDB, and Neo4j, our method shows that 768- and 240-dimensional embeddings retain 92% and 72% edge fidelity against a 3072-D baseline while achieving a 25x faster top-k formulation.

</details>


### [172] [MUFASA: An Information Utility-Aware Preprocessing Framework for Reliable Model Reasoning in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00424)
*Rathinaraja Jeyaraj,Barathi Subramanian,Songmi Noh,Mitchell N. Peterson,Terry Guo,George A. Fisher,Nigam H. Shah,Curtis P. Langlotz,Thomas J. Montine,Jeanne Shen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: MUFASA是一种用于H&E染色全切片图像的可泛化预处理框架，能够排除伪影和低效区域，同时保留生物学上有意义的组织，显著提升下游AI模型在多种临床任务中的性能和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预处理方法常保留伪影或丢弃诊断相关组织，限制了计算病理学中AI模型在异质人群中的性能、可靠性和鲁棒性。需要一种能准确识别高信息量组织区域并排除干扰因素的通用预处理方案。

Method: 提出MUFASA框架，结合切片级伪影掩码、染色感知瓦片过滤、基于重构的信息效用分层及过度过滤组织瓦片的定向恢复，实现对全切片图像的信息效用感知预处理。

Result: 在多个癌症队列的肿瘤诊断、分型、生物标志物预测和生存预后任务中，MUFASA均优于常用预处理基线，提升模型性能，并减少热图中与伪影相关的错误归因。

Conclusion: WSI预处理是决定下游AI模型性能与有效性的关键因素；MUFASA通过保留高信息量组织并排除伪影，提高了模型推理的解剖合理性与可信度。

Abstract: Reliable computational pathology depends on preprocessing methods that identify informative tissue regions while excluding artifacts and low-utility regions from whole-slide images (WSI). However, existing preprocessing pipelines often retain such regions or discard diagnostically relevant tissue, thereby limiting downstream model performance, reliability, and robustness across heterogeneous cohorts. Here, we systematically evaluate how these regions affect downstream AI model performance across multiple clinically relevant applications and introduce MUFASA, a generalizable, information utility-aware preprocessing framework for H&E-stained WSI that excludes artifacts and low-utility regions while preserving biologically meaningful tissue. MUFASA integrates slide-level artifact masking, stain-aware tile filtering, reconstruction-based utility stratification of tiles, and targeted recovery of tissue tiles that are over-filtered by earlier phases. Across tumor diagnosis, tumor subtyping, biomarker status prediction, and survival prognostication tasks in diverse cancer cohorts, MUFASA consistently improves downstream model performance relative to widely used preprocessing baselines. These gains are accompanied by reduced artifact-associated attribution in model heatmaps, indicating improved alignment between retained tissue and model attention. Our findings establish WSI preprocessing as a critical determinant of downstream model performance and validity, revealing that even accurate predictions can conceal important failure modes stemming from anatomically implausible reasoning driven by retained artifact-containing and low information-utility tiles.

</details>


### [173] [Aerodynamic Shape Design Space Exploration with Deep Latent Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00812)
*Zhen Wei,Edouard Dufour,Colin Pelletier,Michaël Bauerheim,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: DiffGeo是一种基于潜在空间扩散的生成框架，用于在极端数据稀缺条件下进行空气动力学设计空间探索，能够在数据量远少于现有方法的情况下生成高质量、多样化且符合约束的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在空气动力学设计中，高质量设计样本稀缺，传统生成模型依赖大量数据，难以有效探索设计空间，因此需要一种能在极小数据下仍保持高性能的生成方法。

Method: 结合学习到的潜在空间模型与扩散采样器，提出DiffGeo框架，实现自动形状参数化和几何有效、可控的新设计生成；通过能量基条件机制融入复杂设计约束，并支持条件采样以指导生成过程。

Result: 在2D翼型生成、代理优化集成和3D涡轮叶片原型生成三个案例中，DiffGeo在样本质量、多样性及约束满足方面优于GAN和VAE基线方法，仅需十分之一的数据量即可达到更优性能。

Conclusion: DiffGeo能高效推动早期设计阶段的自动化探索，提升设计效率与多样性，并通过可控引导嵌入工程知识，具备在复杂工程设计中广泛应用的潜力。

Abstract: We propose DiffGeo, a latent space diffusion-based generative framework for aerodynamic design space exploration under extreme data scarcity. DiffGeo combines a learned latent space model for automatic shape parameterization, with a diffusion sampler to directly generate novel, geometry-valid and controllable designs. We validate the approach on a series of case studies: (i) a 2D airfoil generation benchmark, where DiffGeo's latent diffusion model is compared against GAN- and VAE-based baselines in terms of sample quality, diversity and constraint adherence under limited data; (ii) integration into a surrogate-based optimization pipeline, where DiffGeo's conditional sampling produces task-informed airfoil data that improve both surrogate modeling and optimization performance; and (iii) extension to 3D turbomachinery blade prototyping, where DiffGeo generates realistic and high-performance blade geometries from a small set of reference designs. Throughout these investigations, DiffGeo achieves high-quality and diverse shape generation with at least an order of magnitude less data than alternatives, decouples geometry representation from design targets for flexible reuse, and seamlessly incorporates complex design constraints via energy-based conditioning. These capabilities demonstrate DiffGeo's potential to enhance early-stage design by automating design space exploration--improving efficiency, expanding design diversity and embedding engineering knowledge through controllable guidance.

</details>


### [174] [VPID: An Integrated Framework for Vulnerability Prioritization and Intrusion Detection in Enterprise Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00819)
*Xuanren Chen,Xin Wang,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的漏洞优先级排序与入侵检测框架VPID，专为资源有限的小型企业设计，包含受控漏洞验证和智能入侵防御两个模块，并在大规模数据集上验证了其高精度和低误报率的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型企业面临日益严重的内部网络安全威胁，但缺乏足够的资金、计算资源和专业人员来部署复杂的安全部署平台，因此需要一种轻量且高效的解决方案。

Method: VPID框架分为两个模块：一是使用OpenVAS进行资产映射和漏洞识别，结合决策树进行漏洞优先级排序，并通过规则引擎生成针对性验证载荷；二是利用Scapy捕获流量，通过结合决策树与多项朴素贝叶斯的检测流程分析流量，再用Snort规则验证高风险流量，并通过iptables实现阻断与告警。

Result: 在550,000个网络流样本和15,000个标记漏洞记录的评估中，漏洞排序模块的决策树达到91.8%的精确率、89.5%的召回率和90.6%的F1分数；在55,000个独立流量测试样本中，联合检测流程达到94.5%的精确率、88.3%的召回率、91.3%的F1分数，且误报率低于1.5%。

Conclusion: VPID是一种适用于资源受限小型企业的高效、轻量级安全框架，在漏洞优先级排序和入侵检测方面表现出高准确性与实用性，同时保持较低的误报率。

Abstract: Small enterprises face increasingly serious threats to their internal networks but often lack the financial resources, computing capacity, and specialist staff required to deploy resource intensive security platforms. This paper designs and implements VPID, a lightweight framework for vulnerability prioritization and intrusion detection that consists of two principal modules: controlled vulnerability validation and intelligent intrusion defense. The first module uses OpenVAS for asset mapping and vulnerability identification, applies a decision tree to prioritize vulnerabilities, and employs a rule engine to generate targeted validation payloads. The second module captures network traffic using Scapy, analyzes it through a detection pipeline that combines a decision tree with multinomial Naive Bayes, verifies traffic assessed as high risk using Snort rules, and performs blocking and alerting through iptables. The evaluation uses 550,000 network flow samples containing normal and attack traffic for detector training, together with 15,000 labeled vulnerability records. On the vulnerability ranking test set, the decision tree achieves a precision of 91.8%, a recall of 89.5%, and an F1 score of 90.6%. On an independent test set containing 55,000 traffic samples, the combined detection pipeline achieves a precision of 94.5%, a recall of 88.3%, and an F1 score of 91.3%, while maintaining a false positive rate below 1.5%.

</details>


### [175] [Connectome-Based Modelling Reveals Orientation Maps in the Drosophila Optic Lobe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01330)
*Jia-Nuo Liew,Shenghan Lin,Bowen Chen,Wei Zhang,Xiaowei Zhu,Wei Zhang,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 通过整合果蝇全脑连接组和生物神经元模型，研究发现即使没有皮层结构，果蝇视觉系统中也能从连接组约束的动力学中产生一致的方向选择性图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无皮层结构的物种是否能演化出类似哺乳动物的视觉方向图谱，理解跨物种视觉系统的普遍计算原理。

Method: 结合完整的果蝇脑连接组与基于生物学的脉冲神经元模型，使用不同方向刺激驱动网络，并分析下游神经元响应以检测方向选择性图谱的形成。

Result: 模拟结果显示，在仅由连接组决定的网络动力学下，果蝇视觉系统中可自发形成连贯的方向选择性图谱。

Conclusion: 方向选择性图谱可在无皮层结构的神经系统中通过连接组约束的动态过程演化产生，提示不同起源的物种可能独立演化出相似的视觉结构。

Abstract: The ability to extract oriented edges from visual input is a core computation across animal vision systems. Orientation maps, long associated with the layered architecture of the mammalian visual cortex, systematically organise neurons by their preferred edge orientation. Despite lacking cortical structures, the Drosophila melanogaster brain contains feature-selective neurons and exhibits complex visual detection capacity, raising the question of whether map-like vision representations can emerge without cortical infrastructure. We integrate a complete fruit fly brain connectome with biologically grounded spiking neuron models to simulate neuroprocessing in the fly visual system. By driving the network with oriented stimuli and analysing downstream responses, we show that coherent orientation maps can emerge from purely connectome-constrained dynamics. These results suggest that species of independent origin could evolve similar visual structures.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [FALCON: Fault-Tolerant Magnetic Tunnel Junction-Based In-Memory Stochastic Architecture for Reliability-Critical Edge AI Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00701)
*Farzad Razi,Mehran Moghadam,Sercan Aygun,M. Hassan Najafi,Marc Riedel*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FALCON的容错、基于磁隧道结（MTJ）的存内计算架构，结合随机计算（SC）技术，有效缓解了传统二进制存内计算在工艺偏差、噪声和能效方面的局限性，适用于高可靠性要求的边缘AI应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据密集型应用面临冯·诺依曼内存墙和高能耗问题，传统基于二进制的存内计算架构对工艺偏差和噪声敏感，缺乏足够的计算可靠性，亟需一种兼顾能效与鲁棒性的新型计算架构。

Method: FALCON采用随机计算（SC）将数值编码为均匀比特流，在MTJ基的存内计算架构中实现算术运算；引入确定性比特映射机制和可重构逻辑存内（LIM）结构，避免使用外部处理器和随机数生成器，在内存阵列内直接完成计算。

Result: 基于14 nm FinFET技术的实验表明，FALCON在极端电压缩放、严重工艺变异及高达30%噪声注入下仍能正常工作，并在形态学闭运算这一图像处理案例中验证了其功能正确性与噪声容忍能力。

Conclusion: FALCON通过融合随机计算与MTJ存内计算，实现了高能效、高可靠性的算术运算，为面向可靠性关键的边缘人工智能应用提供了一个稳健的计算框架。

Abstract: As modern data-centric applications such as neural inference and sensor-edge analytics expand, they increasingly encounter the von Neumann memory wall, suffering from excessive data movement overhead and stringent energy constraints. In-Memory Computing (IMC) utilizing emerging non-volatile technologies, such as Magnetic Tunnel Junctions (MTJs), promises to mitigate these bottlenecks. However, conventional binary radix-based IMC architectures suffer from excessive vulnerability to process-induced variations, restricted operating margins, and thermal noise. To bridge the gap between energy efficiency and computational reliability, this work proposes FALCON, a fault-tolerant, MTJ-based in-memory arithmetic architecture integrated with Stochastic Computing (SC). By encoding numerical values into uniform bit-streams, SC naturally absorbs localized soft errors and enables the execution of an essential suite of arithmetic operations using highly compact logic primitives directly within the memory arrays. FALCON integrates a deterministic bit mapping mechanism with reconfigurable logic-in-memory (LIM) structures, eliminating the need to transfer data to external processors or area- and power-hungry random number generators. Experimental results using 14 nm FinFET technology validate the correct functionality of FALCON even under aggressive voltage scaling, severe process variation, and noise injection levels up to 30%, making it a robust framework for reliability-critical edge AI applications. We investigate the proper functionality of FALCON on morphological closing as a realistic noise-tolerant image processing case study.

</details>


### [177] [A Novel Space-Time Coding Architecture for Rydberg Atomic Quantum Receiver-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01180)
*Asifa Zannat,Milad Abolpour,Dani Korpi,Mikko A. Uusitalo,Mikko Valkama,Ertugrul Basar*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于里德堡原子量子接收机辅助的MIMO链路的低复杂度时空编码框架，通过实正交设计和强参考外差接收实现线性化模型和符号级检测，理论与仿真验证了其性能优越性和全分集增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 里德堡原子量子接收机（RAQR）虽具有高灵敏度和宽调谐性，但其基于幅值的读出导致非线性模型，无法兼容传统的复数域MIMO处理方法，因此需要一种新的编码框架以支持高效MIMO通信。

Method: 采用实正交设计对数据符号进行编码，并利用强参考外差接收技术获得等效的实值线性模型，从而保持正交性，实现无需矩阵求逆或向量搜索的匹配滤波符号级检测。

Result: 推导了解析的误比特率表达式，证明所提方案可实现完整的收发分集增益；仿真结果验证了理论分析的正确性，并显示其性能优于空间复用基准方案。

Conclusion: 该低复杂度时空编码框架有效解决了RAQR在MIMO系统中应用时的非线性建模问题，实现了高性能、低复杂度的符号检测，适用于未来的量子辅助无线通信系统。

Abstract: Rydberg atomic quantum receivers (RAQRs) offer high sensitivity and wide tunability, but their magnitude-based readout yields a nonlinear model incompatible with conventional complex-valued multi-input multi-output (MIMO) processing. We propose a low-complexity space-time coding framework for point-to-point RAQR-assisted MIMO links. Data symbols are encoded using real orthogonal designs, while strong-reference heterodyne reception yields an equivalent real-valued linear model. The preserved orthogonality enables matched filter symbol-wise detection without matrix inversion or vector search. An analytical bit error probability expression is derived, proving the proposed scheme achieves the full transmit-receive diversity. Simulations validate the analysis and demonstrate improved performance over spatial multiplexing benchmarks.

</details>


### [178] [Collision-based logic in Lenia and its composition boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01348)
*Chakshu Gupta*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续细胞自动机Lenia中的碰撞逻辑计算，通过Orbium滑翔机的碰撞构建了一个INHITIBIT门，并验证了其在多种相位和偏移下的稳定性，但尚未实现信号的重定向与下游门的连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索固定规则的连续细胞自动机（如Lenia）是否能支持基于碰撞的通用计算，填补离散系统与连续系统在碰撞逻辑上的差距。

Method: 利用Orbium滑翔机在四种连续CA规则类型中的碰撞行为，搜索能够存活并保持完整的模式，构造INHIBIT逻辑门，并测试其在不同相位和空间偏移下的表现。

Result: 成功构建了稳定的INHIBIT门，能在24个呼吸周期相位和9个整数偏移下阻断信号；两个此类门可串联形成正确的AND-NOT链；但未能恢复被偏转信号的位置，也未找到可重复使用的吸收器。

Conclusion: 证明了在Lenia中存在基于碰撞的逻辑门的可能性，但实现更复杂的可组合计算仍需解决信号重定向与标准化输出的问题，下一步关键是实现被偏转信号传递至下游门。

Abstract: Continuous cellular automata such as Lenia spontaneously produce lifelike, self-propelling patterns, including the Orbium glider, which travels in a straight line while pulsing through a fixed breathing cycle. Collision-based logic, where moving patterns compute by colliding, is established in discrete cellular automata and continuous physical media. Within continuous cellular automata, computation has so far been trained into the rule rather than emerging from collisions, and whether a fixed-rule automaton like Lenia can support general collision-based computation remains open. This paper constructs an INHIBIT gate from collisions of the Orbium glider. Of the patterns searched across four continuous-CA rule types, the Orbium glider is the only one shown to survive a collision with both copies intact. A control glider deflects a signal glider off its output line, so the output carries a signal only when no control is present. The gate blocks across all twenty-four phases of the breathing cycle and nine integer offsets of the control. Two such gates in series, with one signal line and two controls, compose into an AND-NOT chain, correct on all eight input combinations. By contrast, routing a signal beyond that single chain is undemonstrated. A deflected signal is not restored to a fixed landing position, and no reusable absorber for the surviving gliders was found. The immediate open question for collision-based computation in Lenia therefore narrows from whether a gate exists to whether a deflected signal can be delivered to a downstream gate, the next requirement for composing the gate beyond a single straight chain.

</details>


<div id='nlin.AO'></div>

# nlin.AO [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Synchronization Pathways and Resilience in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00910)
*Cook Hyun Kim,Jihye Kim,Sangjoon Park,B. Kahng*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径的框架来研究电力网络中的同步现象，揭示了两种不同的种子簇类型及其传播路径，并指出惯性对簇持续性的影响，为维持电网稳定提供了策略依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解微观机制如何引发和传播同步簇对于确保可持续能源供应和电网稳定性至关重要。现有的模型未能充分解释这些过程。

Method: 采用受实证电网数据启发的最小模型和Adhoc势能方法，分析同步簇的形成与传播路径。

Result: 发现了两种不同类型的种子簇及传播方式：一种是由功率分布中心驱动并通过转子聚集向外扩展；另一种在对称分布下由尾部耦合驱动并通过簇合并向内发展。惯性决定了种子簇是否以不同的角速度持续存在。这些发现同样适用于欧洲电网的数据推导退火表示。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，通过强调簇形成和恢复的动力学过程，而非仅依赖静态标准，可以更好地理解和控制电网同步行为，从而指导稳定电网运行策略的设计。

Abstract: Ensuring a sustainable energy supply requires maintaining power-grid stability. Rotor dynamics are governed by the swing equation, which takes the form of a second-order Kuramoto model with a correlation between power and total coupling strength. Yet the microscopic mechanisms that nucleate and propagate synchronized clusters remain poorly understood. Using a minimal model motivated by empirical grid data and the \Adhoc potential method, we reveal two distinct seed cluster types and propagation pathways: a population-driven seed cluster at the center of the power distribution propagating to its tails by rotor accretion, and, for a symmetric distribution, coupling-driven seed clusters at its tails propagating inward to the center by cluster merger. These differences generate distinct order-parameter patterns, while inertia controls whether the seed clusters persist with distinct angular velocities. The same propagation pathways also govern recovery following external disturbances. We further confirm that the same selection--persistence rule holds in data-derived annealed representations of European power grids. Therefore, our results can inform strategies for sustaining stable power-grid operation. More generally, our pathway-based framework reframes synchronization by emphasizing the dynamics of cluster formation and recovery rather than relying solely on static criteria.

</details>


<div id='physics.geo-ph'></div>

# physics.geo-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [A Frequency-Aware Dynamic Knowledge Distillation Framework: An Effective Tool for Bridging Low- and High-Frequency Seismic Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00828)
*Jun Ma,Xinyang Wang,Xintong Dong*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种频率感知的动态知识蒸馏框架（FADKD-Net），通过在低频和高频地震特征之间进行有针对性的知识迁移，提升地震特征学习中的结构连续性和细节保留能力，并引入跨域特征对齐策略以增强模型在不同数据集间的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于知识蒸馏的方法通常以全频带方式处理地震特征，忽略了不同频率成分之间的关系，限制了低频和高频知识的协同传递，难以同时保持大尺度结构和精细细节。

Method: 提出FADKD-Net，构建师生学习框架，将地震特征分解为低频和高频成分，分别进行针对性的知识蒸馏；低频蒸馏用于学习稳定的结构先验，高频蒸馏用于增强细节建模，并设计跨域特征对齐策略减少不同数据集间的分布差异。

Result: 所提方法能有效整合低频和高频地震信息，在保持地震事件整体连续性的同时提升对复杂小尺度结构的表征能力，且跨域对齐策略提升了模型在不同地震调查数据间的可迁移性。

Conclusion: FADKD-Net通过频率感知的知识蒸馏实现了低频与高频地震特征的协同学习，显著提升了地震特征表示的质量和跨数据集的泛化性能。

Abstract: Seismic data contain rich information across different frequency bands, with low-frequency components primarily characterizing large-scale geological structures and high-frequency components preserving fine-scale seismic details. Effectively integrating these frequency-dependent components is essential for seismic feature learning to better preserve structural continuity and fine-scale details. Knowledge distillation provides an effective means for transferring informative representations from high-quality data. However, existing distillation-based frameworks usually treat seismic features in a full-band manner, ignoring relationships across frequency bands and thereby limiting the coordinated transfer of low- and high-frequency knowledge. To bridge low- and high-frequency seismic features through knowledge distillation, we propose a frequency-aware dynamic knowledge distillation framework (FADKD-Net), which establishes a teacher-student learning framework and performs frequency-aware knowledge transfer between low- and high-frequency bands. Specifically, FADKD-Net decomposes seismic features into low- and high-frequency components and performs targeted distillation to exploit their complementary information. Low-frequency distillation guides the student model to learn stable structural priors, thereby improving the overall continuity of seismic events. Meanwhile, high-frequency distillation enhances detailed feature modeling and improves the representational capability for complex and small-scale structures. Furthermore, a cross-domain feature alignment strategy is proposed to reduce distributional discrepancies across different surveys and enhance the transferability of the seismic representations learned by FADKD-Net.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.RM'></div>

# q-fin.RM [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Pricing the DeFi Tail: Do Protocols or Depositors Price Operational Risk?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00911)
*Nils Bundi*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文研究了去中心化金融（DeFi）协议中的操作风险，发现自2020年以来已发生超过94亿美元的损失，但多数协议未设置资本缓冲，导致风险主要由存款人承担。作者采用基于巴塞尔标准的损失分布模型分析四大核心领域，发现部分领域存在极端尾部风险，尤其是借贷领域的风险需18%的TVL作为资本缓冲。市场对无缓冲平台确实要求更高的收益率（中位数高出125个基点），但补偿远不足以覆盖实际风险，零售投资者尤其处于信息劣势。因此，作者建议通过强制信息披露而非资本金要求来加强监管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别并量化DeFi协议中的操作风险，评估当前市场机制是否合理地将风险定价，并探讨在缺乏传统银行监管框架下如何保护投资者，特别是零售投资者。

Method: 采用按行业划分的巴塞尔式损失分布方法，基于新的操作风险事件数据集拟合尾部风险模型，估算VaR_{99.9}所需的资本缓冲，并比较有无缓冲的平台之间的收益率差异以检验市场纪律的有效性。

Result: 四大核心领域的尾部风险不超过Moscadelli银行业区间[0.85, 1.39]，但Bridge、Derivatives和Other部门表现出接近或超过无限均值边界的重尾特征（\hatξ≈1.6）；Lending部门需要18% TVL作为资本缓冲，而十大平台中仅四家持有缓冲，平均覆盖5%；无缓冲平台支付的收益率中位数高出125个基点，显示市场能区分风险，但补偿不足。

Conclusion: 当前DeFi市场未能充分为操作风险尾部正确定价，尤其是在缺乏透明度的情况下，零售投资者承担了不成比例的风险。建议通过标准化的信息披露（如公开损失数据、资本缓冲和尾部覆盖率）替代强制性资本要求，以提升市场效率与投资者保护。

Abstract: Similar to banks, DeFi protocols expose depositors to operational risk (USD 9.45 billion across 1,075 events since 2020). Unlike banks, they are not required to hold capital against it. A protocol may maintain a buffer voluntarily. Absent one, the risk falls on the depositor, who should then demand a risk premium in the supply yield. I quantify the underlying tail on one benchmark, a per-sector Basel loss-distribution approach fitted to a new operational risk event dataset, and test both margins against it. Tails in the four core sectors are no heavier than the Moscadelli banking band $[0.85, 1.39]$. Bridge, Derivatives, and the residual Other sector exhibit cyber-loss-level tails ($\hatξ\approx 1.6$), with point estimates past the infinite-mean boundary. The Lending tail implies a $\mathrm{VaR}_{99.9}$ capital buffer of 18% of TVL and of the ten largest Lending venues, the four holding a buffer cover on average 5% of it. Under market discipline, depositors should demand a higher yield in compensation where a venue does not maintain a buffer. I find that venues without a buffer pay a higher premium than those with (a 125-bps gap in medians): evidence the market discriminates in the right direction. However, the premium falls far short of an adequately priced tail. This unpriced tail falls disproportionately on the retail depositor, who sees only the posted rate but lacks the information and skills to price it. Because these products are not bank-regulated, I recommend disclosure over capital mandates: protocols, and any service providers that front access to it, should publish standardized losses, existing capital buffers and tail coverage.

</details>


### [182] [Illiquidity at Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00943)
*Demetrio Lacava,Paolo Santucci de Magistris*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的尾部风险度量指标——流动性风险（IlliQaR），用于量化极端流动性枯竭的程度。基于高频数据计算的实现Amihud指标，研究比较了多种线性和非线性计量经济模型在预测流动性风险方面的表现，特别关注跳跃成分的影响。实证结果表明，在系统性压力期间，考虑跳跃成分能显著提高对个股和市场整体流动性枯竭的预测准确性，并揭示了个股流动性风险事件常在大盘流动性紧张时聚集的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测流动性动态对于投资者和监管机构至关重要。传统的连续模型在市场压力时期往往低估流动性枯竭的严重性，因此需要更精确的风险度量工具来捕捉极端情况下的流动性变化。

Method: 采用基于高频数据的实现Amihud作为非流动性测度，构建线性和非线性计量模型，重点引入跳跃成分进行建模，并评估其对IlliQaR预测能力的提升效果。研究覆盖S&P 500指数及25只大型美国股票。

Result: 包含跳跃成分的模型显著提高了IlliQaR预测的准确性，特别是在系统性压力时期；个股的流动性风险事件常与S&P 500指数的流动性紧张时期重合，显示出流动性风险具有系统性特征。

Conclusion: IlliQaR是一种有效的尾部流动性风险度量工具，纳入跳跃成分的模型能够更好预测极端流动性枯竭，且市场整体流动性可作为个股流动性风险的领先指标。

Abstract: Market efficiency relies fundamentally on stable liquidity. Consequently, forecasting liquidity dynamics is a priority for both investors and regulators. We introduce a new tail-risk metric, Illiquidity-at-Risk (IlliQaR), designed to quantify the magnitude of extreme liquidity dry-ups. Relying upon the realized Amihud (a precise illiquidity measurement derived from high-frequency data as the ratio of realized volatility to trading volume) we assess the predictive power of various linear and non-linear econometric models, with a specific focus on the impact of discontinuous jump components. Accounting for these jumps is essential for achieving accurate probability coverage and better IlliQaR predictions during periods of systemic stress, where standard continuous models systematically underestimate the severity of liquidity evaporation. Our empirical analysis, encompassing the S&P 500 index and a cross-section of 25 large U.S. equities, demonstrates that incorporating jumps significantly improves forecasts of illiquidity. Our results suggest that individual stock IlliQaR violations often cluster during periods of S&P 500 liquidity stress. This indicates that Illiquidity at Risk is not just a localized concern but a systemic one, where the main index acts as a leading indicator for extreme dry-ups in individual stock liquidity.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Core-periphery identification in massive networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00008)
*Eric Yanchenko*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于边列表表示的分治算法，用于在大规模网络中识别核心-外围结构，能够在不将整个网络加载到内存的情况下处理包含近1400万条边的真实网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在具有数百万甚至数十亿节点和边的现代大规模网络中有效识别核心-外围结构，需要开发可扩展的算法。

Method: 提出一种分治算法，利用网络的边列表表示而非邻接矩阵，以提高速度和内存效率。

Result: 该算法在合成和真实世界数据上进行了测试，成功处理了接近1400万条边的大规模网络，且无需将整个网络载入内存。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法能够高效地识别大规模网络中的核心-外围结构，具备良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Modern networks can be huge with millions or even billions of nodes and edges. Thus, algorithms must be capable of scaling to such large networks in order to be practically useful. In this work, we are interested in developing an algorithm to identify core-periphery structure in massive networks. Core-periphery structure is a meso-scale feature where nodes are grouped into a densely connected core or sparsely connected periphery. To identify such structures in large networks, we propose a divide-and-conquer algorithm. The key feature of our algorithm is leveraging the edge list representation of the network, instead of the adjacency matrix, as it tends to be faster and makes a more efficient use of memory. We apply the proposed algorithm to synthetic and real-world data, notably demonstrating its performance on a real-world network with almost 14 million edges without loading the entire network into memory.

</details>


### [184] [Centrality Measures in Temporal Networks: A Critical and Comparative Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00011)
*Aksa Urooj,Iqra Altaf Gillani*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文对时序网络中的中心性度量进行了批判性和比较性的综述，提出了一种功能分类法，将现有方法分为基于交互、路径、游走、谱和鲁棒性五类，并通过实验评估了不同方法在影响力传播、排名一致性和计算效率方面的表现，最后指出了大规模网络可扩展性和标准化评估框架等未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序网络中节点重要性的识别比静态图更具挑战性，且现有中心性度量研究分散，缺乏统一的分类和性能比较，亟需系统性梳理与评估。

Method: 提出一种功能分类法，将时序中心性度量分为五类；通过流行病扩散模型进行影响传播实验、基于肯德尔相关系数的排名一致性分析以及运行时间复杂度分析进行多维度评估。

Result: 实验表明不同类别中心性度量在真实数据集上表现各异，部分方法在特定场景下更优，但普遍存在可扩展性不足的问题，且缺少统一评估标准。

Conclusion: 该分类法为理解时序中心性提供了统一视角，有助于根据应用场景选择合适方法，同时呼吁建立标准化评估框架并提升算法在大规模网络上的效率。

Abstract: Temporal networks offer a suitable representation for complex systems in which interactions vary over time, such as communication, transportation, and social networks. Identifying influential nodes in such networks is more challenging than in static graphs because node importance depends not only on network structure but also on the timing and ordering of interactions. Although many temporal centrality measures have been proposed, the literature remains fragmented, with limited consensus on their comparative performance and applicability. This paper presents a critical and comparative survey of centrality measures in temporal networks. We review both temporal extensions of classical centrality metrics and measures specifically designed for temporal graphs, and propose a functional taxonomy that categorizes existing approaches according to the primary mechanism through which influence is quantified in temporal networks. The proposed taxonomy organizes temporal centrality measures into interaction-based, path-based, walk-based, spectral-based and robustness-based categories, providing a unified perspective on their underlying principles. In addition, we provide comparative insights to help select appropriate temporal centrality measures under different network characteristics and application settings. To complement the survey, we conduct experiments on multiple real-world temporal network datasets. The measures are evaluated through influence spreading experiments using epidemic diffusion models, ranking consistency analysis based on Kendall's rank correlation, and runtime complexity analysis to assess computational efficiency and scalability. Finally, we highlight key open challenges and future research directions, including scalability for million-sized networks and the need for standardized evaluation frameworks.

</details>


### [185] [Social bots weaken activist cohesion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00197)
*Linda Li,Orsolya Vasarhelyi,Balazs Vedres*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 在2020年“黑人的命也是命”抗议活动中，研究发现人类用户在接触更多社交机器人（bots）后，其社交网络的凝聚力在个体和社区两个层面上均出现显著下降，且这种影响主要集中在运动支持者中，表明机器人可能通过削弱集体行动的社会纽带而非改变信念来影响社会运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨社交机器人在政治传播中的角色是否不仅限于传播虚假信息，而是进一步影响人类用户之间的社会关系和运动内部的凝聚力。

Method: 通过追踪2020年BLM抗议活动前后核心参与者的转推网络，从个体ego网络的三元闭包和社区内的边密度两个层面衡量人类网络凝聚力的变化，并分析其与机器人暴露程度的关系。

Result: 在抗议高峰期间接触更多机器人的用户，在之后的人际网络凝聚力下降更明显，且这种下降在支持者群体中尤为集中。

Conclusion: 社交机器人可能并非主要通过改变信念，而是通过破坏维持集体行动的社会联系来削弱社会运动的持续性。

Abstract: Social bots now make up a substantial share of online political communication, where they are studied mainly as producers of misinformation and amplified content. Far less is known about whether their presence reshapes the human relationships that hold movements together. We ask whether exposure to bots during a protest peak is followed by the erosion of cohesion in human networks. Tracking retweet networks of core participants in the 2020 Black Lives Matter (BLM) protests before, during, and after the peak, we measure change in cohesion at two scales: triadic closure in individual ego networks and edge density within detected communities. Greater bot exposure during the peak predicts steeper subsequent declines in human cohesion at both scales, and the loss concentrates among supporters of the movement. Bots may weaken activism less by changing what people believe than by dissolving the ties through which collective action is sustained.

</details>


### [186] [Aligned but Flattened: Analyzing the Trade-off between Cultural Alignment and Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00565)
*Jingshen Zhang,Shaoyang Xu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协同评估框架，揭示了当前文化微调范式在追求文化对齐时导致“文化扁平化”的问题，即牺牲了文化多样性。研究表明，这种现象源于神经网络优化中的低秩偏差，呼吁建立能保持跨文化多元性的新对齐目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化对齐评估仅关注一致性得分，忽视了文化多样性，无法判断模型是否真正理解文化差异还是仅仅记忆主流价值观。因此需要一种更全面的评估方法来揭示模型的文化感知能力。

Method: 提出一个联合形式化文化对齐与多样性的评估框架，并在世界价值观调查数据集上对六种主流大语言模型进行基准测试，结合行为分析与机制分析探究文化多样性丧失的原因。

Result: 发现文化对齐与文化多样性之间存在系统性权衡，模型通过对主流文化的锚定形成单一应答模式，造成严重‘文化扁平化’；机制分析表明这是神经网络优化中固有的低秩偏差所致。

Conclusion: 当前基于后训练的文化微调范式存在根本局限，应转向能够保留跨文化多元性的新型对齐目标。

Abstract: Cultural fine-tuning has become the de facto paradigm for building culture-aware large language models (LLMs), yet existing optimization exclusively for alignment scores provides an incomplete portrait of cultural fidelity by systematically obscuring inherent cultural diversity. This unidimensional evaluation lens prompts a fundamental question: do models genuinely perceive distinct cultural nuances, or do they merely memorize dominant cultural values? To address this, we propose a synergistic evaluation framework that jointly formalizes cultural alignment and diversity. Through extensive benchmarking of six mainstream LLMs on the World Values Survey, this framework uncovers a systematic and critical trade-off: the pursuit of cultural alignment consistently incurs an acute expense of diversity, leading to severe "cultural flattening." Investigating this behavioral shift, we demonstrate that these superficial alignment gains stem from models artificially anchoring to dominant majorities, converging onto a monolithic response pattern that wipes out the heterogeneous distributions inherent to human groups. Crucially, our mechanistic analysis suggests that this diversity collapse is not merely a behavioral anomaly but more likely a structural consequence of the low-rank bias inherent in neural network optimization. Therefore, our findings expose the limitations of current post-training paradigms and call for a shift toward alignment objectives that preserve cross-cultural pluralism.

</details>


### [187] [Agent-Enhanced Heterogeneous Graph RAG for Academic Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00761)
*Runsong Jia,Mengjia Wu,Ying Ding,Jie Lu,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于代理的异构图RAG方法，通过查询感知检索、充分性感知重排序和图基础验证三个代理决策步骤，显著提升了学术问答中多跳推理与复杂查询的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成（RAG）系统在处理学术异构图上的复杂查询时存在固定检索策略、缺乏证据充分性评估和无结构化验证的问题，难以支持从属性查找至多跳推理的多样化查询。

Method: 将RAG流程的三个核心阶段转化为显式的代理决策步骤：1）查询感知的检索代理根据查询类型选择图遍历策略；2）充分性感知的重排序代理评估证据完整性并自适应扩展检索子图；3）图基础验证代理检查实体、关系和属性的正确性以确保答案准确。

Result: 在基于OpenAlex和DBLP构建的异构图上的实验表明，该方法在多种基准上持续优于强基线，包括大语言模型、图增强RAG和基于代理的方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入具有明确角色分工的代理机制，所提方法有效解决了传统RAG在学术图问答中的关键局限，实现了更精准、可解释且鲁棒的多跳推理。

Abstract: Academic question answering requires reasoning over heterogeneous scholarly graphs, where queries range from simple attribute lookups to multi-hop inference across author--paper--venue structures. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems struggle in this setting due to three limitations: (1) fixed retrieval strategies that do not adapt to varying query complexity, (2) the absence of sufficiency evaluation leading to incomplete or misaligned evidence, and (3) a lack of structured verification against graph facts. To address these issues, we propose an agentic heterogeneous graph RAG method that transforms the three core stages of the RAG pipeline into explicit agentic decision steps. A query-aware retrieval agent analyzes query type and selects an appropriate graph traversal strategy; a sufficiency-aware reranking agent assesses evidence completeness and adaptively expands the retrieved subgraph; and a graph-grounded verification agent checks entity, relation, and attribute correctness before finalizing the answer. Experiments on heterogeneous graphs constructed from OpenAlex and DBLP suggest that our method consistently outperforms strong LLM, graph-augmented RAG, and agent-based baselines.

</details>


### [188] [A Network Science Perspective on Evaluating Deep Graph Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01015)
*Tianrui Mao,Abele Malan,Megha Khosla,Lydia Chen,Huijuan Wang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文评估了深度图生成模型和传统网络科学模型在生成与真实世界网络结构相似的合成网络方面的能力，并探讨了这些合成网络在识别有效节点免疫策略以抑制疫情或虚假信息传播中的应用。研究发现，两种深度图生成模型能够生成结构上接近真实网络的合成网络，并能有效支持免疫策略的识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于真实社交网络因隐私问题无法共享，需要生成结构逼真的合成网络用于流行病防控策略的研究。传统模型仅能复现少量拓扑特征，而深度图生成模型有望从数据中学习更复杂的结构分布。

Method: 从网络科学角度评估深度图生成模型（基于深度神经网络）和配置模型，通过比较生成网络与真实网络之间的拓扑相似性，并测试其在节点免疫策略识别中的实用性。

Result: 两种深度图生成模型生成的合成网络在拓扑结构上与真实网络高度相似，并能有效识别出抑制传播的关键节点，表现出优于传统模型的实用价值。

Conclusion: 深度图生成模型能够生成结构真实且具有实际应用价值的合成社交网络，适用于流行病或虚假信息传播控制策略的开发与评估。

Abstract: Traditional network models from network science, such as the Erdos-Renyi and configuration models, generate random networks that reproduce few selected topological properties observed in real-world networks. Deep graph generative models emerge as a data-driven approach, leveraging deep neural network architectures to learn complex structural distributions directly from real-world networks to generate more realistic synthetic networks. Because real social contact networks cannot be shared due to privacy risks, synthetic networks serve as an alternative for developing and evaluating epidemic mitigation strategies. In this work, we evaluate deep graph generative models as well as the configuration from a network science perspective by assessing both the topological similarity between generated and real-world networks and their utility in identifying effective node immunization strategies to sup- press epidemic/misinformation spreading. It is found that two deep graph generative models produce synthetic networks that closely resemble the structural properties of real-world networks, enabling them to identify effective immunization strategies.

</details>


### [189] [Don't You Know, Pump it Up! Investigating Cryptocurrency Manipulation in Telegram-Driven Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01176)
*Filipe Moura,Giordano Paoletti,Carlos H. G Ferreira,Jussara Almeida*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种可扩展的框架，通过分析公开Telegram数据与加密货币交易数据的关联，识别大规模的市场操纵行为（如拉高出货），并揭示了社交信号与价格波动之间的时序关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于已知的操纵聊天群或少数加密货币，缺乏对Telegram上大规模推广活动（shilling）如何影响市场的系统性理解。因此，本文旨在填补这一空白，探究信息流与市场活动之间的相互作用。

Method: 提出一个三阶段框架：(1) 使用微调的编码器模型分类加密相关消息以过滤语义噪声；(2) 通过自适应阈值检测加密货币提及的异常激增；(3) 利用准实验计量经济学方法（RDD和DiD）验证社交爆发与市场变动的时间关联。

Result: 分析一年内14,499个频道的2000多万条消息及17,000多种加密货币的交易数据，发现47起疑似拉高出货行为和73次持续市场反应，操纵信号在价格变动前数秒出现且具有极高时间同步性；心理语言学分析显示其文本与自然讨论难以区分；估计相关金融规模超2亿美元。

Conclusion: Telegram上的加密货币操纵活动具有高度时序协调性，仅依赖文本内容难以有效识别，需结合时间模式与市场数据进行检测；研究提供了公共词典与分类器以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Telegram plays a pivotal role in cryptocurrency communication and has been repeatedly associated with coordinated schemes, such as pump-and-dump manipulation. However, existing studies typically focus on known manipulation chats or a limited set of cryptocurrencies, leaving open the question of how Telegram is leveraged for mass promotional activity (shilling) at scale. Moving beyond these limitations, this work analyzes the interplay between information flows and market activity across public Telegram channels. To this end, we propose a scalable framework that (i) classifies crypto-related messages using a fine-tuned encoder model to filter semantic noise, (ii) detects anomalous spikes in cryptocurrency mentions via adaptive thresholding, and (iii) validates temporal associations between social bursts and market movements using quasi-experimental econometric methods (RDD and DiD). We apply this framework to one year of public Telegram data (14,499 channels and over 20 million messages) aligned with transaction data for more than 17,000 cryptocurrencies. Our analysis identifies 47 events consistent with potential pump-and-dump activity and 73 sustained market reactions, showing that manipulative signals are characterized by extreme temporal synchronization and precede price movements by seconds. Notably, psycholinguistic analysis reveals that pump-and-dump messages are linguistically indistinguishable from organic discussions, highlighting the limits of text-based detection alone. Finally, we estimate the cumulative financial volume of detected pump-and-dump events to exceed $200 million and release a public cryptocurrency dictionary and a fine-tuned classifier to support future research.
